However, existing methods usually treat consistency and conflict in a coarse and static way, limiting their ability to capture sparse conflicts and adapt to varying conflict levels. Inspired by the distinct knowledge encoded across layers in large language models, we propose Multi-Level Consistency and Conflict Decoupling (MLCCD), which captures conflicts at different levels from different layers of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Specifically, we first use the MLLM's final decision representation to select visual and textual tokens relevant to the task, preventing irrelevant content from diluting sparse conflicts. Then, we preserve tokens whose representations change sharply between adjacent layers, capturing the knowledge each layer adds. Finally, we decouple this knowledge into consistency and conflict evidence and aggregate each across layers with its own router, adapting to varying conflict levels. Experiments on three benchmarks show that MLCCD outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [36] [LabWorld: Learning Interactive 4D Worlds for Scientific Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36592)
*Suozhi Huang,Kelsey Fu,Binxu Li,Luna Lyu,Kaize Wu,Wenbo Lu,David Smerkous,Jiahan Li,Siqi Zhu,Zhizhou Sha,Jiacheng Guo,Zhixuan Liang,Shilong Liu,Le Cong,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LabWorld：从单目实验室视频以 agent 驱动方式重建可交互 4D 实验室世界（Real2Sim），恢复可复用 3D 资产、度量场景、时变物体状态与流程事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验室仿真器依赖手工建模资产与预定义流程，缺乏可扩展性；而实验室视频蕴含真实设备、布局与交互信息，却隐含在像素中未被利用。目标是让自主科学 agent 能在真实实验室的数字化副本中推理并执行实验。

Method: agent 驱动的 Real2Sim 框架：多帧观测互补用于资产与度量场景重建；运动重建融合视觉与几何观测，并引入编码实验室交互时空要求的事件约束。整体由 agent 编排多步流程，产出可编辑的显式状态（几何、运动、关节、事件）。

Result: 在真实移液（pipetting）与涡旋（vortexing）视频上优于通用 agentic 与专用重建基线：移液 Mask IoU 0.501（+0.033），涡旋 Tube-Length-Normalized PCK-AUC 0.638（+0.035）；事件条件重建将满足物理约束比例从 14.3%(5/35) 提升至 100%(35/35)。

Conclusion: 以事件约束为桥梁的 agent 驱动 Real2Sim 可将单目视频转为显式、可编辑的交互式 4D 实验室世界，支持回放、检视与未来具身科学系统；局限在于仅验证两类液体处理操作、单目视频与小规模评测。

Abstract: Autonomous scientific agents must not only reason about experiments but also interact with the physical environments in which they are executed. Existing laboratory simulators typically rely on manually constructed assets and predefined procedures, while laboratory videos capture real equipment, layouts, and interactions but leave this information implicit in pixels. We introduce LabWorld, an agent-driven Real2Sim framework for reconstructing interactive 4D laboratory worlds from monocular RGB video. LabWorld recovers reusable 3D assets, a metric scene, time-varying object states, and procedure events. Multi-frame observations provide complementary evidence for asset and scene reconstruction, while motion reconstruction combines visual and geometric observations with event constraints encoding the spatial and temporal requirements of laboratory interactions. We evaluate LabWorld on real pipetting and vortexing videos collected in our laboratory against general-purpose agentic and dedicated reconstruction baselines. For pipetting, LabWorld achieves a mean Mask IoU of 0.501, exceeding the strongest baseline by 0.033. For vortexing, it achieves a mean Tube-Length-Normalized PCK-AUC of 0.638, exceeding the strongest baseline by 0.035. On five pipetting videos, event-conditioned reconstruction increases the fraction of modeled physical constraints satisfied from 14.3% (5/35) to 100% (35/35). The resulting worlds expose geometry, motion, articulation, and events as explicit, editable state for replay, inspection, and future embodied scientific systems.

</details>


### [37] [WithinVAE: Compact Visual Autoencoding with Self-Distilled Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36593)
*Yuzhou Ji,Jiepeng Wang,Tianyu Jin,Lizhuang Ma,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WithinVAE：无需预训练识别编码器的紧凑变分视觉自编码器，通过自身EMA副本的DINO/iBOT式自蒸馏与位置相关token掩码学习语义结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义tokenizer通常从预训练识别编码器继承语义，但其不变性会丢弃重建所需的外观细节；论文希望验证语义结构可以在自编码器自身的重建表征内部学得，从而摆脱对外部语义监督或预训练编码器的依赖。

Method: 构建带顺序瓶颈的紧凑变分VAE（4倍压缩比）；从自编码器自身的EMA副本进行DINO/iBOT式自蒸馏，并以位置相关的token掩码作为训练信号；不使用预训练表征编码器或外部语义目标。

Result: ImageNet-1K 256×256上，四项重建指标全面优于RPiAE，PSNR提升1.36 dB且潜变量标量容量仅为其一半；冻结评估下线性探针比RPiAE高6.5点、比仅重建基线高5.1点，证明语义增益来自自蒸馏；latent-to-latent与pixel-to-latent辅助实验复现了压缩有序趋势并将目标迁移至预训练生成空间。

Conclusion: 语义监督的来源与其使用可以分离：紧凑视觉自编码器无需借用识别表征即可自行组织语义结构，同时保持甚至改善重建保真度。

Abstract: Visual autoencoders must balance reconstruction fidelity, compactness, and semantic structure. Existing semantic tokenizers typically inherit semantics from pretrained recognition encoders, whose invariances can discard the appearance detail required for inversion. We show that this inheritance is unnecessary: semantic structure can be learned inside an autoencoder's own reconstructive representation. We introduce WithinVAE, a compact variational visual autoencoder with a sequential bottleneck, DINO/iBOT-style self-distillation from an exponential-moving-average copy of the autoencoder itself, and position-dependent token masking to achieve a 4-fold compression ratio, which is free of pretrained representation encoder or external semantic target. On ImageNet-1K at $256\times256$, WithinVAE improves all four reported reconstruction metrics over RPiAE including PSNR by 1.36 dB, while using half its latent scalar capacity. Under frozen evaluation, the same latents improve linear probing by 6.5 points over RPiAE and 5.1 points over our reconstruction-only baseline, attributing the semantic gains to self-distillation rather than architecture or external supervision. An auxiliary latent-to-latent model reproduces the ordered-compression trend, and a pixel-to-latent model transfers the objective to a pretrained generative space. These results separate the origin of semantic supervision from its use: a compact visual autoencoder can organize semantic structure without borrowing a recognition representation.

</details>


### [38] [4D Reconstruction with Events & RGB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36610)
*Dulanga Weerakoon,Darshana Rathnayake,Van Minh Do,Hemanth Sabbella,Kaichen Zhou,Fangneng Zhan,Alok Prakash,Sanjay E. Sarma,Archan Misra,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在Monst3R的RGB-only 4D重建主干上仅增加一个事件相机输入分支，通过轻量RGB-事件融合提升动态场景重建鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目4D重建的feed-forward方法仅依赖RGB，继承了传统相机在快速/非均匀相机与物体运动下运动模糊、时序混叠的失效模式；事件相机以微秒级异步亮度变化提供互补信号，但能否在不重构架构的前提下融入现有RGB 4D管线尚不明确。

Method: 初步研究：以现有RGB-only模型Monst3R为基线，额外接入一个事件相机输入分支，其余架构保持不变，构成最小化的RGB-事件融合方案；在3个基准数据集上评估，并做运动幅度敏感度分析。

Result: 相对RGB-only Monst3R基线，Abs Rel平均降低3.4%；敏感度分析显示增益随相机与物体运动幅度增大而增大，在物体高速运动、RGB-only退化最严重的条件下增益最大。

Conclusion: 轻量、非侵入式的事件数据融合即可显著提升4D重建鲁棒性，为后续更深入的架构级研究提供动机；当前仅为初步验证，架构设计仍待系统探索。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes over time (4D reconstruction) from a single moving camera is central to robotics and AR/VR applications, yet state-of the-art feed-forward methods rely solely on RGB frames and therefore inherit the failure modes of conventional cameras, including motion blur and temporal aliasing under fast or non-uniform camera and object motion. Event cameras report per-pixel brightness changes asynchronously at microsecond resolution, offering a complementary signal that is largely immune to these artifacts, but whether this modality can be incorporated into existing RGB based 4D reconstruction pipelines without a full architectural redesign remains an open question. In this preliminary study, we investigate a minimal extension: we augment Monst3R, an existing RGB-only 4D reconstruction model, with an additional event-camera input branch, leaving the rest of the architecture unchanged. Across 3 benchmark datasets, this simple RGB-event fusion reduces Abs Rel by 3.4% on average relative to the RGB-only Monst3R baseline, and a sensitivity analysis shows that the improvement grows with the magnitude of camera and object motion, with the largest gains observed under high object motion conditions where RGB-only reconstruction degrades most. These early results suggest that even a lightweight, non-invasive fusion of event data can meaningfully improve 4D reconstruction robustness, motivating a more thorough architectural investigation in future work.

</details>


### [39] [Adapting Video Models for Robot Control via Motion-guided Asymmetric Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36624)
*Taeyoung Kim,Junyoung Sung,Heeseung Kwon,Minseop Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoGAP：在WAM中按运动区域非对称分配视觉预测监督（动态区加噪更重、静态区作上下文），以共享视频扩散骨干的训练期辅助路径提升机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM沿用视频预训练的均匀视觉预测目标，但各视觉区域对机器人控制的相关性不同；作者发现将监督集中于动态区域可显著提升策略性能。

Method: 分析WAM视觉监督的空间分配；提出Motion-Guided Asymmetric Prediction：对图像区域施加非对称噪声，使模型以较少损坏的静态区为上下文重建重度损坏的动态区；该辅助预测路径仅在训练时存在，与标准视频-动作预测共享视频扩散骨干；运动线索直接取自训练视频，无需外部教师或额外模态。

Result: 在多个机器人操作基准与多种WAM架构上一致提升：GR-1桌面基准成功率Fast-WAM（Wan2.2-5B）38.8%→47.5%，Cosmos3-Nano 52.3%→62.3%。

Conclusion: 视觉监督的空间分配是WAM适配机器人域的关键设计维度，运动引导的非对称预测是一种无需额外监督源的通用即插即用改进。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video generative models for robotic control by jointly predicting future visual observations and actions. The visual prediction objective plays a key role in adapting pretrained video representations to the target robot domain. Yet standard visual prediction inherited from video pretraining treats all visual regions uniformly, despite their differing relevance to robotic control. We therefore investigate the spatial allocation of visual supervision in WAMs and show that focusing supervision on dynamic regions can substantially improve policy performance. Building on this finding, we introduce Motion-Guided Asymmetric Prediction (MoGAP), which augments WAM training with motion-focused visual prediction. MoGAP allocates noise asymmetrically across regions, training the model to reconstruct more heavily corrupted dynamic regions using less corrupted static regions as context. MoGAP is trained alongside standard video-action prediction through a training-only auxiliary prediction path within the shared video diffusion backbone. It uses motion cues derived directly from training videos, requiring neither an external teacher nor additional modalities. Across robot manipulation benchmarks and WAM architectures, MoGAP consistently improves policy performance, increasing success rates on the GR-1 tabletop benchmark from 38.8% to 47.5% for Fast-WAM (Wan2.2-5B) and from 52.3% to 62.3% for Cosmos3-Nano.

</details>


### [40] [SAGE: Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts for Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36658)
*Tomoyasu Shimada,Kazuhiko Murasaki,Shoichiro Takeda,Shogo Sato,Riku Inoue,Toshihiko Nishimura,Ryuichi Tanida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE将冻结的预训练点云语义表征引入配准流程，通过语义引导的混合专家（SG-MoE）路由让各专家专精于不同的语义-几何模式，并结合结构感知估计器，在低重叠点云配准中显著减少几何歧义导致的错误对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低重叠场景下，仅依赖几何的方法无法区分几何相似但语义不同的结构（如电视角与桌角、椅腿与桌腿），从而产生错误对应，导致配准失败。作者希望在不增加传感器、不微调语义编码器的前提下，利用语义信息消解几何歧义。

Method: 提出Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts (SAGE)：1) 将冻结的预训练点云语义表征集成进配准管线，无需额外传感器或微调语义编码器；2) 设计Semantic-Guided Mixture of Experts (SG-MoE)，通过语义感知路由使每个专家专注于特定语义-几何模式，抑制仅几何方法易混淆的错配；3) 采用结构感知估计器完成位姿求解。

Result: 在3DLoMatch上取得最高配准召回率，在3DMatch上保持竞争力；在相同精化迭代次数下，于两个基准上均取得最高的内点率与配准召回率。

Conclusion: 实验表明冻结的语义表征能够为低重叠点云配准中的几何歧义问题提供有效线索，语义-几何专家路由是提升对应质量的可行思路。

Abstract: Point cloud registration is an essential task for scene reconstruction, requiring the alignment of partially overlapping point clouds. Under low-overlap conditions, this task remains challenging because existing geometry-only methods cannot distinguish geometrically similar but semantically distinct structures (e.g., TV corners vs. desk corners and chair legs vs. table legs), resulting in incorrect correspondences. To address this geometric ambiguity, we present Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts (SAGE), which integrates frozen pre-trained point cloud semantic representations into the registration pipeline without requiring additional sensors or fine-tuning the semantic encoder. To effectively leverage these semantic distinctions, SAGE employs a Semantic-Guided Mixture of Experts (SG-MoE) architecture. Through semantic-aware routing, SG-MoE enables each expert to specialize in distinct semantic-geometric patterns, reducing mismatches that confound geometry-only methods. With a structure-aware estimator, SAGE achieves the highest registration recall on 3DLoMatch while remaining competitive on 3DMatch. When evaluated with the same number of refinement iterations, SAGE further achieves the highest inlier ratio and registration recall on both benchmarks. These results indicate that frozen semantic representations provide effective cues for resolving geometric ambiguity in low-overlap registration.

</details>


### [41] [Haste Makes Waste? Towards Safe Few-Step Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36665)
*Lior Ziv,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 少步蒸馏文生图模型比其教师模型更不安全，且加速会削弱概念擦除效果；作者提出DICE（蒸馏集成式概念擦除），让潜在构造与保持损失对齐蒸馏方式，在FLUX Schnell上将不安全生成率降至0.046。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏模型因生成快而流行，但其加速对不安全生成率的影响未知；实证发现速度与安全存在张力：少步模型往往比教师更不安全，推理步数越少不安全率越高，且现有概念擦除方法在少步模型上效果显著变差。

Method: 机制诊断：少步模型生成过程脆弱、引导信号被蒸馏进模型的方式会阻碍既有擦除方法。据此提出DICE——一种面向蒸馏后模型的定制化擦除后处理方法，使其潜在空间构造与生成保持损失均与产生该模型的蒸馏过程对齐，从而保证擦除对少步模型"忠实"。

Result: 在FLUX Schnell上，DICE将不安全生成率降至0.046（现有擦除方法至少0.161），同时基本保持良性生成质量。

Conclusion: 少步蒸馏带来的速度—安全权衡可通过与蒸馏过程对齐的定制化概念擦除缓解；概念擦除方法需针对少步模型的特殊性重新设计。

Abstract: While few-step distilled text-to-image models have gained traction due to their fast generation times, the speed-up implications on their unsafe generation rates remain unknown. We uncover an inherent tension between speed and safety: few-step models are often more unsafe than their teachers, and reducing the number of inference steps consistently raises unsafe generation rates. We further find that acceleration makes unsafe concepts harder to erase in few-step models, with existing concept-erasure methods markedly less effective than in teachers. We offer a mechanistic diagnosis for why erasure methods fail on few-step models, finding that the fragility of few-step models' generation process and the way generation guidance is usually distilled into those models may hinder established concept-erasure methods. To address the speed-safety trade-off, we propose DICE (Distillation-Integrated Concept Erasure), a post-distillation erasure method tailored for few-step models. DICE aligns both its latent construction and its generation-preservation loss with the distillation method that produced the model, keeping erasure faithful to few-step models. DICE demonstrates strong results in our evaluation. For example, on FLUX Schnell, DICE reduces unsafe-generation rate to 0.046 (vs. at least 0.161 for prior erasure methods we test), while mostly preserving benign generation quality.

</details>


### [42] [Look Only When Needed: Certificate-Guided Visual Routing for Efficient Diffusion VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36671)
*Donghyeon Cho,Joowon Kim,Seungho Shin,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOWN：无需训练的 D-VLM 解码方法，按需在部分去噪步跳过图像计算并经 KV-cache 复用已认证预测，在保留 99.6% 性能的同时将解码 FLOPs 降至 35.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散视觉语言模型在每一步去噪都处理完整输入，视觉 token 占 89–97%，导致大量重复计算。现有视觉 token 剪枝会永久丢失信息，且仍需每步处理剩余图像。作者实测 LLaDA-V 在每一步有无图像条件下比较，发现移除图像仅影响 22.8%（最低 7.9%）步骤的 token 解锁，说明图像需求是稀疏且因基准而异的。

Method: 提出 LOWN（按需处理图像的解码策略）：每步先用无图像条件预测掩码 token，若无图像预测与上一次含图像预测一致，则复用（"认证"）旧预测并跳过图像前向；被认证的 token 数量决定本步解锁的 token 数，从而自适应调节每步计算量与并行解码宽度。无需训练、无需调阈值，且与 KV-cache 兼容。

Result: 在十个基准上保留 LLaDA-V 99.6% 的得分，平均仅用 35.3% 的解码 FLOPs；验证了图像需求跨基准差异显著，固定调度不如自适应决策；方法可泛化到其他基于 LLaDA 的 D-VLM，并可与视觉 token 剪枝正交组合，KV-cache 下进一步加速。

Conclusion: 图像条件在扩散 VLM 解码中具有强稀疏性，可被"预测一致性认证"机制低成本利用；该策略为 D-VLM 推理加速提供了免训练、可组合、可移植的通用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion vision-language models (DVLMs) process their entire input at every denoising step. Since visual tokens make up 89-97% of this input on the benchmarks we study, most of the decoding computation is spent repeatedly processing the same image. Visual token pruning methods partially relieve this burden by removing part of the visual tokens, but they lose the removed information and still process the remaining image at every step. To measure how often the image is actually needed, we compare LLaDA-V's predictions with and without the image at every decoding step and find that removing the image changes a token being unmasked at only 22.8% of steps on average and at as few as 7.9%. We therefore propose LOWN, a decoding method that decides at every step whether to process the image and how many tokens to unmask. LOWN predicts the masked tokens without the image and processes the image only when this prediction disagrees with the last image-conditioned prediction. Agreement certifies the earlier prediction for reuse, and the number of certified tokens sets how many are unmasked in that step. We further observe that the need for the image differs across benchmarks, so LOWN's adaptive decision is needed rather than a fixed schedule. Without training or a tuned threshold, LOWN retains 99.6% of LLaDA-V's score on ten benchmarks at 35.3% of the decoding FLOPs on average. LOWN also generalizes across LLaDA-based DVLMs, composes orthogonally with visual token pruning, and runs under key-value caching for further efficiency gains. Code and project page are available at this https URL

</details>


### [43] [SAGE: Saliency-Annealed Gated Encoder for Universal Vision Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36719)
*Le Yang,Yucheng Huang,Junyu Chen,Qi Dang,Xin Kong,Haifeng Huo,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE 是一种增强的 ViT-Adapter 骨干网络，通过显著性引导门控、退火训练与两阶段 focal→VFL 损失切换，在 COCO、ADE20K、TinyImageNet、DIOR-R 等多任务上取得 SOTA 或竞争性结果，并在华为 Ascend NPU 上实现多任务统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督 ViT 适配到稠密预测时仅把 ViT 当作被动特征提取器，未充分利用其表征中蕴含的显著性、全局上下文和高频细节等先验。

Method: (1) 显著性引导架构：用 DINO 风格 CLS 显著性引导特征门控与高频增强，配合动态多层聚合与局部细节分支；(2) 退火式训练范式，早期加速收敛、后期抑制特征漂移；(3) 两阶段质量感知训练，在同一次训练中从 focal loss 平滑过渡到 Varifocal Loss。

Result: COCO 62.8 box AP / 55.3 mask AP；COCO 关键点 78.5 AP；ADE20K 语义分割 60.3 mIoU、实例分割 44.9 mask AP；TinyImageNet 94.76% Top-1；DIOR-R 78.8 AP50。同一骨干配不同任务解码器。

Conclusion: SAGE 表明显式利用 ViT 内在先验可有效提升稠密预测多任务性能，并首次在 Ascend NPU 上取得多任务 SOTA；代码与模型待内部审查后发布。

Abstract: When adapting self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs) to dense prediction, existing methods often treat the ViT as a passive feature extractor and may underuse the rich priors encoded in its representations, including saliency, global context, and high-frequency details. We propose SAGE (Saliency-Annealed Gated Encoder), an enhanced ViT-Adapter backbone that explicitly exploits these priors through three complementary innovations: (1) a saliency-guided architecture that extracts DINO-style class-token (CLS) saliency to guide feature gating and high-frequency enhancement, together with dynamic multi-layer aggregation and a local-detail branch; (2) an annealing-based training paradigm designed to improve convergence and feature quality during early training while reducing feature drift later in training; and (3) a two-stage, quality-aware training mechanism that smoothly transitions from focal loss to Varifocal Loss (VFL) within a single training run. Across the evaluated tasks (Fig.~\ref{fig:fig_com}), SAGE achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) or competitive results on COCO (62.8 box AP and 55.3 mask AP), COCO keypoint estimation (78.5 AP), ADE20K (60.3 mIoU for semantic segmentation and 44.9 mask AP for instance segmentation), TinyImageNet classification (94.76\% Top-1 accuracy), and DIOR-R oriented object detection (78.8 AP$_{50}$). The same backbone is used across tasks, with task-specific decoders. To the best of our knowledge, SAGE is the first framework to achieve multi-task state-of-the-art results on Huawei Ascend NPUs. Code and pretrained models will be released after internal review.

</details>


### [44] [Progressively Refining SAM for Accurate Phytoplankton Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36722)
*Jialin Li,Jinkun Jiang,Yuezun Li,Junyu Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于SAM的浮游植物分割框架PhySeg，加入轻量多尺度特征融合细化模块恢复细粒度细节，并手工标注实例级掩码构建首个相关分割数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮游植物生态监测需要精确分割，但其视觉特征细微、像素级标注稀缺，导致该方向研究不足。

Method: 以SAM为骨干，附加轻量细化模块，通过多尺度特征融合逐步恢复细粒度边界与细节；同时对现有数据集进行人工实例级掩码标注，构建浮游植物分割基准数据集。

Result: 实验表明PhySeg提升了分割精度，验证细化模块的有效性；并公开代码与数据集。

Conclusion: 微小的域适应式改进（SAM+细化模块）加数据集即可在显微目标分割上取得提升，但方法新颖性与跨域推广性有限。

Abstract: Accurate phytoplankton segmentation is essential for ecological monitoring, yet it remains largely underexplored due to the subtle visual characteristics of phytoplankton and the scarcity of pixel-level annotations. To address these challenges, we propose PhySeg, a SAM-based segmentation framework equipped with a lightweight refinement module that progressively restores fine-grained details through multi-scale feature fusion. In addition, we construct a phytoplankton segmentation dataset by manually annotating instance-level masks for an existing dataset, providing a valuable resource for future research. Experimental results demonstrate that PhySeg improves segmentation accuracy, validating the effectiveness of the proposed module. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [Hy-Nomas: Towards Free-form Interactive World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36729)
*Qiaoling Chen,Jiaxiang Cheng,Xueqing Deng,Tiankai Hang,Ji Hou,Jiaqi Li,Yifan Liu,Tao Lu,Junshu Tang,Yu Tang,Yao Teng,Yu Tian,Heng Wang,Weizhi Wang,Xiyao Wang,Xilun Wu,Mingyu Yang,Yangbin Yu,Rui Zhao,Longjun Cao,Linghao Chen,Haoyi Duan,Jiaming Han,Mingji Han,Enhua Jiang,Shunkai Li,Yanzhe Liang,Qinwei Ma,Cong Wang,Lei Wang,Bing Wu,Ke Xing,Ray Ying,Zhentao Yu,Key Zhang,Yuan Zhou,Zhengguang Zhou,Kai Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hy-Nomas：8B 扩散视频世界模型，支持多轮自由文本指令与连续相机控制的交互式世界生成，通过对齐结构化视频字幕与动作感知因果注意力实现历史保持与动作窗口隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界生成需要模型在连续多轮指令下既遵循新命令，又保留相关历史与动作后果；现有世界模型在多轮交互一致性、指令时序定位方面不足。

Method: 以8B扩散主干生成可变长分段，条件于当前输入与缓存历史；核心设计是将结构化视频字幕与动作感知因果注意力对齐——描述性上下文跨段可见，动作指令限制在其目标时间窗；视觉语言Prompt Enhancer将用户请求与多模态交互历史转为下一段的结构化条件；多阶段课程学习依次引入因果生成、文本交互、相机控制及模型自生成历史训练，最后做扩散蒸馏；并发布权重、代码、基准与轻量适配流程。

Result: 人工评测中优于现有世界模型，且开源模型权重、代码、基准与适配工作流。

Conclusion: 通过因果注意力与提示增强实现可控、可延续的交互式视频世界模型，具备开源可复现性。

Abstract: Interactive world generation requires models to follow successive commands while preserving relevant history and action consequences. We present Hy-Nomas, a video world model with an 8B diffusion backbone that supports free-form text instructions and continuous camera control across multiple interaction turns. It generates variable-length segments conditioned on current inputs and cached history. Our central design aligns structured video captions with action-aware causal attention: descriptive context remains accessible across segments, while action instructions are restricted to their intended temporal windows. A vision-language Prompt Enhancer (PE) translates user requests and multimodal interaction histories into structured conditions for the next segment. A multi-stage curriculum progressively introduces causal generation, text interaction, camera control, and training on model-generated histories, followed by diffusion distillation. In human evaluation, Hy-Nomas shows superior performance over existing world models. We release model weights, code and benchmarks, together with a lightweight adaptation workflow, to support reproducible research on interactive world generation.

</details>


### [46] [Readout and Operating-Point Sensitivity in Event-Stream Candidate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36743)
*Yifan Shuai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在DailyDVS-200事件相机数据上做被试无关的离线候选选择评测，比较DINO/LeJEPA/MobileNet等事件累积表征的抓捕率-误触发权衡，结论是不存在JEPA特有的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件触发记录需同时兼顾目标捕捉率与误触发率，但表征比较结果会随读出方式与阈值校准而改变；作者还希望检验原始研究中JEPA相对对比学习方法是否存在特异性优势。

Method: 使用520段DailyDVS-200片段、47名被试，按被试划分训练/验证/测试；原始设置变化冻结编码器规模、累积时长与时间视图数；复审扩展加入两个有监督预训练卷积编码器、一个61,586参数小型CNN（3个随机种子）、10次重复被试划分及阈值校准敏感度分析；指标含选定阈值下的抓取/误触发计数与归一化部分AUC。

Result: 50ms/8视图、握手或跌倒策略下，DINO-S CLS为101/132目标、40/388误触发，patch mean为78/132与35/388；DINO-B CLS选定阈值抓取率较低但逐折低FPR部分AUC更高（0.746 vs 0.732）；MobileNetV3-Small为97/132与53/388；10次重复划分平均归一化部分AUC：DINO-B CLS 0.738、MobileNet 0.674；原始LeJEPA S→B提升8.3个点但多重校正置信区间含零；时长与视图数效应依策略而变。

Conclusion: 未发现既定的JEPA特异性优势；结论限于已用开发数据上的离线、动作定义候选选择，不涉及个人记录价值、每小时连续误报率或眼镜能耗。

Abstract: Event-triggered recording needs a useful combination of target capture and false triggers, yet representation comparisons can change with readout and threshold calibration. We evaluate real event accumulations from 520 DailyDVS-200 clips and 47 participants using participant-disjoint training, validation, and testing. The original study varies frozen encoder scale, accumulation duration, and temporal view count; a post-review extension adds two supervised-pretrained convolutional encoders, a 61,586-parameter trained CNN with three seeds, ten repeated participant partitions, and calibration sensitivity. At 50 ms/eight views under the primary handshake-or-fall policy, DINO-S CLS captures 101/132 targets with 40/388 false triggers, versus 78/132 and 35/388 for patch mean. DINO-B CLS has lower selected-threshold capture but higher fold-wise low-FPR partial AUC (0.746 versus 0.732). MobileNetV3-Small captures 97/132 with 53/388 false triggers, demonstrating why lightweight baselines and actual operating points matter. Across ten dependent repartitions, mean normalized partial AUC is 0.738 for DINO-B CLS and 0.674 for MobileNet. The original LeJEPA S-to-B capture difference is 8.3 points, but its multiplicity-adjusted interval includes zero. Duration and view-count effects remain policy dependent. We find no established JEPA-specific advantage. The evidence concerns offline action-defined candidate selection on previously used development data; it does not establish personal recording value, continuous false alarms per hour, or eyewear energy.

</details>


### [47] [Multi-task Disentangling Fusion Mamba-CNN for MODIS Spectral-Temporal Unmixing with Endmember Variability Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36750)
*Motasem Alkayid,Yimin Zhu,Naser El-Sheimy,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MDF-Mamba：按通道光谱专家 + 双向 Mamba 时序分支解耦 MODIS 光谱-时序耦合，用 mHCFusion 稳定融合多专家输出，并在线性混合模型中引入像素级可学习光谱变异权重，配合监督丰度+自监督重建+端元正则的多任务目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MODIS 时序光谱解混在丰度制图与光谱库构建中需求大，但存在三点困难：光谱-时序信息耦合、类内端元光谱变异与类间特征歧义、以及朴素多专家表征融合不稳定。

Method: 解耦架构：卷积模块逐单光谱通道处理，双向状态空间（Mamba）分支编码跨通道季节轨迹，形成多分支专家集成并用 squeeze-and-excitation 做软重加权；mHCFusion 在流形约束 hyper-connection 框架下融合专家输出；以像素级可学习光谱变异权重扩展线性混合模型；多任务训练目标联合监督丰度估计、自监督光谱重建与端元正则。

Result: 在 Alberta 与 Saskatchewan 的 MODIS 时序数据上，14 项指标中 12 项最优（Alberta 7/7，Saskatchewan 5/7），其余 2 项居第二。

Conclusion: 该工作以解耦-融合+变异建模+多任务自监督的组合显著提升 MODIS 时序解混精度，尚未涉及代码/数据公开或跨传感器泛化验证。

Abstract: Spectral-temporal unmixing of MODIS time series is essential for applications such as abundance mapping and spectral library construction, yet it remains challenging due to (i) spectral-temporal information coupling in MODIS data, (ii) intra-class endmember spectral variability and inter-class signature ambiguity, and (iii) the instability of naively fusing multiple expert representations. This paper presents the Multi- task Disentangling Fusion Mamba (MDF-Mamba) to address these limitations. To resolve spectral-temporal coupling, we design a disentangling architecture that pairs per-channel spectral experts with a dedicated bidirectional state-space (Mamba) temporal branch. Convolutional modules each process a single spectral channel, while the Mamba branch encodes the cross-channel seasonal trajectory, forming a multi-branch expert ensemble with soft inter-expert reweighting based on squeeze-and-excitation. For the integration of these spectral and temporal expert outputs, we introduce mHCFusion, adapting the manifold-constrained hyper-connection framework to merge the outputs in a stable and interpretable manner. Endmember variability is addressed by extending the linear mixing model with pixel-wise learnable spectral variability weights that capture intra-class endmember variation. Finally, we design a multi-task training objective that combines supervised abundance estimation with self-supervised spectral reconstruction and endmember regularization terms on the same training data, yielding a highly efficient framework for spectral-temporal unmixing. Experiments on MODIS time series covering the Alberta and Saskatchewan provinces show that MDF-Mamba achieves the best performance on 12 of 14 evaluation metrics across both datasets (7 of 7 on Alberta; 5 of 7 on Saskatchewan), ranking second on the remaining two.

</details>


### [48] [Efficient Remote Sensing Image-Text Retrieval via Interaction-Free Recall and Text-Guided Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36756)
*Xi Chen,Xu Chen,Xiamgyang Jia,Wei Wang,Xu Zhang,Zhenyuan Shun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 fast-then-fine (FTF) 遥感图文检索框架：先用无交互多粒度召回（预计算图库嵌入，免全库 token 级交互），再用 BTIB 参数无关的文本引导 Top-K 重排序细化局部语义对齐，并配合 inter/intra-modal 损失联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像目标密集、背景复杂，现有方法要么用复杂跨模态交互导致检索效率低，要么依赖大规模视觉-语言适配，需海量数据与算力，难以兼顾细粒度对齐与高效检索。

Method: 两阶段检索：1) interaction-free 多粒度召回，离线预计算 gallery 嵌入并与 query 嵌入匹配，避免全库 token 级图文交互；2) parameter-free 的 text-guided cross-modal reranking，用 Balanced Text-guided Interaction Block (BTIB) 在 top-K 候选集内细化局部语义对应；3) 设计 inter- and intra-modal loss 在多粒度表示上联合优化跨模态对齐。

Result: 在公开遥感图文检索基准上取得与现有方法有竞争力的准确率，同时显著提升检索效率。

Conclusion: 以“召回-重排”解耦的多粒度、两阶段范式可在遥感跨模态检索中兼顾细粒度对齐与效率，且重排序模块不引入额外可训练参数；属于遥感领域跨模态检索工作，与具身/3D/世界模型等核心方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Remote sensing (RS) image-text retrieval plays a critical role in understanding massive RS imagery. However, the dense multi-object distribution and complex backgrounds in RS imagery make it difficult to simultaneously achieve fine-grained cross-modal alignment and efficient retrieval. Existing methods either rely on complex cross-modal interactions that lead to low retrieval efficiency, or depend on large-scale vision-language adaptation, which requires massive data and computational resources. To address these issues, we develop a fast-then-fine (FTF) retrieval framework centered on interaction-free multi-granular recall and parameter-free text-guided cross-modal reranking with the Balanced Text-guided Interaction Block (BTIB). Specifically, interaction-free multi-granular recall pre-computes gallery embeddings and matches them with the corresponding query embedding without full-gallery token-level text-image interaction, while text-guided cross-modal reranking with BTIB refines local semantic correspondence within the top-K candidate set. Furthermore, an inter- and intra-modal loss is designed to jointly optimize cross-modal alignment across multi-granular representations. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that the FTF achieves competitive retrieval accuracy while significantly improving retrieval efficiency compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [49] [GeoGSplat: Geometry-Grounded Non-Pixel-Aligned Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36760)
*Jiale Wang,Chen Zhao,Haoyang Zhang,Yanhao Wu,Fei He,Yanhu Shan,Rui Wu,Wei Ke,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoGSplat 提出几何先验引导的非像素对齐前馈高斯泼溅框架：双分支编码器构建 Anchors（粗结构）与 Triplane（细特征），分层解码器逐步解码稠密三维点与高斯基元，实现前馈三维重建与新视角合成，并在 DTU/NRGBD 上超越像素对齐与非像素对齐 SOTA，视角偏移越大优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素对齐前馈高斯泼溅使基元数量随图像分辨率与视角数增长，冗余大、算力成本高；非像素对齐方法虽可预测固定数量高斯，但缺乏显式射线与深度约束，导致高斯中心定位错误并在大视角变化下产生渲染伪影。

Method: 将几何先验显式注入非像素对齐框架。双分支编码器分别生成 Anchors（捕捉粗粒度三维结构）与 Triplane（细粒度场景特征）；随后由分层解码器级联式地先解码稠密三维点、再解码高斯基元，统一支撑重建与新视角合成。

Result: 在 DTU 与 NRGBD 上，新视角合成与重建两项指标均优于像素对齐与非像素对齐的高斯泼溅 SOTA；目标视角距离输入相机越远，几何接地范式的相对优势越显著，表明对大幅视角变化具有更强泛化性。

Conclusion: 以几何接地的非像素对齐建模可在保持固定基元规模（分辨率/视角数不变性）的同时提升位姿敏感精度与外推鲁棒性，为前馈三维重建提供了兼顾效率与几何一致性的范式；但仅报告标准物体级数据集定量结果，缺少开放场景泛化与代码/实时性证据。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel view synthesis, but pixel-aligned prediction makes the primitive count grow with image resolution and view count, leading to substantial redundancy and increased computational cost. Non-pixel-aligned methods address this limitation by predicting a fixed number of Gaussians. Without explicit ray and depth constraints, these methods yield misplaced Gaussian centers, causing rendering artifacts under large viewpoint changes. To address this issue, we introduce GeoGSplat, a geometry-grounded feed-forward model that explicitly incorporates geometric priors into the non-pixel-aligned framework. A dual-branch encoder constructs Anchors and Triplane to capture coarse 3D structure and fine-grained scene features, respectively. Subsequently, a hierarchical decoder progressively decodes dense 3D points and Gaussian primitives, enabling 3D reconstruction and novel view synthesis. GeoGSplat surpasses both state-of-the-art pixel-aligned and non-pixel-aligned Gaussian Splatting methods on DTU and NRGBD in terms of novel view synthesis and reconstruction. The advantage of our geometry-grounded paradigm further increases as target viewpoints move farther from the input cameras, demonstrating stronger generalization to large viewpoint changes. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [50] [Evaluation-Guided Demonstration Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Policies: The Winning Entry in the MetaFine Open Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36765)
*Lifan Zheng,Guotao Chen,Zhenyang Li,Shujie Cui,Qiang Huang,Fan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对MetaFine Open Competition五个任务分别微调π0.5策略，通过修改示范动作并加入“专家续接”数据修复抓取/推动/插入失败，逐指令对比checkpoint，最终以96.66分排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛型VLA落地任务中，单任务策略在抓取、推动、插入等接触丰富环节反复失败，且单一聚合指标会掩盖某些指令上的性能退化，需要针对性的数据侧修正手段。

Method: 每个任务独立微调π0.5，任务特定的训练数据配比与每次预测执行的动作数；以官方示范与专家流程为起点；通过修正示范动作、并从策略实际到达的状态出发加入专家续接轨迹（同时保留名义示范）构成DAgger式数据增强；按每条指令分别比较checkpoint。

Result: 提交方案MAI在MetaFine Open Competition总榜第一，得分96.66；局部评估确认抓取、推动、插入类失败被有效缓解。

Conclusion: 在VLA操作任务中，示范动作修订＋从策略状态触发的专家续接是低成本、高收益的数据增强范式；逐指令评估是避免指标互相掩盖的必要手段。该方法为竞赛工程实践，未报告成功率的消融量化与真机大规模验证。

Abstract: We fine-tuned a separate $\pi_{0.5}$ policy for each of the five tasks in the MetaFine Open Competition, with a task-specific training mixture and number of actions executed per prediction. Training started from the official demonstrations and expert procedures. Local evaluation exposed recurring grasping, pushing, and insertion failures. We addressed them by revising the demonstrated motions and by adding expert continuations from states the policy reached, while keeping nominal demonstrations in training. Checkpoints were compared on every instruction, so that a gain on one instruction could not hide a loss on another. Our submission, MAI, ranked first overall with a score of 96.66.

</details>


### [51] [CVBL-CCTV-Face Dataset for Real-World Face Enhancement and Recognition in Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36781)
*Manish Das,Ajay Kumar Yadav,Shiv Ram Dubey,Satish Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向监控场景的CVBL-CCTV-Face数据集（8MP IP球机抓拍LQ图与受控环境HQ参考图构成的非配对数据）及"人脸检测—深度学习超分—L2归一化嵌入识别"模块化框架，并在实时非约束环境下部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCTV人脸存在低分辨率、运动模糊、噪声与遮挡等退化，导致识别不可靠；现有超分与识别研究缺乏真实非配对监控数据支撑，需构建真实场景数据集并端到端评估超分对识别的作用。

Method: 自建非配对CCTV人脸数据集（真实LQ抓拍 vs 受控HQ参考）；模块化流水线：人脸检测 → 深度学习超分 → 嵌入模型提取L2归一化特征进行身份比对；按三种不同设置训练与测试增强模型，并做实时部署。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅说明在三种实验设置下完成超分模型训练/测试，并在实时非约束监控环境中完成身份验证部署。

Conclusion: 真实非配对监控数据+模块化超分-识别流水线可用于实时身份验证；但缺少与SOTA的定量对比、超分对识别增益的消融证据及代码/数据开放说明，方法论新颖度有限。

Abstract: In real-world surveillance settings, facial captures from closed-circuit television systems suffer from degradations such as low resolution, motion blur, noise, and occlusions, which undermine dependable face recognition. In this study, we propose a framework for super-resolution and identity recognition, aimed at surveillance applications. For this purpose, we present the CVBL-CCTV-Face Dataset which is a dataset of real-world images taken in an unpaired manner where each pair consists of a low-quality (LQ) image of a face captured by a 8MP IP Dome CCTV camera together with a high-quality (HQ) reference image that is taken in controlled settings. In order to cope with the various problems posed by such datasets, we construct a modular system that involves face detection, deep learning-based super-resolution of low-quality face images, and identity recognition through L2-normalized embeddings extracted by an embedding model. Enhancement models are trained and tested according to three different setups, and the system is deployed for identity verification in a real-time unconstrained environment.

</details>


### [52] [OmniGlimpse: Asymmetric Identity Memory for Online Multi-View Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36785)
*Rangya Zhang,Jiaping Xiao,Yujing Shang,Yuhang Zhang,Mingsheng Li,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniGlimpse：非对称"提议-验证"框架做在线多视角身份维护（OMIM），短期局部/跨视角证据在自适应空间提议关联，长期身份分布由冻结编码器定义的规范空间验证并更新记忆，无需离线场景训练或标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相机跟踪需在遮挡/出画时互补观测，但现有方法要么依赖离线场景特化训练与相机标定，要么在线自适应会放大关联错误；作者希望无需部署特定知识即可在线维持跨视角身份一致性。

Method: 形式化在线多视角身份维护问题（每视角仅初始化一次、之后无标签）。表示层面：短期局部与跨视角证据映射到目标特定的自适应空间用于提议关联；长期身份分布保存在由冻结编码器定义的规范空间中用于验证。更新层面：短期证据仅指导当前关联，bootstrapping 之后只有通过验证的观测才能更新持久记忆或驱动自适应。

Result: 统一目标指定评测协议下，M3Track 上较最佳在线基线 IDF1 +9.1、CVIDF1 +22.1；MvMHAT 上分别 +20.3、+26.2。未用离线场景特化训练，IDF1 仍超过离线训练参考方法，跨视角关联具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过"自适应提议 + 稳定验证"的解耦，可在外观变化中自适应同时把可靠证据固化为身份锚点，为无标定、无离线训练的在线多视角身份维护提供可行路线。

Abstract: Multi-view tracking extends visual coverage beyond a single camera, providing complementary observations when targets are occluded or leave the view. This raises a question: how can observations from different views be matched to the correct identities without offline scene-specific training or camera calibration? Progress toward this goal remains limited because many offline formulations assume such deployment-specific knowledge, while online adaptation can amplify association errors. We study this problem as online multi-view identity maintenance (OMIM): targets are initialized once per view, with no further identity labels provided after each initialization. To this end, we introduce OmniGlimpse, an asymmetric propose-verify framework that separates adaptive association from stable identity consolidation. At the representation level, short-term local and cross-view evidence is mapped into target-specific adaptive spaces to propose associations, whereas long-term identity distributions are maintained for verification in a canonical space defined by a frozen encoder. At the update level, short-term evidence may guide current associations, whereas, after bootstrapping, only verified observations may update persistent memory or drive adaptation. Under a unified target-specified evaluation protocol, OmniGlimpse improves over the best evaluated online baseline for each metric by 9.1 IDF1 and 22.1 CVIDF1 points on M3Track. The corresponding gains on MvMHAT are 20.3 and 26.2 points. Despite using no offline scene-specific training, it outperforms offline-trained references in IDF1 and remains competitive in cross-view association. These results support an approach to online identity maintenance that adapts to changing appearances while consolidating reliable evidence into stable identity anchors.

</details>


### [53] [Visual Autoregressive Generation with Next Token Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36793)
*Ruihang Jiang,Zongyao Zhao,Zhaolin Wang,Yuanwei Liu,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Next Token Imagination (NTI)，让视觉自回归生成模型在受约束条件下超越GT下一个token进行"想象"探索，从而更充分地覆盖视觉分布，显著提升ImageNet生成保真度且训练成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉内容空间近乎无穷，而任何训练集只覆盖极小部分；现有生成模型（尤其是视觉AR）仅从GT前序token预测GT下一token，将学习严格限制在观测数据上，限制了模型对视觉分布的覆盖能力。

Method: 以视觉自回归生成为概念验证（离散固定token空间便于引导与约束），提出NTI方法：允许模型在GT下一token之外探索合理的视觉实现，并设计判据将想象结果约束在原视觉分布内，避免漂移。

Result: 在ImageNet上大量实验表明，NTI稳定且显著提升生成图像保真度，在更小模型、更低训练成本下接近SOTA扩散与AR模型。

Conclusion: 训练时允许模型在分布约束下"想象"未见过的合理视觉实现，是缓解数据覆盖局限、提升生成质量的有效范式，可推广至更广的生成建模场景。

Abstract: The space of possible visual content is enormous, and arguably unbounded. Any training set, however large, therefore covers only a tiny fraction of it. Yet generative models are typically trained to fit only the images they are given. In visual autoregressive (AR) generation, for example, each token is predicted from the preceding ground-truth (GT) tokens and supervised solely by the GT next token. Confining learning to observed data in this way limits how much of the visual distribution a model can capture. In this work, we hypothesize that letting a model imagine beyond its training data, by exploring plausible visual realizations it has never observed, can ease this limitation. We use visual AR generation as a proof of concept, since its fixed, discrete token space makes imagination easier to steer and constrain. We propose Next Token Imagination (NTI), a method that allows the model to explore beyond the GT next token, subject to a carefully designed criterion that keeps this imagination bounded by the underlying visual distribution. Extensive experiments on ImageNet show that NTI substantially and consistently improves the fidelity of generated images, approaching state-of-the-art diffusion and AR models while using a smaller model and lower training cost.

</details>


### [54] [Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning with Multi-Branch Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36809)
*Kegeng Tang,Jingbo Wang,Yangming Zhang,Huanshu Zhang,Shaogang Ren,Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分支感知的潜在视觉推理框架（Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning），用边界框证据标注构建目标证据表示并引入软对比分支-证据对齐目标，使多分支潜在视觉假设对齐真实证据区域，在Qwen2.5-VL 3B/7B上提升感知密集型视觉推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将中间推理移入视觉潜在空间的方法多为单一线性推理轨迹，难以应对需要定位并比较多个问题相关视觉证据、存在多个视觉假设或竞争证据区域的复杂视觉推理任务。

Method: 生成多个以问题为条件的潜在视觉分支作为图像token上的软假设；利用边界框证据标注构建目标证据表示；设计软对比分支-证据对齐损失，拉近与标注证据区域相关分支、推远负证据；在Qwen2.5-VL骨干上以标准SFT实例化训练。

Result: 在感知密集型视觉推理基准上，3B与7B规模均优于对应骨干与单路径潜在推理基线，取得整体提升。

Conclusion: 将潜在视觉推理从单轨迹扩展为多分支软假设并用证据对齐训练，可有效增强多证据视觉推理；但实验限于VQA类基准，未涉及具身或三维任务。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have made substantial progress in visual question answering, but complex visual reasoning often requires locating and comparing multiple pieces of question-relevant visual evidence. Existing methods that move intermediate reasoning into the visual latent space usually follow a single linear reasoning trajectory, which can be limiting when multiple visual hypotheses or competing evidence regions are relevant. We propose Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning, a branch-aware latent reasoning framework that generates multiple question-conditioned latent visual branches as soft hypotheses over image tokens. To train these branches, our method uses bounding-box evidence annotations to build target evidence representations and introduces a soft contrastive branch-evidence alignment objective. This objective encourages evidence-relevant branches to align with annotated visual regions while contrasting them against negative evidence. We instantiate our method on Qwen2.5-VL backbones and train it with standard supervised fine-tuning. Experiments on perception-intensive visual reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves overall improvements over the corresponding backbone and single-path latent reasoning baselines at both 3B and 7B scales.

</details>


### [55] [Right Click, Wrong Box: Grading GUI Grounding by What a Click Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36831)
*Xiaokang Yuan,Binhui Yao,Luwei Yang,Ruimin Chen,Zeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出GUI grounding基准（如ScreenSpot-v2）以单一标注框判定对错并不完整，提出以"执行结果是否与标注目标一致"为正确性定义，并用可执行探测（约千次/页）构建outcome map与执行判官，发现大量被判错的点击实际成功；建议在评测中修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分grounder（93–96%）之间差距仅约1分，瓶颈转为剩余错误分析。界面常以多元素暴露同一动作（重复链接、复选框标签、logo旁标题），单框标注使评测低估正确点击，需更完整的正确性准则。

Method: 提出outcome-based正确性定义：执行点击后结果与点击标注目标一致即算正确。构建无新标注的执行探测，对真实Web应用每页执行并指纹化约千次点击，生成outcome map并训练执行判官；在代码托管站与在线商店上测试三个7–8B grounder；对ScreenSpot-v2的11个模型（3个7–8B + 8个3B变体）做盲审，60样本子集由两名独立评审；用多次运行与配对区间控制训练种子与测试采样噪声；并在3B GRPO奖励中对比等效点击奖励与框奖励。

Result: 被框评分拒绝的点击中，约1/5与1/3（改写为自然任务指令后为1/7与1/3）实际达成意图；盲审在网页截图上复现同样效应，最强grounder占比最高；框评分的漏计无法靠重复消除，审计修正使ScreenSpot-v2分数提高0.2–1.8分，与领域报告的模型差距同量级；GRPO中给等效点击加权在3个种子、4个测试集上无稳定增益。开源probe、outcome map、审计与全部预测。

Conclusion: 现有框级评测系统性低估GUI grounding能力，评测层面需引入执行结果一致性的修正；但该修正用于训练奖励并无稳定收益，收益主要体现在评估准确性而非模型改进。

Abstract: Leading GUI grounders score 93 to 96% on ScreenSpot-v2, and reported gaps between new and prior models are about a point. At this level the useful question is what the remaining errors are. Benchmarks count a click as correct only inside one annotated box, but interfaces often expose one action through several elements: a duplicated link, the label of a checkbox, the title next to a logo. The box is not wrong. The rule is incomplete. We call a click correct when executing it produces the same outcome as clicking the annotated target. A probe that executes and fingerprints about a thousand clicks per page turns this definition into outcome maps and an execution judge for live web applications, with no new annotation. We execute the predictions of three strong 7--8B grounders on a code forge and an online shop. About one in five and one in three of the clicks that box scoring rejects reach the intended outcome, and the shares remain one in seven and one in three when the instructions are rewritten as natural tasks. A blind audit of 11 models on ScreenSpot-v2 (three 7--8B grounders and eight 3B variants) finds the same effect on web screenshots, with two independent reviewers on a 60-unit subset. The share is highest for the strongest grounders. Training seeds and test-set sampling add noise that more runs and paired intervals control. Box scoring instead under-counts correct clicks, which repetition does not remove, and the audit-based correction of 0.2 to 1.8 points on ScreenSpot-v2 is as large as the gaps the field reports. The correction is needed in evaluation. In our 3B GRPO study, crediting equivalent clicks in the reward gives no stable gain over box rewards across three seeds and four test sets. We release the probe, the outcome maps, the audit and every prediction.

</details>


### [56] [Drop the Details, Get the Gist: Dynamic Conceptual Representation for Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36832)
*Ryoya Yoshiyama,Bowen Wang,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将概念瓶颈模型（CBM）扩展到过程性视频理解：用分段级概念分数向量序列作为Transformer编码器输入来预测视频类别，并估计各概念贡献度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBM研究集中于静态图像，视频中的时序动态在概念化表示中被忽视，缺少可解释的多步骤视频识别方法。

Method: 把多步骤视频切成片段，每段经概念层得到概念分数向量，形成时序序列输入Transformer编码器做分类；另外提出概念贡献度估计方式；在烹饪与家务活动视频上比较三组不同粒度概念集。

Result: 粗粒度概念即可有效分类这些视频（细粒度文本描述并非必要）；概念贡献度可用于进一步解释。

Conclusion: 概念瓶颈范式可自然延伸到程序性视频，且粗粒度概念足够；属于可解释视频分类的增量工作，与具身/机器人核心议题关联较弱。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), which route predictions through a layer of human-interpretable concepts, have substantially improved the interpretability of visual classification by exposing an explicit, language-based rationale for each prediction. Most existing work focuses on static images, and temporal dynamics in videos have been underexplored in conceptual representation. We propose a framework that extends concept-based image recognition to procedural video understanding. It represents a multi-step video as a temporal sequence of segment-level concept score vectors and feeds this sequence into a Transformer encoder to predict the class label. We also explore a way to estimate the contribution of each concept for further interpretability. We evaluated three concept sets of different granularities on cooking and household-activity videos and found that coarser-grained concepts are sufficient for classifying these videos, which implies that fine-grained textual descriptions may be unnecessary.

</details>


### [57] [MVRCap: Towards Comprehensive Video Captioning via Hierarchical Multi-Dimensional Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36838)
*Weihuang Lin,Xianing Chen,Zhibin Wang,Congzhi Zhang,Yiwei Ma,Jiayi Ji,Miao Chen,Jun Song,YuCheng,Bo Zheng,Xiaoshuai Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVRCap 将视频描述重构为强化学习任务，用"结构化验证"驱动的细粒度可验证奖励（层级概念覆盖、事实性验证、简洁性约束）提供稠密监督，并构建 MVRCap-SFT 结构化标注数据集作为 RL 策略初始化，在 VDC、CAPability、VC4VG 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频描述模型受限于粗粒度监督信号，难以捕捉时空动态与底层逻辑意图，导致描述不全面。作者希望用可验证奖励替代粗粒度标注，施加严格的视觉-语义对齐约束。

Method: 框架三部分：(1) Fine-Grained Verifiable Reward，由三个互补支柱构成——基于新建 MVRCap-QA 的层级概念覆盖奖励（保证多维完整性与语义深度）、事实性验证奖励（提升事实一致）、简洁性约束（最大化语义密度）；(2) 构建 MVRCap-SFT 数据集，按六个关键维度提供结构化标注，用于 RL 策略的稳健初始化；(3) 在该训练管线中以结构化验证引导强化学习优化。

Result: 在 VDC、CAPability、VC4VG 等权威基准上取得 state-of-the-art 性能；实验为广泛的 benchmark 评测。

Conclusion: 细粒度、可验证的奖励设计能有效弥补粗粒度监督的不足，显著提升视频描述的完整性与事实性；结构化 SFT 初始化 + RL 精调是可行管线。但工作属于通用视频理解/描述生成，缺乏与具身智能、3D 重建或机器人任务的直接关联及真实场景验证。

Abstract: Despite significant progress, current models struggle to generate comprehensive video captions, constrained primarily by coarse-grained supervision, which often fails to capture spatial-temporal dynamics and the underlying logical intent. To address this issue, we introduce MVRCap, a novel framework that reformulates video captioning as a reinforcement learning task steered by structured verification. Central to our approach is a Fine-Grained Verifiable Reward mechanism, serving as dense supervision to enforce rigorous visual-semantic alignment. Specifically, we construct this reward system anchored by three complementary pillars: a Hierarchical Concept Coverage Reward utilizing our newly constructed MVRCap-QA dataset to ensure multi-dimensional completeness and semantic depth, a Factuality Verification Reward to enhance factual consistency, and a Conciseness Constraint to maximize semantic density. Furthermore, we curate the high-quality MVRCap-SFT dataset, providing structured annotations across six key dimensions, to serve as a robust foundational initialization for the RL policy in this training pipeline. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MVRCap achieves state-of-the-art performance on authoritative benchmarks including VDC, CAPability, and VC4VG.

</details>


### [58] [AxisQ: Depth-Adaptive Channel--Frequency Quantization for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36844)
*Chi Ruan,Tianqi Li,Xin He,Xue Geng,Ming-Ming Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AxisQ 提出深度自适应的位分配框架，浅层特征在通道域、深层特征在局部频率域分配量化精度，并用边界感知 Hessian 近似做重建，以保持伪装物体检测中的弱纹理与模糊边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装物体检测（COD）依赖微弱纹理与边界线索，激进的训练后量化（PTQ）会破坏这些线索；现有 PTQ 方法多只优化量化器或重建网络块，且在分层主干中始终使用单一精度分配域。

Method: 在固定的逐层平均激活位宽预算下，按特征深度切换分配轴：浅层用通道域分配，深层用局部频率域分配，并以 COD 感知敏感性作为分配依据；同时提出边界感知 Hessian 近似，引入边界加权梯度与误差统计用于重建。

Result: 在多个 COD 模型与基准上，低比特下优于最强基线；W2A2 量化下 CAMO 上加权 F-measure 相对提升 12.7%（CFRN）与 33.3%（CamoFormer-P）。

Conclusion: 将精度分配域与特征深度自适应匹配，可作为分层 COD 模型的可迁移量化策略，在极低位宽下显著缓解弱纹理与边界信息损失。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) depends on weak texture and boundary cues that can be destroyed by aggressive post-training quantization (PTQ). Existing PTQ methods mainly optimize quantizers or reconstruct network blocks, while using a single precision-allocation domain throughout hierarchical backbones. Instead, we introduce AxisQ, a depth-adaptive bit-allocation framework motivated by the observation that the useful allocation axis changes with feature depth. Under a fixed per-layer average activation-bit budget, AxisQ allocates precision in the channel domain for shallow features and in the local-frequency domain for deep features, using COD-aware sensitivity to better preserve subtle foreground regions and ambiguous boundaries. We also formulate a boundary-aware Hessian approximation for reconstruction by incorporating boundary-weighted gradient and error statistics. Experiments across multiple COD models and diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy over the strongest baselines at low bit widths, with relative weighted F-measure gains of 12.7% for CFRN and 33.3% for CamoFormer-P on CAMO under W2A2 quantization. These results support adapting the allocation domain to feature depth as a transferable quantization strategy for hierarchical COD models.

</details>


### [59] [uTHCD-C: A Corruption-Robustness Benchmark for Tamil Handwritten Character Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36850)
*Preethi S,Meeradevi T,Arun Prakash A*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 uTHCD-C，一个基于 uTHCD 数据集的手写泰米尔字符识别（HCR）鲁棒性基准，含 10 种腐蚀类型（含手写特有的笔画细化腐蚀）× 5 个严重度，评测 9 种 CNN/Transformer 架构，用 mCE（以 VGG16-BN 归一化）度量鲁棒性并给出参数量、计算量与吞吐对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泰米尔 HCR 评测几乎只在干净测试图像上进行，缺乏标准化的多腐蚀鲁棒性基准，无法横向比较不同架构在退化条件下的表现。

Method: 构建含 10 类腐蚀（含笔画细化）与 5 级严重度的基准；采用五折分层交叉验证与书写者独立测试；评测 9 种卷积与 Transformer 架构；以 VGG16-BN 为参考模型计算归一化平均腐蚀误差 mCE；同时测量参数量、FLOPs 与推理吞吐。

Result: ConvNeXt 与 Swin 的 mCE 最低（0.75、0.83），ShuffleNet 与 MnasNet 最高（1.55、1.85）；效率测量刻画了鲁棒性与计算开销之间的权衡。

Conclusion: uTHCD-C 通过标准化腐蚀配置、评测协议与对比结果，为鲁棒且高效的泰米尔 HCR 研究提供可复现基线，但工作本身属基准/评测性质，未提出新的识别方法。

Abstract: Evaluation of Tamil handwritten character recognition (HCR) has largely focused on clean test images, and a standardized multi-corruption robustness benchmark for isolated Tamil HCR has not been established. We introduce uTHCD-C, a corruption-robustness benchmark based on the unconstrained Tamil Handwritten Character Database (uTHCD). The benchmark defines ten corruption types, including a handwriting-specific stroke-thinning corruption, at five severity levels. We evaluate nine convolutional and transformer-based architectures using five-fold stratified cross-validation and writer-independent testing. Robustness is quantified using mean Corruption Error (mCE), normalized against a VGG16-BN reference. We additionally compare parameter count, computational complexity, and inference throughput to characterize the computational requirements of the evaluated architectures. The results establish comparative robustness baselines for Tamil HCR, revealing substantial differences across architectures: ConvNeXt and Swin achieve the lowest mCE values (0.75 and 0.83, respectively), whereas ShuffleNet and MnasNet exhibit higher values (1.55 and 1.85, respectively). The accompanying efficiency measurements characterize the trade-offs between corruption robustness and computational requirements. By providing standardized corruption configurations, evaluation protocols, and comparative results, uTHCD-C establishes a reproducible basis for future research on robust and computationally efficient Tamil HCR.

</details>


### [60] [LingGS: Decoupling Feed-Forward Geometry Prediction from Camera Tracking for High-Fidelity Gaussian SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36853)
*Da Li,Yihan Zang,Ivan Viola,Zeyu Wang,Dominik Engel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种在线单目RGB高斯泼溅SLAM系统，用冻结的流式3D基础模型作为虚拟RGB-D传感器提供深度，再以显式几何求解器（特征重投影+高斯地图）进行相机跟踪，在同等优化预算下兼顾重建质量与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB高斯泼溅SLAM在有限优化预算下难以同时获得高质量重建与快速响应的相机跟踪；直接用学习式相机预测精度不足，需要更可靠的跟踪手段。

Method: 冻结的流式3D基础模型从单目视频估计深度，充当虚拟RGB-D传感器；显式几何求解器通过特征重投影估计相机运动；流式高斯映射器将观测融合为稠密场景表示。并验证几何跟踪对多种流式几何骨干的通用增益。

Result: 在室内外数据集上重建质量显著优于现有方法，RTX 4090单卡约7 FPS，吞吐量为对比SLAM系统的2-3倍。

Conclusion: 将学习式几何先验与显式几何相机跟踪结合，可显著提升单目高斯SLAM的跟踪精度与效率，且该几何跟踪策略在不同几何骨干上具备可推广性。

Abstract: Achieving high-quality reconstruction and responsive camera tracking remains challenging for monocular RGB Gaussian splatting SLAM under a limited optimization budget. We present an online system that addresses this challenge by combining learned geometric priors with explicit geometric camera tracking, which can substantially outperform learned camera prediction in terms of tracking accuracy. A frozen streaming 3D foundation model serves as a virtual RGB-D sensor, providing depth estimation from monocular video. An explicit geometric solver then estimates camera motion through feature reprojection, and a streaming Gaussian mapper integrates the resulting observations into a dense scene representation. We further show that geometric tracking improves camera pose accuracy across representative streaming geometry backbones, demonstrating the broader applicability of our approach. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor datasets show that our method significantly improves reconstruction quality over existing approaches, achieving about 7 FPS on a single RTX 4090 and delivering 2-3 times the throughput of the evaluated prior SLAM systems.

</details>


### [61] [Evaluating AlphaEarth Embeddings for Crop Classification: Separability, Temporal Dynamics, and Cross-Regional Transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36857)
*Priyanka Goyal,Maitreyee Sar,Adway Mitra,Manjira Sinha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个与表征无关的评估框架（几何可分性、时间动态、空间稳健性、跨区域泛化），在CONUS与北印度田块尺度数据上系统比较AlphaEarth 64维嵌入与Sentinel-2反射率、NDVI物候、谐波特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EO农业监测正从像元级光谱分析转向表征驱动建模，但对不同学习/工程表征在空间、时间与域泛化维度上的系统性评估仍不足；地理空间基础模型嵌入在农业任务中的效用尚不清楚。

Method: 构建四维度表征评估框架：Fisher比度量线性可分性、Bhattacharyya/Mahalanobis距离度量分布感知可分性、多年度轮作时间动态可分性比、残差Moran's I度量空间自相关、以及US→India跨大陆迁移（Macro F1）。基准对象为AlphaEarth 64维嵌入 vs Sentinel-2反射率、NDVI物候与谐波特征，数据为CONUS 2017–2023及北印度2021田块尺度样本。

Result: 传统特征线性可分性更强（Fisher比高）；嵌入在分布感知分离上更优，光谱相似类别（玉米/大豆）距离可达一个数量级提升（3.9 vs 0.10）。嵌入唯一能捕捉多年轮作动态（可分性比1.784 vs 工程特征最高1.003），且分类残差空间自相关更低（平均Moran's I≈0.17）。但谐波与NDVI特征跨大陆迁移更好（US→India Macro F1 0.493 vs 0.326），分布对齐更佳。组合特征空间表现最均衡。

Conclusion: 揭示了表征丰富性与跨区域可迁移性之间的根本权衡，为大规模作物分类的特征选择提供结构化决策框架，并支持混合特征建模。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO)-based agricultural monitoring is shifting from pixel-level spectral analysis toward representation-driven modeling, yet systematic evaluation of learned representations across spatial, temporal, and domain-generalization dimensions remains limited. Geospatial foundation models generate high-dimensional embeddings encoding spatio-temporal land-surface properties, but their utility for agricultural applications remains insufficiently understood. This study presents a representation-agnostic evaluation framework integrating geometric separability, temporal dynamics, spatial robustness, and cross-regional generalisation. A 64-dimensional AlphaEarth embedding space is benchmarked against Sentinel-2 reflectance, NDVI-derived phenology, and harmonic features using field-scale data from the Conterminous United States (CONUS; 2017-2023) and Northern India (2021). Traditional features achieve strong linear separability (Fisher ratio), while embeddings provide superior distribution-aware separation -- Bhattacharyya and Mahalanobis distances up to an order of magnitude higher for spectrally similar pairs (e.g. Corn/Soybeans: 3.9 vs. 0.10). Embeddings uniquely capture multi-year crop rotation dynamics, reaching a separability ratio of 1.784 versus a maximum of 1.003 across all engineered features, and exhibit lower residual spatial autocorrelation of errors (mean Moran's $I \approx 0.17$). However, harmonic and NDVI features show stronger cross-continental transferability (US $\to$ India Macro F1: 0.493 vs. 0.326 for embeddings), reflecting better distributional alignment. The combined feature space yields balanced performance, supporting hybrid modeling. These findings reveal a fundamental trade-off between representational richness and cross-region transferability, providing a structured framework for feature selection in large-scale crop classification.

</details>


### [62] [Forget the Data, Keep the Capability: Switchable Unlearning for Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36858)
*Jingong Chen,Kyle Gao,Guangyuan Li,Chulwoo Pack,Sijia Liu,Kwanghee Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoME 将预训练重识别骨干拆分为保留检索能力的 core 参数与零初始化寄生参数，并通过梯度隔离训练，实现“关闭分支即遗忘、开启分支即全量”的可切换隐私合规模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私合规要求重识别模型能选择性遗忘特定个体，同时保留对其他人的识别能力；而传统的机器遗忘通常需要重训练或破坏检索性能。

Method: 把一个 backbone 划分为 core 参数（保存检索能力）和零初始化 parasitic 参数（承载全量知识），两者在梯度隔离下训练，使 core 永不接收 forget-set 梯度。关闭分支得 Core 模式（满足删除请求），开启分支得 Full 模式（保留原知识）。理论上证明 Core 对遗忘集的独立性（仅限审计过的耦合通道），并提出基于秩的 Forgetting-Rank 统计量用于审计这种独立性。

Result: 在 Market-1501、VeRi-776、RegDB 上，Core 在三种成员推断攻击下与重训练参考模型相当，同时检索性能高于该参考模型；该检索优势在 ResNet 与 CLIP 骨干上同样成立。

Conclusion: MoME 以开关切换替代重训练来实现重识别模型的选择性遗忘，在隐私合规与检索性能之间取得较好平衡，并提供可审计的独立性刻画。

Abstract: Privacy compliance requires re-identification models to selectively forget individuals while preserving their ability to recognize others. For this, we present MoME, a switchable framework that can adapt pre-trained backbones for privacy compliance, allowing the same model to switch between Full mode for standard inference and Core mode for unlearned inference. MoME splits one backbone into core parameters that preserve retrieval and zero-initialized parasitic parameters that carry the full-data knowledge, and training them under gradient isolation so the core never receives a forget-set gradient. Disabling the branch yields Core, which satisfies a request to delete a person's training data, and enabling it yields Full, which retains what the deleted data taught, so honoring the request is a flag flip rather than a retraining run. We prove Core independent of the forget set up to an audited set of coupling channels and introduce the rank-based Forgetting-Rank statistic to audit that independence in a trained model. On Market-1501, VeRi-776 and RegDB, Core reads at a retrained reference under three membership attacks while retrieving above it, and the retrieval advantage carries to ResNet and CLIP backbones. Code and weights are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [RangeRectQuant: Compressing Scientific Fields for Every Future Rectangular Sum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36873)
*Jiahao Zhang,Xinling Wen,Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RangeRectQuant：通过约束前缀和误差的整数区间，在保持像素范围和指定精确样本的前提下实现坐标协同量化，保证任意轴对齐矩形的求和误差均在预算内，且线性时间、单行工作空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学栅格常在被测区域未知时先行存储；独立逐像素误差界迫使整数精确重建，若用一个绝对误差预算覆盖所有未来矩形查询，代价过高。作者希望在允许像素协同变化的前提下，使任意矩形和依然准确，从而获得更大压缩率。

Method: 以从左上角累加的前缀和为核心：任取四个前缀和即可确定任意矩形总和，故只需控制前缀和误差。RangeRectQuant 保持像素取值范围与指定精确采样点，在每个行位置精确刻画"剩余像素能满足约束"的误差集合，证明该集合为单个整数区间（即使存在多解）；反向遍历求区间端点，前向遍历据此选择量化像素。复杂度为线性时间，工作空间与一行等宽。

Result: 在地理上留出的建筑表面图与水体出现图上的实验显示，在满足相同约束的备选中实现显著存储节省；每个轴对齐矩形均满足给定误差预算。数据生产方只需编码一次，后续用户无需源数据、重编码或按查询修正即可测量任意矩形。

Conclusion: 该工作将矩形查询误差控制转化为前缀和误差的区间可达性分析，给出可证明的误差保证与高压缩率，适用于预先未知查询区域的科学数据存储场景。

Abstract: Scientific rasters are often stored before the regions to be measured are known. Independent pixel-error bounds can force exact integer reconstruction when one absolute error budget covers every future rectangle. Coordinated changes allow every pixel to change while every rectangular sum remains accurate. A prefix sum accumulates values from the top-left corner. Four such sums determine any rectangular total. RangeRectQuant preserves the pixel range and designated exact samples while controlling errors in prefix sums. At each row position, we characterize exactly which errors let the remaining pixels meet these constraints. These errors form a single integer interval, even when many completions exist. A backward pass finds its endpoints; a forward pass uses them to choose quantized pixels. The resulting field meets the prescribed error budget for every axis-aligned rectangle, with linear work and workspace proportional to one row. Experiments on geographically held-out built-surface and water-occurrence maps show substantial storage savings over alternatives meeting the same requirements. Data producers encode once; later users measure arbitrary rectangles without the source, re-encoding, or query-specific correction.

</details>


### [64] [AdaGround: Token-Normalized Adaptive Zooming with Verifier-in-the-Loop Refinement for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36874)
*Bingchao Wang,Yongcheng Jing,Chengyu Wang,Jun Huang,Jie Yang,Wei Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaGround：免训练GUI定位框架，依据模型自估的目标尺寸自适应缩放，并用标记点击Yes/No验证器在回路中筛选/回退/弃答，另以表头组合单元格，显著提升高分辨率小目标定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率屏幕上的GUI grounding主要失败于小目标；缩放是常用免训练补救，但现有缩放流程存在三点缺陷：缩放倍率未与目标尺寸匹配、无条件接受放大后的答案、回答错误后不回溯修正。

Method: 三组件免训练框架：(1) Token-Normalized Adaptive Zooming，依据模型对目标尺寸的自身估计确定倍率、视场与缩放深度；(2) Verifier-in-the-Loop Refinement，用单个"标记点击Yes/No"验证器保留或拒绝每次缩放、在缩放链之间选择、触发重新grounding，并通过存在性查询决定何时弃答；(3) Compositional Structure Grounding，由表头组合出电子表格单元格。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL骨干上超过SOTA免训练缩放方法与众多训练过的GUI模型，在OSWorld-G与ScreenSpot-Pro上相对骨干提升7.4–20.6点，增益可迁移至留出的UI-Vision。

Conclusion: 以自适应缩放+验证器回路+结构组合的免训练组合，可在不改模型参数前提下显著提升高分辨率GUI grounding，并具备跨基准泛化性。

Abstract: GUI grounding on high-resolution screens fails mostly on small targets, and zooming in is a common training-free remedy. However, most existing zooming pipelines do not match the zoom to the target's size, accept the zoomed answer unconditionally and never revisit a wrong answer. To address these issues, we propose AdaGround, a training-free framework with three components: (1) Token-Normalized Adaptive Zooming, which sets the magnification, field of view and zoom depth of each sample from the model's own estimate of the target size; (2) Verifier-in-the-Loop Refinement, which uses one marked-click Yes/No verifier to keep or reject each zoom, choose between zoom chains, trigger re-grounding and, with an existence query, decide when to abstain; and (3) Compositional Structure Grounding, which composes spreadsheet cells from their headers. On Qwen3-VL models, AdaGround surpasses state-of-the-art training-free zooming methods and many trained GUI models, improving accuracy over the backbone by 7.4 to 20.6 points on OSWorld-G and ScreenSpot-Pro, and its gains carry over to held-out UI-Vision. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [65] [HoloRecon: Incremental Reconstruction of Long-Range Panoramas with Global Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36876)
*Zhuoxiao Li,Xinyi Liu,Hongye Hou,Jing OU,Kang Du,Shuai Zhang,Dongli Wu,Taoyu Wu,Siyuan Feng,Xiaohui Zhu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全景重建框架：将ORB稀疏全景SLAM前端与微调的前馈单目几何模型耦合，利用预测的局部几何对远距离重访的特征匹配做3D验证，生成长程相机约束，标定深度尺度并优化全局位姿图，从而更新稠密几何；并发布PanoRecon七场景基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列全景重建需细节几何与全局一致位姿，但重复结构、小重叠使特征对应在远距离重访处不可靠，漂移无法消除；前馈模型局部几何精细但算力受限、窗口小，缺乏全局一致性。

Method: ORB特征跟踪+稀疏全景建图前端与前馈深度/几何模型紧耦合：用稀疏地标标定预测深度尺度，以前馈局部几何对跨重访匹配做3D验证以得到长程约束，回环/跨区域连接入全局位姿图优化，再以校正位姿更新稠密几何；配套PanoRecon基准（7场景，重复建筑、大小回环、参考位姿与LiDAR）。

Result: 在评测方法中取得最低平均绝对轨迹误差(ATE)与较高F-1分数。

Conclusion: 稀疏特征SLAM与前馈局部几何互补有实效：预测几何可作为跨重访的3D验证器弥补特征匹配脆弱性，实现长序列全局一致的全景稠密建图。

Abstract: Long-sequence panoramic reconstruction requires detailed scene geometry and globally consistent camera poses across distant revisits. Panoramic SLAM and incremental SfM rely on feature correspondences, but repeated, similar structures and small partial overlap make reliable long-range connections difficult to establish, leaving accumulated drift unresolved. Feed-forward models offer detailed local geometry, but their high computational costs limit inference window size, necessitating additional constraints for global consistency. This motivates combining continuous feature-based tracking and sparse mapping with rapid, high-quality local reconstruction from feed-forward models to achieve accurate localization and globally consistent dense mapping over long sequences. We therefore propose a panoramic reconstruction framework that couples an ORB-based sparse panoramic mapping frontend with a fine-tuned feed-forward model. Our key insight is that predicted local geometry enables 3D verification of feature matches across distant revisits, yielding long-range camera constraints even between regions. We calibrate predicted depths with sparse landmarks and use verified connections to optimize the global pose graph, updating dense geometry with the corrected camera poses. We also introduce PanoRecon, a seven-scene benchmark with repetitive architecture, large and small loops, and reference camera poses and LiDAR geometry for evaluating global consistency. Our method achieves the lowest mean absolute trajectory error and high F-1 Score among the evaluated methods. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [66] [PREEN: Exemplar-Based Color Style Transfer with Smartphone Editor Presets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36896)
*Beomjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PREEN 将颜色风格迁移转化为对确定性图像编辑器 29 个参数的回归，输出可解释、可被编辑器直接使用的预设向量而非栅格图像；借教师模型生成伪预设监督，并用 LoRA + CIELAB 图像级损失微调，在 PST50 上优于基线，且具备单旋钮连续强度调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式颜色风格迁移输出的是栅格图，缺乏可编辑性与可解释性；而直接用编辑器参数回归又缺少真实参考图对应的预设真值监督。作者希望在低维、可解释的编辑器参数空间中完成风格迁移，并保留可控性。

Method: 以风格迁移教师模型的目标效果反演为编辑器伪预设标签；预训练 CLIP 条件回归网络，再以图像级 CIELAB 损失用 LoRA 微调；推理输出 29 维预设向量，并暴露单一强度旋钮在一次前向中生成从默认到鲜艳的连续路径。

Result: 在 PST50 基准上，PREEN 在像素级与感知保真度指标上领先基线，同时较好保持结构保真；预设紧凑、端侧亚秒级推理；双轴人工评测显示，评分者认为鲜艳端更贴近参考风格，而默认端作为独立照片更佳。

Conclusion: 将风格迁移表述为编辑器参数回归是可行且高效的：监督可通过教师反演获得，参数空间低维性天然带来紧凑表示、鲁棒泛化与连续可控性（强度旋钮无需重训即可覆盖风格匹配—照片自然度的权衡）。

Abstract: We present PREEN (Preset Regression via an Exemplar-based Editor Network), which casts color style transfer as regression onto the 29 parameters of a deterministic image editor. Given a content image and a style reference, PREEN predicts a preset, a vector of editor slider values that is interpretable and editor-consumable, not a raster image. The key obstacle is supervision, since an arbitrary real reference lacks a preset ground truth. We therefore supervise with a style-transfer teacher, whose target looks we invert into the editor's parameter space as pseudo-presets, then pretrain a CLIP-conditioned regressor and refine it with LoRA under an image-level CIELAB loss. On the human-curated PST50 benchmark, PREEN applies the reference's color while preserving structural fidelity, leading the baselines on pixel- and perceptual-fidelity metrics. The preset is compact and predicted at sub-second on-device latency. The low-dimensional subspace also exposes a single inference-time strength knob that traces a continuous default-to-vivid trajectory in one forward pass, with every intermediate state a valid editor preset. A two-axis human study shows that raters prefer the vivid end for matching the reference but the default as a standalone photo, a trade-off the knob spans without retraining.

</details>


### [67] [Dense Representation Learning and Differentiable Shape Modeling for Spine Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36951)
*Augustin Daridon,Sofiane Sifaoui,Ayman Assi,Mohamad Karam,Wafa Skalli,Elsa Angelini,Loic Le Folgoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reframes spine landmark detection as pixel-level contrastive representation learning, matching dense pixel embeddings to landmark prototypes, plus learnable global shape priors, improving heatmap discriminability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional heatmap regression for spinal landmark detection suffers from ambiguous heatmaps that confound corners of adjacent vertebrae; the authors seek a formulation yielding more discriminative landmark heatmaps.

Method: Pixel-level contrastive learning framework: dense pixel embeddings matched to landmark prototypes instead of direct heatmap regression, with a generic U-Net backbone; heatmaps then combined with learnable shape priors over the global landmark configuration to disambiguate locations.

Result: Competitive with state-of-the-art in landmark accuracy and clinical parameter estimation on the public AASCE dataset and two private spinal X-ray datasets.

Conclusion: Contrastive prototype matching is a simple, practical alternative to heatmap regression for anatomical landmark detection, and shape priors effectively resolve residual ambiguity.

Abstract: Spine landmark detection from spinal X-ray is a key step for estimating spine deformation severity, for building biomechanical models, and for pre-operative planning. We reframe anatomical landmark detection as a pixel-level contrastive representation learning problem, matching dense pixel embeddings with landmark prototypes. Surprisingly, even with a generic U-Net backbone, this formulation results in more discriminative heatmaps compared to the traditional heatmap regression paradigm, leading to improved discrimination between vertebral corners on distinct vertebra. Furthermore, we combine the resulting heatmaps with learnable shape priors over the global configuration of spinal landmarks to resolve ambiguities in landmark locations, yielding a simple and practical approach to spinal landmark detection. Our approach is competitive, in terms of landmark accuracy and clinical parameter estimation, with the state-of-the-art on the public AASCE dataset and on two private spinal X-ray datasets. Our code and pre-trained weights are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [68] [Mining the Nineteenth Century for the Thirteenth: Register-Scale Forced Alignment and HTR Bootstrapping via Legacy Regesta and Antiquarian Editions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36956)
*Tamas Kovacs,Daniel Luger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用十九世纪Pressutti regesta等"遗层"文献辅助教皇Honorius III登记册的手写文本识别（HTR），提出正交、词汇、拓扑三层分工框架：将旧版原文投影为教廷拼写并回放现代行可作零标签自适应目标，WER降低1.50点；遗留词表覆盖未编辑条目OOV词82.2%，并可蒸馏中世纪NER模型达到F1 82.1%；regesta日期条款与罗马数字形定位73.9%条目起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reg. Vat. 9仅有第1-2年度的现代专家转写，其余登记册仅有Pressutti regesta与部分旧版。作者希望量化这些旧层文献对HTR的实际贡献与边界，而非将其视为现代转写的替代品。

Method: 在fol. 1-203双层重叠区先审计转写，再分三层评估：(1) 正交层——拼写投影(curial spelling)+现代源行回放构成的零标签自适应；(2) 词汇层——语料级遗留词表用于OOV覆盖与识别错误检测，并将预训练中世纪NER蒸馏进识别器NER头；(3) 拓扑层——regesta起止条款、日期转教廷罗马形用于条目分段，并以版本一致性估计CER。

Result: 第2年度134个版面池化后WER下降1.50点（95% CI −1.84~−1.15），闭合至oracle差距的33%；拼写变化发生在年度分界（抄写员或转写者所致），而字迹仅在fol. 191r可见变化。遗留词表覆盖82.2% OOV词，错误检测精确率66.7%；蒸馏NER在专家验证集上F1 82.1%，与识别后最佳标注器持平，达到CER 7.80%（fol. 203之后估算）。

Conclusion: 古籍学术文献不能取代现代转写，但可在未转写登记册上用于引导、校验与分段HTR，形成可量化的三层辅助机制。

Abstract: Handwritten text recognition (HTR) for the registers of Pope Honorius III depends on modern expert transcriptions, which exist only for regnal years 1-2 of Reg. Vat. 9. For the remaining registers, nineteenth-century scholarship offers Pressutti's regesta and partial editions. On fol. 1-203, where both layers exist, we measure what this legacy layer can contribute, on transcriptions that we first audit against it, and find a division of labor across three tiers. At the orthographic tier, raw editions are at best neutral for adapting a recognizer to the second regnal year and harmful without replay, whereas projection onto curial spelling with replay of modern source lines turns them into zero-label adaptation targets: pooled over all 134 year-2 sides, the WER falls by 1.50 points (95% CI -1.84 to -1.15), closing 33% of the gap to an oracle; the spelling changes at the year split, through the scribe or the transcriber, whereas the hand changes visibly only at fol. 191r. At the lexical tier, the corpus-wide legacy lexicon contains 82.2% of the out-of-vocabulary words of unedited entries and flags recognition errors with 66.7% precision, and labels from pre-trained medieval NER models, distilled into the recognizer's NER head, reach F1 82.1% on an expert validated benchmark, level with the best tagger applied after recognition. At the topological tier, Pressutti's regests, the closing datum clauses, and the regests' dates converted to the chancery's Roman form find 73.9% of entry starts in the recognized line stream, and edition agreement estimates a CER of 7.80% beyond fol. 203. Antiquarian scholarship thus cannot replace modern transcription, but it can steer, check, and segment HTR across the untranscribed registers.

</details>


### [69] [Explicit Regional Representations For Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36978)
*Fuyi Yang,Lei Yang,Andrew Liu,Chenyu Wei,Xiaoyi Guo,Ming Kai Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出像素级区域表示 QRH（Quality-Relevance Heatmap），在视觉语言模型引导下融合局部退化证据与感知先验（无需像素级标注），并以分布保持采样与质量感知采样两种确定性片段采样策略将其接入现有 VQA 模型（如 DOVER）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视频质量评估（VQA）模型的预测依据基本隐含，缺乏可解释的区域级证据，作者希望为 VQA 提供一个显式的区域表示接口。

Method: 在 VLM 引导下构造逐像素的 QRH 热图，融合局部退化证据与感知先验，无需像素级标注；再通过两种确定性片段采样策略接入下游 VQA 模型：分布保持采样（免微调，近似原采样器的 QRH 分数分布）与质量感知采样（优先高 QRH 区域并配合微调）。

Result: 以 DOVER 在多个基准上实验：分布保持采样可很好地逼近随机采样平均预测；质量感知采样在多数主流 VQA 基准上提升大部分评估指标。

Conclusion: 显式区域表示可作为下游 VQA 的有效接口；但该工作属视频质量评估领域，与物理 AI/具身智能核心方向关联有限。

Abstract: End-to-end video quality assessment (VQA) methods leave the regional evidence underlying their predictions largely implicit. We introduce an explicit pixel-level regional representation for VQA, instantiated as a Quality-Relevance Heatmap (QRH) that combines localized degradation evidence with perceptual priors under vision-language model guidance, without requiring pixel-level annotations. We apply QRH to existing VQA models through two deterministic fragment-sampling strategies: distribution-preserving sampling approximates the original sampler's QRH-score distribution without retraining, while quality-aware sampling prioritizes regions with high QRH scores and is paired with fine-tuning. Experiments with DOVER on multiple benchmarks show that the former closely approximates predictions averaged over random samples, while the latter improves most evaluated metrics across popular VQA benchmarks. These results support explicit regional representations as a useful interface for downstream VQA.

</details>


### [70] [LearnPrunerV2: Learnable Progressive Token Pruning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36986)
*Rinyoichi Takezoe,Yaqian Li,Weiren Wang,Zihao Bo,Huihong Shi,Anzhou Hou,Mo Guang,Jiale Yan,Kaiwen Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LearnPrunerV2 将两阶段启发式视觉 token 剪枝扩展为全可学习架构：在 VLM 每层插入轻量可学习模块，融合内在视觉显著性与通过 shadow token 捕获的查询相关性来估计 token 重要性，并提出预算确定剪枝（BDP）实现固定逐层预算的硬件友好调度，在剪除 77.8% 视觉 token 时保留 LLaVA-v1.5 99.8% 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 的视觉 token 数量庞大导致计算开销过高；现有剪枝方法存在两点不足：剪枝准则不可靠（注意力偏置或与查询无关，难以定位任务相关区域），以及剪枝策略僵化、采用非自适应的固定调度，忽略冗余度随模型深度动态演化。

Method: 1) 在 VLM backbone 每一层集成轻量可学习模块，估计 token 重要性；2) 重要性由内在视觉显著性（intrinsic visual saliency）与通过 shadow token 捕获的外在查询相关性（extrinsic query relevance）融合而成；3) 发现跨层存在一致的“阶梯式冗余模式”（staircase redundancy pattern）；4) 基于该模式提出 Budget-Deterministic Pruning (BDP)，构造每层预算固定的硬件友好剪枝调度。

Result: 在多种 benchmark 与多种 VLM 上一致优于此前 SOTA 剪枝方法；在 LLaVA-v1.5 上剪除 77.8% 视觉 token 仍保持原始性能的 99.8%，取得更优的精度—效率折衷。

Conclusion: 用全可学习、查询感知的分层重要性估计替代启发式判据，并用阶梯冗余模式指导的固定预算调度替代刚性调度，可在极高剪枝率下近乎无损地加速 VLM 推理，是一种可落地的推理效率优化方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable multimodal capabilities but suffer from prohibitive computational overhead due to the massive number of visual tokens. While token pruning is a promising solution, existing methods still suffer from two major drawbacks: 1) unreliable pruning criteria that fail to accurately localize task-specific regions due to inherent attention biases or query-agnostic designs, and 2) suboptimal pruning strategies that employ rigid, non-adaptive schedules, overlooking the dynamic evolution of token redundancy across different model depths. To address these challenges, we propose LearnPrunerV2, which extends the previous two-stage heuristic framework (LearnPruner) into a fully learnable architecture. Specifically, we integrate lightweight learnable modules into every layer of the VLM backbone, which estimate token importance by fusing intrinsic visual saliency with extrinsic query relevance captured via a shadow token. By uncovering a consistent staircase redundancy pattern across VLM layers, we further introduce Budget-Deterministic Pruning (BDP) to establish a hardware-friendly schedule with fixed per-layer budgets. Extensive experimental results across various benchmarks and VLMs demonstrate that LearnPrunerV2 consistently outperform previous state-of-the-art methods.
