Building upon the HSG, we pose assignment as a graph partition task, and employ a heuristic solution that measures vertex affinity for partitioning adaptively. The entire pipeline is training-free. Comprehensive experiments on two challenging event datasets, eTram and Ev-UAV, show that our method outperforms existing event-based and most image-based association models. Particularly, it achieves over 2$\times$ the performance of OC-SORT across all metrics in the many-to-many association scenario.

</details>


### [106] [ScIntBench: Physical Interaction Plausibility in 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37326)
*Wendi Ying,Daochang Liu,Leonid Sigal,Ajmal Saeed Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ScIntBench 基准，用物理仿真中的操作动作（推、开、表面探测）评估文本生成 3D 室内场景的“交互就绪性”，发现语义与静态物理指标好并不等于可交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件 3D 室内场景生成评测偏重静态语义保真度与物理合理性，缺乏对交互运行时物理可靠性的评估，导致生成场景可能无法支撑真实操作。

Method: 构建统一基准 ScIntBench：在物理仿真中执行规定操作动作，度量任务成功率、能力与进度、交互后完整性、动态合理性，并用多视角语义与静态物理评测作为补充与解释；系统评测 12 个 SOTA 系统、10 类房间。

Result: 静态合理性近乎完美的系统交互完成率仅 11.54%；12 个系统中 7 个生成的场景无法支持任何交互；最佳系统任务成功率仅 36.92%。

Conclusion: 交互就绪性存在两项根本挑战：提供必要的物理交互能力，以及在主动操作下保证物理行为可靠；静态指标不能替代交互评测。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation largely emphasize semantic fidelity and physical validity at rest, leaving interaction-time physical reliability insufficiently assessed. To address this gap, we introduce ScIntBench, a unified benchmark centered on interaction readiness: it executes prescribed manipulation actions (e.g., pushing, opening, and surface probing) in physics simulation and measures task success, capability and progress, post-interaction integrity, and dynamic plausibility. Multi-view semantic and static physical evaluations complement these metrics and help explain interaction outcomes. We systematically evaluate 12 state-of-the-art scene generation systems across 10 room types. Our results reveal that strong semantic and static physical performance does not necessarily translate into interaction readiness: a system with near-perfect static plausibility completes only 11.54% of interactions, 7 of the 12 systems produce scenes that cannot support any interaction, and even the best system succeeds in only 36.92% of prescribed tasks. These findings highlight two fundamental challenges in generating interaction-ready scenes: providing the necessary physical interaction capabilities and ensuring reliable physical behavior under active manipulation.

</details>


### [107] [CodeCap-Bench: Can Code Serve as a Good Video Caption?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37341)
*Jiafu Tang,Yifan Xu,Eric Tang,Junjie Zhao,Shipeng Luo,Te Sun,Haoxuan Hu,Yiyan Ji,Xinlong Chen,Zihao Wang,Yang Shi,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"Code as Video Caption"范式，用可执行程序（图形图元、布局规则、时间函数）作为视频的补充表示，并构建CodeCap-Bench（323个视频、5类结构化2D内容）评估MLLM生成忠实代码描述的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言难以精确、无歧义地表达细粒度空间结构与时间动态，导致视频描述冗长且模糊；需要一种更精确紧凑的视觉信息载体。

Method: 以可执行代码作为视频描述形式；在下游问答与视频重建任务上对比代码描述与自然语言描述；提出CodeCap-Bench基准，含323个视频、细粒度标注与多级评测框架，系统评测主流MLLM的代码描述生成能力。

Result: 代码描述在推理准确率和重建保真度上优于自然语言描述；领先的闭源MLLM展现出初步的代码描述能力，但对复杂时间动态的忠实刻画仍不足，开源模型明显落后。

Conclusion: 代码是一种有效且更精确的视频表示形式，但其潜力受限于当前MLLM的忠实生成能力；CodeCap-Bench为后续研究提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Video captioning aims to preserve visual information through compact descriptions, predominantly using natural language. However, precisely expressing fine-grained spatial structures and temporal dynamics in natural language often leads to lengthy and potentially ambiguous descriptions. In this work, we explore Code as Video Caption, a paradigm where executable programs serve as a complementary representation of visual content through graphical primitives, layout rules, and functions of time. To examine whether code captions effectively preserve video information, we compare them with natural language captions on downstream question answering and video reconstruction tasks. Our results show that code captions achieve superior reasoning accuracy and reconstruction fidelity. Despite these advantages, realizing the potential of code captioning depends on whether current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate faithful code captions. To systematically assess this capability, we introduce CodeCap-Bench, which comprises 323 videos spanning five categories of structured 2D content, along with fine-grained annotations and a multi-level evaluation framework. Extensive experiments reveal that while leading proprietary MLLMs demonstrate promising code captioning capabilities, they still struggle to faithfully capture complex temporal dynamics, with open-source models lagging substantially behind.

</details>


### [108] [TimeLineXR: An Interactive Exploration of Time in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37349)
*Nidham Tekaya,Markus Passecker,Matthias Zeppelzauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeLineXR 把 VLM 潜在空间中的"时间轴"可视化为可交互的 3D/WebXR 场景，用户上传图像即可看到其在彩色编码时间轴上的落点与估计年份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Tekaya et al. 2025）定量证明 VLM 嵌入空间中存在低维非线性的时间流形，但该结构对终端用户不可见、不可查询、也无法检查查询点相对其他年份的位置，缺乏可交互的探查界面。

Method: 统一预测管线：CLIP 编码查询图像 → 余弦核 KPCA 投影 → 对齐（snap）到 Bézier 拟合的时间轴。两个客户端（Web demo 与 WebXR 房间尺度场景）共享同一缓存时间索引；XR 端支持抓取、缩放、检视时间轴，并可通过"投掷图像卡片"进行查询。

Result: 摘要仅描述系统能力：每张图像被映射为时间轴上的一个点，用户可比较不同物体的落点并读取其估计的首个年份；未给出定量评估或用户研究结果。

Conclusion: 属于交互式可视化演示系统，复用已有方法完成工程集成与 XR 呈现，方法学新颖性有限，与 Physical AI/VLA/世界模型/3D 重建/具身操作等核心方向关联较弱，更接近多模态表征可视化的工具性工作。

Abstract: Recently, it has been shown that vision-language models (VLMs) have a certain intrinsic understanding of time. They can estimate the temporal origin of objects and encode time along a low-dimensional, non-linear manifold inside their embedding space~\cite{tekaya2025matter}. That result is quantitative and reproducible, but the structure itself stays out of reach for end users: they cannot see it, query it with their own images, or inspect where a query lands relative to other years on the time axis. We present TimeLineXR, an interactive demonstration that turns this time axis into an explorable 3D scene. A web demo lets users upload or select images and see each placed on a color-coded 3D time axis; a this http URL WebXR scene renders the same time axis as a room-scale representation that can be grabbed, scaled, inspected, and queried by throwing image cards into it. Both clients run the same prediction pipeline against the same cached temporal index: encode the query with CLIP, project it through cosine-kernel KPCA, and snap it to the Bézier time axis. The system turns each image into a point on the time axis, so users can compare where different objects land and read off their estimated first year of this http URL source and reproduction steps can be found in: this https URL .

</details>


### [109] [CTBakeGS: training-free baking of photorealistic CT volume renderings into 3D Gaussian splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37406)
*Haruki Fukuda,Constantin Lux,Wolf Schweitzer,Tadashi Hosoya,Lars Ebert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 无需训练的CT体渲染到3DGS转换：在毫米级自适应八叉树上放置splat，用GPU按渲染器光照计算颜色与不透明度，解析地将体素不透明度转为splat不透明度，并对低于1/255下限的弱splat做随机保留；6例公开CT在3种传递函数下PSNR约30-33dB，笔记本单次转换4-30s、实时68FPS渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT体渲染需要原始数据、专用软件与强GPU；3DGS可在弱设备上实时显示照片级场景，但已有医学方法需对每个场景训练数分钟至数小时，成本高。

Method: 提出免训练的直接转换：在毫米级自适应八分体上放置splat，在GPU上按体渲染器光照计算颜色与不透明度，解析地把体素不透明度映射为splat不透明度，并以随机保留策略处理低于标准3DGS光栅化器1/255不透明度下限的弱splat。

Result: 200万splat模型PSNR为骨骼32.6±0.9dB、增强血管30.1±1.0dB、皮肤+骨骼32.3±1.5dB；单次转换4-30s（笔记本）或9-60s（4GB显卡台式机），笔记本上800×800分辨率达68FPS。

Conclusion: 免训练转换可在一分钟内于普通计算机上生成照片级CT渲染的实时3DGS模型，保真度足以支持交互式查看。

Abstract: Photorealistic volume rendering of computed tomography (CT) may convey anatomy intuitively, but it needs the CT data, dedicated software and a powerful graphics processor. Three-dimensional Gaussian splatting (3DGS) displays photorealistic scenes in real time on less powerful devices, but existing medical approaches train each scene for minutes to hours. We aimed to convert a CT volume rendering directly into a 3DGS model without training. Splats were placed on an adaptive octree of millimetre-sized cells. Their colour and opacity were computed on the graphics processor with the renderer's lighting, cell opacity was converted analytically into splat opacity, and faint splats were retained stochastically above the 1/255 opacity floor of standard 3DGS rasterisers. Six public CT scans (1.5-mm voxels) were converted with three transfer functions and compared with converged references by peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity and a learned perceptual metric. Two-million-splat models reached a PSNR of 32.6 +/- 0.9 dB for bone, 30.1 +/- 1.0 dB for contrast-enhanced vessels and 32.3 +/- 1.5 dB for skin and bone. A conversion pass (usually two or three per model) took 4-30 s on a current laptop and 9-60 s on a desktop with a 4-GB graphics card. On the laptop, the models rendered at 68 frames per second at 800 x 800 pixels. Training-free conversion produced real-time 3DGS models of photorealistic CT renderings in about a minute or less per conversion pass on ordinary computers, with fidelity that we consider sufficient for interactive viewing.

</details>


### [110] [Segmentation Confidence for Arbitrary CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37431)
*Baris Oguz,Xing Yao,Claudia Tawil,Alison Pouch,Brett Byram,Gabriel Arenas,Nadav Schwartz,Ipek Oguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究可加装到已有分割流程、无需从头训练的基于体素的预测不确定性估计方法，并提出从不确定图导出单张置信分数的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN医学图像分割缺乏内在置信度量；现有不确定性估计方法需要定制网络结构和专用训练流程，且需从头训练，单输入置信分的推导也较为困难。

Method: 探索可即插即用于现有分割管线的体素级不确定性估计方法；提出由不确定性图聚合为单一置信分数的计算方法。

Result: 在不同ROI和成像模态上的评估表明，所提置信分数能够预测模型在未见数据上的分割性能。

Conclusion: 基于体素的不确定性估计与置信分数可在不重新训练的前提下用于医学分割的质量自评估。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) are widely used for medical image segmentation. However, they lack an inherent measure of confidence. Existing methods for estimating prediction uncertainty often require a custom network architecture and/or dedicated training setups and need to be trained from scratch. In addition, the deduction of a single confidence score per input from the uncertainty maps is not always straightforward. In this study, we explore voxel-based uncertainty estimation methods that can be added to existing segmentation pipelines without training from scratch. We also propose a novel method to estimate a single confidence score from the uncertainty maps. Our evaluations on different ROIs and modalities suggest that our confidence score can predict segmentation performance in unseen data.

</details>


### [111] [Representing the Unseen: Rethinking Visual Imagination for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37437)
*Zengqi Zhao,Zun Wang,Shoubin Yu,Mohit Bansal,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ImagiRep 基准（922 题、7 类空间问题、2 种设定），在相同未观测状态下对照 oracle 及模型估计的 RGB 与 6 种替代表征（深度、全景、俯视几何等），发现准确想象的 RGB 并不保证准确空间推理、不同问题偏好不同表征；据此提出轻量测试时自适应表征选择策略 GEARS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型默认用 RGB 作为空间想象接口，但这一选择更多源于其便于生成，而非对下游空间推理最优；需要隔离"表征"本身对空间想象的作用。

Method: 构建模拟器支撑的受控基准 ImagiRep：922 个问题、7 种题型、Viewpoint Imagination 与 Cross-view Integration 两个设定，为每个未观测状态提供 oracle RGB 与六种替代表征；对 oracle 状态与世界模型估计状态分别做同分布对照分析，并据此设计测试时自适应表征选择方法 GEARS。

Result: 即使 RGB 想象准确，空间推理仍可能出错；不同题型受益于不同表征（俯视几何利于全局距离比较，全景利于开阔空间上下文）；世界模型估计状态下趋势一致，单用估计 RGB 会误导推理，替代表征保留互补收益；GEARS 在测试时自适应选表征，优于现有空间想象方法。

Conclusion: 对未见状态的空间推理不能等同于生成 RGB 视图，表征选择本身是关键变量；自适应表征选择是低成本且有效的改进方向，为世界模型的空间接口设计提供依据。

Abstract: Visual world models increasingly support spatial reasoning by generating RGB views of unobserved states. However, RGB has become the default interface largely because it is well suited for visual generation, rather than necessarily being the most effective representation for downstream reasoning. Different spatial questions may benefit from different representations of the same imagined state: top-down geometry can facilitate global distance comparisons, while panoramic views provide broader context for reasoning about open space. To isolate the role of representation in spatial imagination, we introduce ImagiRep, a simulator-grounded benchmark of 922 questions spanning seven question types across two settings, Viewpoint Imagination and Cross-view Integration, providing oracle RGB and six alternative representations (e.g., depth, panoramic views, and top-down geometry) of the same unobserved state. This controlled comparison reveals that even accurate imagined RGB does not guarantee accurate spatial reasoning, and different questions benefit from different representations. Similar trends hold for representations derived from world-model-estimated states. Notably, estimated RGB alone can mislead spatial reasoning, while alternative representations retain complementary benefits. Building on these findings, we introduce GEARS, a lightweight test-time strategy for adaptive representation selection, outperforming existing spatial imagination methods and showing that reasoning about unseen states requires more than generating RGB views.

</details>


### [112] [PerceptRL: Making Visual Perception Rewardable in MLLMs from Answers to Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37471)
*Tongkun Guan,Zhibo Yang,Mingkun Yang,Haocheng Wang,Wei Shen,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptRL 通过"感知强化学习"（多智能体抽取密集caption中的已验证错误并转为规则可判的QA对）与"感知智能体强化学习"（多轮工具调用+坐标注入+接地感知过程奖励）两级强化MLLM的细粒度视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型后训练阶段，细粒度视觉感知仍是瓶颈：感知错误会限制以推理为导向的优化收益；而caption预训练的监督信号在后续阶段随数据配比和目标变化而被稀释。后训练阶段继续用caption监督的难点在于密集caption主观且信息密度高，难以设计可验证的奖励。

Method: 1) Perception RL：多智能体流水线从密集图像caption中抽取经核验的感知错误，将对应视觉事实转为固定答案标签的QA对（Chain QA连接相关错误，多选QA组合无关错误），规则奖励；设有高错误领域专用路由与领域无关通用路由。2) Perception Agentic RL：针对单轮无法解决的细粒度/密集场景，用视觉工具多轮检查；发现冷启动轨迹存在坐标漂移（错误裁剪/标注扭曲证据并传播错误），因此引入 Coordinate Injection 修正冷启动轨迹中的空间操作，并使用接地感知（grounding-aware）过程奖励监督中间空间保真度与最终答案正确性。3) 训练流程：共享冷启动SFT → 单轮Perception RL → 多轮Perception Agentic RL。

Result: 在统一工具使用协议下的七个基准上，PerceptRL 平均准确率71.6%，分别超过使用相同工具的基座模型15.7个百分点、超过所评估的最强前沿VLM 5.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 在caption监督难以直接可验证的约束下，通过"错误抽取→可验证QA+规则奖励"和"带坐标纠偏与过程奖励的多轮工具智能体RL"，可有效且可持续地在后训练阶段强化MLLM的细粒度感知能力。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception remains a bottleneck during post-training of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as perceptual errors can constrain gains from reasoning-focused optimization. Although image-caption pre-training improves perception, its influence can weaken as subsequent stages change data mixtures and objectives. Continuing caption supervision during post-training is challenging because subjective, information-dense descriptions complicate verifiable reward design. We present PerceptRL, which strengthens perception at two complementary levels. Perception RL uses a multi-agent pipeline to extract verified errors from dense image captions and convert the corresponding visual facts into QA pairs with fixed answer labels and rule-based rewards. Chain QA links correlated errors, while multiple-choice QA combines unrelated errors. A specialized route targets high-error domains with tailored verification, and a domain-agnostic general route covers errors beyond these domains. Perception Agentic RL addresses fine-grained and dense cases unresolved by single-turn Perception RL through multi-turn inspection with visual tools. We observe coordinate drift even in models trained to use tools, where incorrect crops or annotations distort evidence and propagate errors. Coordinate Injection corrects spatial operations in cold-start trajectories, while a grounding-aware process reward supervises intermediate spatial fidelity alongside final-answer correctness. Training proceeds through shared cold-start SFT, single-turn Perception RL, and multi-turn Perception Agentic RL. Across seven benchmarks under a shared tool-use protocol, PerceptRL achieves 71.6% average accuracy, surpassing the base model with the same tools and the strongest evaluated frontier VLM by 15.7 and 5.2 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [113] [How Reliable Is Individualized Functional-Network Attribution in Resting-State fMRI Cognitive Prediction in ADNI?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37472)
*Sheethal Bhat,Mahfuzur Rahman Chowdhury,Imam Ahmed Shaik,Rose Dawn Bharath,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估ADNI中基于静息态FC预测器的逐被试网络消融归因的可重复性：分半信度接近上限（ρ≈0.95），但扫描-重扫描信度明显下降（ρ≈0.61），且仍显著高于错配被试零分布，说明个体归因中"人群共享结构"占主导、个体特异成分较小但非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化归因常被当作可解释的 rs-fMRI 生物标志物，但其重测信度很少被量化，容易把会话内稳定误当作个体特异性。

Method: 在ADNI N=875 上训练冻结的FC预测器（Schaefer-200 → MMSE/ADAS-Cog13），对被试做逐网络消融归因；报告分半信度、扫描-重扫描信度，并引入被试级置换零分布（含错配被试零）作为对照。

Result: 分半 Spearman ρ=0.948/0.950 接近上限；扫描-重扫描降至 ρ=0.609/0.616；错配被试零下相关仍达 ρ≈0.46–0.47，但匹配对显著超出零分布（z≈6.6–6.9，p≤1e-4）。

Conclusion: 个体化网络归因 = 强人群共享模式 + 适度但非零的个体特异成分；建议同时报告扫描-重扫描信度与置换零分布控制，避免仅用会话内代理指标高估个体特异性。

Abstract: Individualized attribution estimates which functional networks drive a model prediction for a given subject and is often proposed as an interpretable resting-state fMRI (rs-fMRI) biomarker, but its test-retest reliability is rarely quantified. We evaluate per-subject network-ablation attribution for a frozen resting-state functional connectivity (FC) predictor (Schaefer-200 FC predicting MMSE/ADAS-Cog13) trained on N=875 subjects from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), using, and (iii) a subject-level permutation null. Split-half reliability is near-ceiling (Spearman p=0.948/0.950) but drops on scan-rescan (p=0.609/0.616). Under the mismatched-subject null, correlations remain substantial (p~0.46-0.47), indicating strong population-shared structure, yet matched pairs exceed this null (z~6.6-6.9, p<=10-4). Thus, in ADNI, individualized network attribution contains a modest but non-zero person-specific component atop a strong population-shared pattern. We recommend reporting scan-rescan reliability together with a permutation-null control, rather than within-session proxies alone, to avoid overstating individual specificity.

</details>


### [114] [ALADDIN: Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37503)
*Ivan Shchekotov,Timofei Gritsaev,Viacheslav Pirogov,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALADDIN：用预训练多步流匹配模型初始化生成器并做对抗蒸馏，得到单步人脸替换器，在身份迁移与目标属性保持间可调，效果达单步SOTA并可匹配最佳多步模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式人脸替换真实度高但需多次网络评估、推理慢；GAN 从零训练快但真实感弱。作者还指出标准评测存在偏向"与身份编码器同源训练"方法的偏差。

Method: 以预训练多步 flow-matching 模型初始化生成器，在身份与目标保持约束下进行对抗训练实现单步蒸馏（Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation）；用三元组伪标签监督训练学生模型以获得身份迁移—目标属性保持的可控权衡；并用独立身份编码器重评所有基线。

Result: 在同等身份迁移水平下，真实感与目标保持为单步方法SOTA；以28倍更少的网络评估（NFE）和约一个数量级更少的训练数据对，匹配最佳多步模型。修正评测偏差后基线的相对排序发生变化。

Conclusion: 对抗蒸馏+预训练流匹配初始化是获得高质量单步生成的有效途径；评测中应使用与训练无关的独立身份编码器以避免方法论偏差。

Abstract: Face swapping transfers a source identity onto a target image while preserving the target's pose, expression, hair, and lighting. The most realistic current methods are diffusion-based, but they need many network evaluations per image. GAN-based methods are fast, yet trained from scratch they must learn realism from the adversarial signal alone and produce visibly weaker results. We present ALADDIN (Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping), a one-step face swapper obtained by initialising the generator from a pre-trained multi-step flow-matching model and training it adversarially under identity and target-preservation constraints. We expose a controllable trade-off between identity transfer and target-attribute preservation by training a student model with triplet pseudo-label supervision. We additionally identify a bias in the standard evaluation protocol, which favours methods trained against the same identity encoder used to score them, and re-evaluate all baselines with an independent one. ALADDIN is state-of-the-art among one-step methods in realism and target preservation at comparable identity transfer, and matches the best multi-step model with 28 times fewer network evaluations and an order of magnitude fewer training pairs.

</details>


### [115] [A note on data-efficient finetuning of vision foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37510)
*Niclas Hergenröther,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统实证比较基础模型架构（Attention/CNN/Mamba）与迁移范式（Visual Prompting、Linear Probing、LoRA、全量微调）在资源受限下的高效微调表现，并探讨正则化对 VP 的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Visual Prompting 与 AutoVP 等流水线日趋成熟，Mamba 等新基础模型族涌现，但缺乏对最新模型与传统微调方法在统一条件下（含超参调优）的系统评估，需要为资源受限场景下的高效迁移提供实用指南。

Method: 构建统一实证评测框架：交叉组合三类基础模型架构（Attention-based、Convolution-based、Mamba-based）与四类迁移范式（VP、Linear Probing、FFT、LoRA），并进行超参数调优；在此基础上扩展评估模型与技术范围，并测试常用正则化技术对 VP 性能的提升效果。

Result: 给出架构与迁移范式之间交互关系的实证结论与对比性能概览，识别高效微调领域当前的进展、挑战与机会，并总结 VP 场景下正则化的实际收益。

Conclusion: 该工作为资源受限下的高效微调选择提供经验性指导，但属于基准/评估类研究，未提出新的核心方法，对具身与 3D 核心方向的方法论贡献有限。

Abstract: Adapting foundation models to a new task within or beyond the training domain is the de facto standard for building specialized machine learning models. Recently, Visual Prompting (VP) has emerged as a promising technique for efficient knowledge transfer. As new foundation model families~(like Mamba) get introduced and VP pipelines such as AutoVP reach greater maturity, we find a growing need for a systematic evaluation of current approaches. In this work, we assess the performance of the latest models, comparing them to earlier architectures and alternative fine-tuning methods, to better understand the progress, challenges and opportunities in the field of efficient fine-tuning under resource limitations. Towards this goal, this paper provides a concise empirical overview of the interactions among foundation model families (Attention-, Convolution-, and Mamba-based) and transfer paradigms: VP, Linear Probing (LP), Full Fine-tuning (FFT) and Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Our work builds up on previous findings by broadening the selection of evaluated models and techniques while tuning hyperparameters. In the interest of delivering practical guidelines for the user, we also explore application of prevalent regularization techniques to boost performance in the context of VP.

</details>


### [116] [GroundControl: Satellite-Conditioned Multi-View Diffusion for Reconstruction-Free Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37545)
*Akshay Krishnan,Matias Turkulainen,Jamie Wynn,Zawar Qureshi,Mohamed Sayed,Guillermo Garcia-Hernando,Sara Vicente,James Hays,Michael Firman,Daniyar Turmukhambetov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用卫星图像作为额外条件，通过cross-attention将卫星图与街景输入图联合注入预训练多视角扩散模型，实现户外稀疏输入下的外推式新视角合成，无需三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角/视频扩散模型在稀疏图像新视角合成上质量高，但在输入视角之外（场景外推）易产生不真实的空间布局幻觉。户外场景中缺少全局布局约束是主因，作者提出用卫星图像提供俯视几何先验来缓解。

Method: 提出卫星条件化的多视角扩散模型：以卫星图像和带位姿的街景图像为条件，通过cross-attention引入卫星条件，保留预训练多视角扩散先验，从而在少量源视角下生成既连贯真实又符合卫星布局的新视角。不使用显式3D重建，仅依赖带位姿的街景与卫星视图。

Result: 在户外数据集上，相较仅卫星条件或仅街景条件的基线，新视角合成效果更优，尤其在目标视角与源视角重叠极少的困难情形下提升明显。

Conclusion: 卫星先验可作为轻量全局布局条件，与街景多视角扩散先验互补，有效抑制外推幻觉；方法免显式重建，泛化于稀疏输入的户外新视角合成，可作为场景外推与生成式重建的条件化范式参考。

Abstract: Multi-view or video diffusion models have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for novel-view synthesis from sparse image collections. While they can produce high quality images, they tend to hallucinate unrealistic spatial layouts when synthesizing views outside the input views (scene extrapolation). We propose to address this challenge for outdoor scenes by using satellite images as an additional information source to condition the multi-view generative model. We introduce a new satellite-conditioned multi-view diffusion model that combines conditioning from a satellite image and street-level input images when generating novel-views. Our cross-attention based conditioning allows us to retain pretrained multi-view diffusion priors to generate seamless, realistic, novel views from a handful of source views, while also adhering to the scene layout as seen from satellite images. Our model does not use 3D scene reconstruction, and only relies on posed street and satellite views. Our experiments on outdoor datasets show that our model is better than both satellite-conditioned or street-view conditioned baselines on novel-view synthesis, especially on challenging cases where target views have very little overlap with source views. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [117] [ReferStream: Interactive Subject Injection for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37566)
*Shuolin Xu,Guanyuan Li,Boyu Li,Kelu Yao,Xiaogang Xu,Hongchuan Yu,Shuicheng Yan,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReferStream：免训练流式视频生成中自然注入新主体的框架，结合 Joint Latent 场景内参考构造与 Cache Restoration 瞬态缓存注入，并发布 ReferStream Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成中需要在新主体出现时注入其视觉参考，同时保持已有主体与场景一致。独立编码参考（脱离当前场景）缺乏集成引导；将参考特征长期驻留内存又会干扰已建立的上下文，二者存在权衡。

Method: 无需额外训练，两阶段：(1) Joint Latent：将参考 latent 放置在最新场景 latent 的目标区域，使参考特征在上下文中被提取；(2) Cache Restoration：用提取特征条件化注入 chunk，随后恢复被覆盖的全局缓存条目，同时保留新生成历史。不重训生成器、不回改已输出帧。同时构建 ReferStream Bench：按日程到达的参考、演化指令、显式主体存在标注，覆盖 InShot 与 MultiShot 设定。

Result: 在 ReferStream Bench 上，文本遵循度、主体相似度与计数准确率均优于基线，包括预先给定全部参考的离线方法。

Conclusion: 通过"场景内参考构造 + 瞬态缓存注入"可在不重训的前提下实现流式视频中新主体的自然融入，兼顾新主体保真与既有主体保持；配套基准为该任务提供可评测协议。

Abstract: Injecting new subjects naturally during streaming video generation requires integrating newly supplied visual references into the existing video while preserving established subjects and the scene. Encoding references independently of the current scene provides little guidance for this integration, while keeping their features in persistent memory can interfere with established context. We propose ReferStream, a framework that requires no additional training and combines reference construction using the current scene with transient cache injection. Joint Latent places reference latents in target regions of the latest scene latent, allowing reference features to be extracted in context. Cache Restoration uses these features to condition the injection chunk, then restores the overwritten global cache entries while retaining the newly generated history. This procedure incorporates new visual conditions without retraining the generator or revising emitted frames. We further introduce ReferStream Bench, a new benchmark with scheduled reference arrivals, evolving instructions, and explicit annotations of subject presence for InShot and MultiShot generation. Evaluation follows the event schedule and measures both the fidelity of new subjects and the preservation of established subjects, alongside video quality, text adherence, and count accuracy. Experiments on ReferStream Bench show higher scores for text adherence, subject similarity, and count accuracy than the compared baselines, including offline methods given all references in advance. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [118] [The Noise Plane: A Diffusion Bridge Across Exponent and Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37576)
*Xuran Hu,Yuru Jia,Tengxi Wang,Miloš Daković,Ljubiša Stanković,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 p-Bridge：以 Tweedie 噪声族为对象的统一、由测量量索引的扩散框架，用指数 p 描述方差-强度关系、累积测量 ν 控制噪声水平，构成含 Gaussian/Poisson/Gamma 的噪声平面，并以共享反向均值更新与 deviance 目标实现从观测测量水平向指定更高水平的还原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散去噪几乎都假设高斯扰动，而传感器噪声（荧光显微、SAR 等）呈现信号依赖的非高斯统计特性，用固定高斯先验建模存在模型失配；同时不同噪声模型通常需分别训练，缺乏统一、可解释的去噪框架。

Method: 1) 以 Tweedie 族作为统一噪声模型，用指数 p（方差-强度关系）与累积测量 ν（噪声水平）张成"噪声平面"；2) 用独立增量构造随机 bridge，使去噪等价于渐进测量累积，可同时指定起始与终止索引；3) 测量叠加导出共享的反向均值更新，配合基于 deviance 的目标学习所需条件均值；4) 潜变量复合 Poisson–Gamma 构造支持中间 p 值采样；5) 单一以 p 为条件的网络适配多种 Tweedie 噪声，无需逐 p 单独训练。

Result: 指数条件化相比固定指数模型提升跨指数鲁棒性；在指数经估计（非真实值）条件下，单一共享网络达到与 oracle 挑选的 5 个专家模型相当的鲁棒性；在合成图像、FMD 荧光显微图像与多传感器 SAR 图像上验证了在不同信号依赖噪声机制下的有效性。

Conclusion: p-Bridge 将信号依赖噪声去噪统一为测量索引的参数化扩散桥过程，兼顾理论一致性与工程可用性（单模型多噪声机制），为真实传感成像中的去噪与后续视觉任务提供了通用替代高斯特化的方案；但其验证集中于图像恢复层面，未涉及下游几何/机器人任务。

Abstract: Diffusion-based denoising commonly uses Gaussian perturbations, whereas sensor noise follows diverse signal-dependent statistical laws. In this paper, we propose $p$-Bridge, a unified measurement-indexed diffusion framework for Tweedie noise. The noise exponent $p$ describes the variance--intensity relation, while accumulated measurement $\nu$ controls the noise level. Together, they define a noise plane that includes Gaussian, Poisson, and Gamma models. The stochastic bridge connects measurement distributions through independent increments, giving denoising a physical interpretation as progressive measurement accumulation. Both the starting and stopping indices can be specified, allowing restoration from an observed measurement level to a prescribed higher level. Measurement superposition yields a shared reverse-mean update, paired with a deviance-based objective for learning the required conditional mean. A latent compound Poisson--Gamma construction enables sampling at intermediate exponents. A single exponent-conditioned network adapts to different Tweedie noise models without separate training for each exponent. Experiments show that exponent conditioning improves cross-exponent robustness over fixed-exponent models. Using estimated exponents, the shared network achieves robustness comparable to an oracle-selected set of five specialist models. Experiments on synthetic images, FMD fluorescence microscopy, and multi-sensor SAR images further demonstrate its effectiveness across different signal-dependent noise regimes.

</details>


### [119] [As the Story Unfolds: Watching a Film and Identifying Characters as a Human Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37579)
*Zhongrui Gui,Junyu Xie,Tengda Han,Weidi Xie,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"角色命名"任务：仅用影片自身的对话与视频，判断对话中出现的名字指向哪个可见角色，并构建渐进式角色库以支持在线人物理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影角色是随剧情逐步引入的，现有角色识别方法依赖外部演职员表、演员照片或预建角色库，无法实现仅从影片本身推断身份的类人理解。

Method: 构建"角色命名"任务与数据集及标注（含首次出场）；设计视频-对话联合模型，利用正反打结构、自我介绍、直呼、第三方指称、视线与时间连续性等电影语言线索；提出随影片推进逐步建立角色库的渐进式框架；并与商用多模态大模型对比。

Result: 提供了角色命名与首次出场评测的数据集和标注，所提模型与商用多模态大模型进行了对比（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 该工作支持仅凭影片内部信息在线推断角色身份的类人电影理解，减少对外部元数据的依赖。

Abstract: Films introduce characters incrementally as they progress. We study the problem of identifying principal characters and assigning their names directly from the movie itself, without using external cast lists, actor photographs, or pre-built character banks. We introduce the character naming task: given a name mentioned in dialogue and the associated video context, the model must determine which visible character bears that name. This task is challenging because name resolution often depends on cinematic cues such as shot-reverse-shot structure, self-introduction, direct address, third-party reference, gaze, and temporal continuity. We provide a dataset and annotations for evaluating character naming and first character appearances, develop a video-and-dialogue model for the task, and compare it with proprietary multimodal large language models. We further introduce a progressive framework for building a character bank as the film unfolds. Together, these contributions support online, human-like movie understanding in which character identities are inferred from the film itself.

</details>


### [120] [Zero-Shot Multimodal Event Spotting in Soccer Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37583)
*Sadia Kamal,Mahmudul Hasan,Tony Braskich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 零样本足球事件检测框架：将整场比赛切为重叠片段，用紧凑视觉故事板+对齐解说文本联合输入通用多模态模型，完成事件检测、分类与时序定位，无需任务特定训练；130个事件上F1@5s达72.0%，跨数据集优于CALF与NetVLAD++，推理成本降72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球事件检测方法依赖大规模标注数据和足球领域专用训练，迁移性差、成本高；作者希望用通用多模态大模型的零样本能力替代领域定制模型。

Method: 将比赛切成重叠片段，每段用紧凑"视觉故事板"（少量代表帧拼图）表示并对齐解说转录文本，联合输入通用多模态模型进行事件检测、分类与时间定位；系统分析模型族、输入表示与提示优化的影响；并加入基于证据的兴奋度打分阶段，用自然语言解释对事件排序以支持集锦选取。

Result: 在3场人工标注比赛、共130个事件上，最佳配置取得72.0% F1@5s；跨数据集设置下优于SoccerNet训练的CALF与NetVLAD++；故事板表示相比逐帧输入降低72%推理开销。

Conclusion: 视觉故事板加解说文本的联合输入可让通用多模态模型零样本完成体育事件定位，兼顾精度与效率，并可通过证据化打分支持可解释的集锦生成。

Abstract: Automatic soccer event spotting supports highlight generation, video indexing, and sports analytics, but existing methods depend on large annotated datasets and soccer-specific training. We present a zero-shot framework that detects, classifies, and temporally localizes events in full-length soccer broadcasts without task-specific parameter training. Each match is segmented into overlapping clips represented by a compact visual storyboard and the aligned commentary transcript, which a general-purpose multimodal model processes jointly. On three manually annotated matches containing 130 events, the best configuration achieves 72.0% F1@5s and outperforms SoccerNet-trained CALF and NetVLAD++ in a cross-dataset setting, while the storyboard representation reduces inference cost by 72% compared with individual frames. We further analyze the effects of model family, input representation, and prompt optimization, and introduce an evidence-grounded excitement-scoring stage that ranks detected events with natural-language explanations for highlight selection.

</details>


### [121] [MoI-Bench: A Benchmark for Image Generation and Editing on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37599)
*Kailai Feng,Haoxiang Rao,Yuxiang Wei,Bo Chen,Junsong Li,Yang Pan,Chengkun Cai,Jagmohan Chauhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoI-Bench：面向移动端图像编辑的基准，用16种操作×8类视觉目标解耦定义64个子任务1150样本；配套MoI-Eval给出指令遵循、视觉质量、细节保持等任务相关指标，并构造样本级问答聚合为能力画像与Quality-Deployability二维部署能力图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以为“端上模型与服务器模型之间的能力差距”提供细粒度指导，也缺乏结合用户真实需求与设备资源约束的评测方式，导致紧凑模型优化目标模糊（只能给出“提升文本编辑”这类宽泛应用级目标）。

Method: 构建基于移动用户需求的MoI-Bench：64子任务、1150样本；定义16种操作与8类视觉目标作为两条正交轴，组合确定具体任务（如去妆容）；提出MoI-Eval，采用覆盖指令遵循、视觉质量、细节保持的任务特定准则，生成样本级评估问题并沿操作/目标/准则三轴聚合为整体质量与能力画像；同时评估代表性端上/服务器模型并测量移动端部署开销，构造质量-可部署性二维图。

Result: 解耦的Operation×Target设计提供更细粒度改进指引，可定位具体缺口（如text-replacement）而非宽泛应用目标；端上模型已能实现高效生成/编辑，瓶颈在任务覆盖度与编辑质量（尤其身份保持与文本渲染），扩展覆盖比继续提升部署效率更值得关注。

Conclusion: 该工作以正交任务分解+多维准则评测替代单一分数排名，将质量与部署成本联合映射，指出紧凑模型后续方向：扩大任务覆盖、提升身份保持与文本渲染质量。

Abstract: Developing on-device image models requires considering practical user needs, task types and device resource constraints. However, existing benchmarks provide limited guidance on identifying capability gaps between on-device models and server-side models. We introduce MoI-Bench, a benchmark grounded in mobile user needs, comprising 64 subtasks and 1150 samples. It defines 16 operations, such as removal and replacement, and eight visual target categories covering content such as portraits and text. These form two orthogonal axes whose applicable combinations define specific tasks, such as removing facial makeup. To better guide model improvement, we further propose MoI-Eval, which introduces task-specific criteria covering instruction following, visual quality and detail preservation. We further construct sample-level evaluation questions and aggregate their scores into overall quality and capability profiles along the operation, target and criteria axes. We evaluate representative on-device and server-side models and measure mobile deployment costs. Results show that this decoupled Operation x Target design provides finer-grained guidance for improving compact models. For example, it highlights the need to prioritize specific capability gaps (e.g., text-replacement) over broad application-level goals (e.g., improving text editing). Unlike conventional benchmarks that rank models using a single score, we introduce a two-dimensional Quality-Deployability capability map to assess models for practical mobile use. The map shows that current on-device models can achieve efficient generation or editing but expanding their task coverage deserves greater attention than further improving deployment efficiency. Further progress requires improving editing quality, particularly identity preservation and text rendering, while broadening task coverage.

</details>


### [122] [VideoLVR: Latent Visual Reasoning over Grounded Video Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37613)
*Shilong Li,Xingyuan Bu,Hao Shi,Yun Xing,Weixin Feng,Qiangpeng Yang,Shilei Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoLVR 在视频 MLLM 推理过程中用潜在视觉 token 表示关键时刻的局部证据，并以"视觉 token 预测"目标与文本生成统一在同一框架下，支持交错文本-潜在视觉状态的CoT推理，并可扩展到强化学习联合优化，从而缓解视觉遗忘且不显著扩张多模态上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言的CoT推理轨迹缺少可靠的视觉回溯机制，导致"视觉遗忘"；现有方案或用文本坐标（仅符号位置、无视觉内容），或把裁剪区域/帧重新插入上下文（保真但上下文膨胀）。

Method: 从相关视频时刻的局部区域构建"潜在视觉证据"，注入中间推理步骤；设计视觉 token 预测目标，把视觉 token 重建转换为在由输入视频导出的词表上的预测，从而统一视觉证据重建与文本生成；进一步将该过程扩展到强化学习，把采样得到的视觉 token 与文本 token 一同视为动作，用最终答案奖励联合优化策略。

Result: 多个视频理解基准上验证有效，尤其在需要细粒度视觉证据的任务上增益明显，且多模态上下文扩张很小。

Conclusion: 以潜在视觉 token 替代坐标引用或上下文插入，是兼顾视觉回溯保真度与上下文效率的可行路线；与 RL 结合可端到端优化"何时看什么"的证据检索策略。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning has substantially advanced video understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, language-based reasoning traces do not provide a reliable mechanism for models to revisit relevant visual evidence, leading to visual forgetting and impairing their ability to reason over video content. Existing approaches maintain visual grounding during reasoning in two main ways: they either refer to evidence using textual coordinates, which specify symbolic locations rather than the visual content itself, or reinsert cropped regions or frames into the multimodal context, preserving visual details at the cost of substantial context expansion. To address these limitations, we introduce VideoLVR, which constructs latent visual evidence from local regions at relevant video moments, thereby providing visual support for intermediate reasoning steps. To supervise latent visual evidence generation, we introduce a visual token prediction objective that formulates visual token reconstruction as prediction over a vocabulary derived from the input video, thereby unifying visual evidence reconstruction and text generation within the same token prediction framework. This shared prediction framework enables MLLMs to reason over interleaved text tokens and latent visual states while integrating evidence from different moments. We further extend VideoLVR to reinforcement learning by treating sampled visual tokens as actions alongside text tokens and jointly optimizing the policy using final-answer rewards. By efficiently revisiting task-relevant visual information during reasoning, VideoLVR improves video understanding without substantially expanding the multimodal context. Experiments on multiple video benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness, especially on tasks requiring fine-grained visual evidence.

</details>


### [123] [Perceiving and Predicting Dynamic Sports: Spatiotemporal Intelligence in Vision Models for Egocentric Tennis Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37619)
*Tudor Braicu,Joseph DelPreto,Jared Boyer,Mónika Farsang,Maximilian Stölzle,Tim Niklas Seyde,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作构建了同步5路第三人称视角的自中心网球视频数据集，系统评估图像编码器、视频编码器与VLM在7项时空预测任务（定位、轨迹预测、正反手预判、接触时间等），并提出条件扩散模型从视觉预测实时生成全身运动学网球动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基础视觉模型在以移动自中心相机为视角时，能否支撑机器人所需的时空智能（场景理解、交互推理、未来事件预测）；现有基准缺乏此类系统评估，且度量深度与长时运动表征的局限尚不清楚。

Method: 采集带标注的自中心网球数据集：自中心视频与5路非自中心视频同步，含全局位置估计、接触时刻与击球类型标注。在自中心图像上定义7个预测任务，对比图像编码器、视频编码器与视觉语言模型并接联合训练的预测头；分析辅助深度信息与图像历史长度的影响；最后用条件扩散模型将视觉编码器预测映射为全身运动学动作，支持随预测更新而在线调整。

Result: 最佳模型当前帧定位误差：自身球员0.14 m、球0.54 m；1.1 s内未来轨迹误差0.34 m与0.91 m；接触时间预测平均误差0.14 s。辅助深度显著提升3D球定位，说明度量深度仍是难点；更长的图像历史未改善球轨迹预测，表明表征依赖场景上下文而非纯帧间运动。

Conclusion: 为理解基础视觉模型如何以自中心视频支持实时动态机器人提供了实证依据：定位与短时预测精度较高但度量深度与长时运动建模仍受限，并展示了视觉预测驱动的实时运动生成可行性。

Abstract: Agile robots require spatiotemporal intelligence to interpret scenes, reason about interactions, and anticipate future events. Yet it remains unclear how well foundation vision models support these capabilities, particularly from a moving egocentric camera, which is a natural view for robots. To investigate this, we collect an annotated tennis dataset and train a suite of vision models on spatiotemporal tasks. Our dataset contains egocentric video synchronized with five allocentric videos, estimated global positions, labeled contact times, and annotated stroke types. We then evaluate seven prediction tasks from egocentric images: 2D ego player and 3D ball localization, future player and ball trajectories, forehand or backhand stroke anticipation, time to the next ego player ball contact, and 3D ball position at that contact. We compare image encoders, video encoders, and vision-language models with jointly trained prediction heads. The best performing models achieve mean current-frame localization errors of 0.14 m for the ego player and 0.54 m for the ball, future forecasting errors of 0.34 m for the ego player and 0.91 m for the ball over 1.1 s, and a mean contact-time prediction error of 0.14 s. Auxiliary depth information substantially improves 3D ball localization, suggesting that inferring metric depth remains a challenge. Longer image histories do not improve ball forecasting, indicating that representations exploit scene context rather than rely solely on inter-frame motion. Finally, we propose a conditional diffusion model to generate whole-body kinematic tennis motion from vision encoder predictions on egocentric video at real-time rates, adapting movements as predictions are refined. These results move towards a deeper understanding of how foundation vision models can support real-time dynamic robots using egocentric video.

</details>


### [124] [Know, then Draw: A New Learning Paradigm for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37626)
*Yucheng Mao,Chongjian Ge,Zhuowen Tu,Hao Tan,Hanwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"自回归预训练获取语义知识 + 扩散后训练负责高保真渲染"的视觉生成范式，系统研究学习目标、表示与缩放规律，构建纯语义 tokenizer（无解码器、无重建损失），推导计算最优缩放律，并用6,600 H100小时训练3B文生图模型，GenEval 86、DPG-Bench 87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成同时需要高层语义知识与细粒度像素细节，单一建模方式难以兼顾；希望将"知识获取"与"渲染"解耦，分别交给最合适的训练目标，并给出可外推的计算最优缩放指导。

Method: 在统一框架下做受控对比：(1) 比较自回归 vs 扩散作为预训练目标对下游渲染的影响；(2) 通过仅AR生成的语义质量与最终性能相关性分析，论证语义质量才是关键；(3) 提出仅用语言监督训练的纯语义 tokenizer（无图像解码器、无重建损失）；(4) 分别拟合知识获取与渲染两个阶段的计算最优缩放律。

Result: 受控实验显示 AR 预训练在学习效率与最终生成质量上均优于扩散预训练；AR-only 质量与最终性能不必然相关，语义质量更具决定性；AR 预训练使拟合的渲染损失渐近值低于从零训练的像素扩散模型；依据缩放律以 6,600 H100 GPU 小时训练 3B 文生图模型，GenEval 86 / DPG-Bench 87。

Conclusion: "AR 预训练 + 扩散后训练"的解耦范式在缩放层面得到验证，纯语义 tokenizer 可行，且计算效率显著优于同等规模的从零扩散训练，为高效高保真生成提供了可复用的训练配方与缩放律。

Abstract: Visual generation requires both high-level semantic knowledge and fine-grained pixel details. We propose a learning paradigm that assigns these roles to autoregressive pretraining for knowledge acquisition and diffusion post-training for high-fidelity rendering. Within this framework, we study the objective, representation, and scaling of knowledge acquisition and rendering. Controlled comparisons identify autoregression as the stronger pretraining objective for downstream rendering, improving both learning efficiency and final generation quality. We further find that AR-only generation quality does not necessarily correlate with final performance; semantic quality matters more. This motivates a purely semantic tokenizer trained with language supervision alone, without an image decoder or reconstruction loss. We establish compute-optimal scaling laws for knowledge acquisition and rendering, and find that autoregressive pretraining lowers the fitted asymptotic rendering loss compared with pixel diffusion models trained from scratch. Guided by the laws, we train a 3B-parameter text-to-image model with only 6,600 H100 GPU-hours, achieving 86 on GenEval and 87 on DPG-Bench and validating the proposed paradigm at scale.

</details>


### [125] [Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Cross-View Temporal Object Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37633)
*Siyoon Jin,Dahyun Chung,Honggyu An,Sangbeom Lim,Wonjin Nam,Jaewoo Jung,WonJun Moon,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XTrack: 将预训练视频 DiT 改造为跨视角-跨时间目标对应框架，通过 RGB-to-mask latent transport 统一建模跨视角匹配与时序跟踪，并用 focal cross-view attention alignment、hard negative conditioning、local context crop 及伪标签流水线（Ego-Exo4D 稀疏标注→稠密监督）提升接地可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角目标对应方法依赖逐帧匹配或序列跟踪，在外观变化与遮挡下会传播对应误差；同时缺乏同时保持跨视角与跨时间目标身份的公式化定义及稠密标注数据。

Method: 提出 cross-view temporal object correspondence 任务；XTrack 复用预训练视频 DiT 的语义与时空先验做 RGB 到 mask 的 latent transport，联合跨视角匹配与时序跟踪；引入 focal cross-view attention 对齐、hard negative conditioning、局部上下文裁剪以抑制语义相似但错误的跨视角注意；构建伪标签整理流程将 Ego-Exo4D 稀疏标注转为稠密视频级监督。

Result: 实验表明 XTrack 作为统一框架有效，在时序一致性与身份保持上表现稳健（摘要未给出具体数值指标）。

Conclusion: 以视频生成先验替代逐帧匹配/跟踪的范式可行，跨视角接地需专门注意力与负样本设计保障；稠密伪标签可支撑视频级训练。

Abstract: Cross-view object correspondence identifies the same object across views. However, existing methods often rely on frame-wise matching or sequential tracking, which can propagate correspondence errors under appearance changes or occlusions. Therefore, we introduce cross-view temporal object correspondence, which requires preserving object identity across both views and time from a query mask. To address this, we propose XTrack, a unified framework which repurposes a pretrained video DiT with semantic and spatiotemporal priors for RGB-to-mask latent transport, jointly modeling cross-view matching and temporal tracking. However, naive video DiTs do not guarantee accurate cross-view grounding and often attend to semantically similar yet incorrect objects across views. Thus, we introduce focal cross-view attention alignment, hard negative conditioning, and local context crop strategies to ensure reliable grounding. Finally, beyond these architectural enhancements, we also tackle the lack of dense annotation by introducing a pseudo-label curation pipeline that converts sparse Ego-Exo4D annotations into dense supervision to enable effective video-level training. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of XTrack as a unified framework, ensuring robust temporal consistency and identity preservation.

</details>


### [126] [Can Discrete Tokens Serve as Semantically Lossless Representations of Visual Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37662)
*Jialv Zou,Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Xu Huang,Yaxin Luo,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑"量化误差导致离散视觉token语义损失"的既有假设，指出有效比特预算才是下游性能的关键；提出因子化向量量化（FVQ），用对称并行码本量化互补的固定维视图，在更大比特预算下持续提升，最终在多模态理解上与连续表示持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散视觉token可提供与语言统一的下一个token预测接口，但普遍认为量化误差造成不可避免的语义损失，使连续表示成为内在上界。作者希望重新检验这一假设，并突破现有量化器的语义饱和瓶颈。

Method: 以多模态理解为语义保真度代理，做量化误差与理解性能的相关性分析；提出Factorized Vector Quantization：将视觉特征分解为互补的固定维视图，用对称并行码本分别量化，扩展有效比特预算；在工业级多模态数据上训练。

Result: 量化误差不可靠（误差更大者理解更好），有效比特预算更关键；FVQ在大比特预算下继续提升，达到与连续表示相当的理解性能；九指标平均上超过UniAR 22.8%、Emu3-Chat 59.2%，为SOTA；首次在含细粒度OCR任务上与Qwen3-VL等强连续表示模型具竞争力。

Conclusion: 量化误差并非语义保真度的决定因素，有效比特预算是核心；通过因子化并行码本可缓解码本饱和，使离散表示具备替代连续表示的基础，从而支撑大规模原生多模态基础模型。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens represent vision in a discrete form analogous to language, offering a native interface for multimodal modeling under a shared next-token prediction objective. However, quantization error is widely assumed to cause unavoidable semantic loss, fundamentally limiting the semantic fidelity of discrete visual tokens and positioning continuous visual representations as an intrinsic upper bound. Using multimodal understanding as a proxy for semantic fidelity, we revisit this assumption and find quantization error unreliable, as discrete representations with substantially larger errors can achieve better understanding. Instead, the effective bit budget of discrete indices emerges as a key determinant of downstream performance. However, existing quantizers eventually saturate as additional codebooks contribute progressively less semantic information. As one approach to alleviating this saturation, we introduce Factorized Vector Quantization, which quantizes complementary, fixed-dimensional views of the visual feature with symmetric parallel codebooks. It continues to improve at larger bit budgets, ultimately matching continuous visual representations in multimodal understanding. Trained on industrial-scale multimodal data, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance among discrete-representation models, outperforming UniAR and Emu3-Chat by 22.8% and 59.2%, respectively, on a nine-metric average. To our knowledge, it is also the first discrete-representation model competitive with strong continuous-representation models such as Qwen3-VL, including on fine-grained OCR tasks. We further identify the key principles and a practical recipe enabling this competitiveness. These results and insights support the potential of discrete visual representations to underpin large-scale native multimodal foundation models. Code and models are available at this https URL

</details>


### [127] [Honeycomb-Surfels: Compact Hexagonal Primitives for Efficient Scene Rendering and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37668)
*Prakhar Kaushik,Soumava Paul,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hexels 提出平面六边形面元（planar hexagonal surface elements）配合一阶空间颜色场，用更少、更大的基元高效表示 3D 场景与 2D 图像，在更低显存下显著提升新视角合成与表面重建质量，并推进质量-效率 Pareto 前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场与 splatting 方法在内存与计算上开销大，难以在资源受限环境部署；2D 高斯基元的方形支撑与单一常数颜色难以在大尺度基元下保持外观精度。作者希望用更少的基元、更优的支撑域与更强的表达力来突破质量-效率权衡。

Method: 以平面六边形面元替代 2D 高斯圆盘，六边形提供面积/边界更高效的覆盖域；每个基元附加一阶（线性）空间颜色场，以捕捉基元内部平滑变化的外观；整体构成可优化的表面对齐表示，可直接用于 3D 场景表示、表面重建及 2D 图像表示。

Result: 34 个场景上，相比等显存的 2DGS，PSNR 提升 4.53–7.38 dB 且训练显存更低；单基元对比中最高提升 4.74 dB，训练显存更低、FPS 快 2.31 倍；15 个 DTU 扫描上误差降低 28.9%；6 个 Tanks and Temples 表面场景上平均 F1 最高（34.38% vs 2DGS 31.72%）；在 2D 图像表示上优于 Image-GS；在非受限设置下提供互补的质量-显存折中。

Conclusion: 六边形面元 + 局部颜色场的组合在表面对齐基元范畴内推进了质量-效率 Pareto 前沿，具备作为通用基元（3D 场景、表面重建、2D 图像）的潜力，适合资源受限场景下的高质量重建与合成。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction have advanced rapidly with radiance fields and splatting, yet their memory and computational demands limit their use in resource-constrained settings. We introduce Hexels: novel planar hexagonal surface elements with a first-order spatial color field for efficient 3D scene and 2D image representation. Hexels exploit locally planar regions with smoothly varying appearance using fewer, larger primitives. Their hexagonal support provides an area- and boundary-efficient domain, while the local color field captures appearance variation within each primitive. Across 34 scenes, Hexels improve PSNR over memory-matched 2DGS by 4.53-7.38 dB across datasets while requiring lower training VRAM. We further ablate Hexels by showing that per primitive, Hexels outperform 2DGS by upto 4.74 dB while using lower training VRAM and having 2.31 times faster FPS. Even in unconstrained settings, Hexels offers complementary quality-memory trade-offs. For efficient 3D surface reconstruction, Hexels reduce error by 28.9% compared with memory-matched 2DGS across 15 DTU scans. On 6 Tanks and Temples surface scenes, Hexels achieves the highest mean F1 among compared methods (34.38%, versus 31.72% for 2DGS). We further also show that Hexels, as a general primitive, improves 2D image representation over Image-GS. Hexels push the Pareto frontier on the quality-efficiency trade-off of surface-aligned primitives.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [OmniBrain-0: A Unified Agentic Model for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36013)
*Xiaomi Embodied Intelligence Team,Yuechen Luo,Chong He,Fang Li,Shaoqing Xu,Jingkai Wang,Menglun Su,Jiaxin Liu,Shengyin Jiang,Zhiyi Lai,Long Chen,Longlong Wang,Liang Hu,Haochen Liu,Yuncheng Jiang,Hongwei Xie,Jieming Cui,Hanbing Li,Junjie Huang,Zhixiang Duan,Hexin Zhang,Weixiang Liang,Ying Gong,Yuhan Wang,Yong Pan,Pengna Li,Kangyi Wu,Ken Deng,Kun Ma,Fuxi Wen,Zhi-xin Yang,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OmniBrain-0：轻量统一具身智能体，用共享高层决策模型协调操作与导航，结合三阶段渐进训练与多教师on-policy蒸馏(MOPD)、以及Rubric引导递归自我改进(RSI)数据飞轮，实现跨域任务可靠性与低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用策略缺乏任务级可靠性（长程协调、进度跟踪、失败恢复），而强大多模态智能体依赖昂贵推理与外部经验，紧凑模型难以复现。目标是让小模型获得统一的高层决策能力。

Method: 渐进训练框架：(1) 具身预训练；(2) 领域专家智能体蒸馏（操作/导航）；(3) 多教师on-policy蒸馏MOPD将专家决策能力整合进单一OmniBrain-0，使统一发生在决策模型内部而非仅工具接口层。另提出RSI：Meta-Agent将执行轨迹反馈转化为细化rubric、步级提醒与已验证轨迹，用于专家更新后经MOPD再整合。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-Plus、RoboCasa365、RoboTwin 2.0、R2R-CE Val-Unseen及多项真实世界操作任务上评测，跨域任务性能强，且推理与执行开销显著低于其闭源教师模型。

Conclusion: 验证了将强教师经验高效蒸馏并整合进紧凑统一具身智能体的可行性；但摘要未给出具体数值或真实机器人任务规模细节，证据强度受限于缺乏量化对比。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied intelligence requires robots to coordinate diverse skills over long horizons, track task progress, and recover from failures. Specialist policies alone do not ensure task-level reliability, while powerful multimodal agents rely on costly reasoning and external experience that compact models struggle to reproduce. We introduce OmniBrain-0, a lightweight unified embodied agent that coordinates manipulation and navigation through a shared high-level decision model, integrating task understanding, planning, progress tracking, and feedback-driven recovery. Our progressive training framework combines embodied pretraining, domain-specialized agent distillation, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). It distills powerful teachers' interaction experience into manipulation and navigation experts, then consolidates their decision capabilities into OmniBrain-0. Unification therefore occurs within the learned decision model rather than only at the tool interface. We further introduce Rubric-Guided Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI), a data flywheel in which a Meta-Agent turns execution evidence into refined rubrics, step-level reminders, and verified trajectories for domain-expert updates before their reconsolidation into OmniBrain-0 through MOPD. We evaluate OmniBrain-0 on LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-Plus, RoboCasa365, RoboTwin 2.0, and R2R-CE Val-Unseen, as well as multiple real-world manipulation tasks. OmniBrain-0 combines strong cross-domain task performance with substantially lower inference and execution overhead than its closed-source teacher, demonstrating efficient transfer and consolidation of embodied capabilities into a compact unified agent.

</details>


### [129] [DocSkill: Skill-Integrated Agentic Reasoning for Multilingual Multi-Hop Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36759)
*Jing Huang,Zhiya Tan,Changtao Miao,Shutao Gong,Yang Wang,Joey Tianyi Zhou,Jianshu Li,Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocSkill：面向多语言多跳文档QA的技能编排智能体框架，用有界MCTS离线探索技能轨迹生成SFT示范与DPO偏好对，并构建MMHop-DocBench（60K QA对/10语言/8类文档域）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言多跳文档问答需跨复杂版式、跨页、跨语言定位并整合证据；现有单次处理与自适应工具调用方法难以学习协调证据获取、跨页整合与答案验证的过程。

Method: 提出DocSkill智能体框架，学习技能编排策略，依据演化中的证据状态选择、回访或跳过技能；用有界MCTS离线探索备选技能轨迹，产出SFT示范与DPO偏好对；同时用HopGen流水线构建层级页面-元素图，导出可追溯跨页证据路径并生成带页级/元素级证据标注的QA数据。

Result: DocSkill+Qwen3-VL-8B在MMHop-DocBench上达41.0准确率、49.2 F1，比最强基线分别高5.5与6.3点；在MMLongBench-Doc与DocBench上亦具竞争力。

Conclusion: 将技能编排显式建模为可学习策略并结合搜索式轨迹监督，能有效提升跨页跨语言证据获取与推理；配套基准与可追溯证据标注支持后续研究。

Abstract: Multilingual multi-hop document question answering (MMHop-DocQA) requires locating and integrating evidence across complex layouts, document pages, and diverse languages. Existing approaches include single-pass processing and adaptive tool use, but learning to coordinate evidence acquisition, cross-page integration, and answer verification remains challenging. To address this challenge, we present DocSkill, an agentic framework with a learned skill-orchestration policy for adaptive evidence acquisition and reasoning. The policy selects, revisits, or skips skills according to the evolving evidence state. To construct supervision for skill orchestration, bounded Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) explores alternative skill trajectories offline. These trajectories provide demonstrations for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and preference pairs for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). We further introduce MMHop-DocBench, a multilingual multi-hop document QA dataset with training and test splits, containing 60K QA pairs across 10 languages and eight document domains. Its HopGen pipeline builds hierarchical page-element graphs to derive traceable cross-page evidence paths and generate grounded QA pairs with page- and element-level evidence annotations. Experiments show that DocSkill with Qwen3-VL-8B achieves 41.0 accuracy and 49.2 F1 on MMHop-DocBench, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 5.5 and 6.3 points, respectively, and achieving competitive performance on MMLongBench-Doc and DocBench.

</details>


### [130] [DocXEval: A Systematic Evaluation of Explainability in Multimodal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36929)
*Saifullah Saifullah,David Dembinsky,Stefan Agne,Andreas Dengel,Sheraz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个面向多模态文档理解（文本、布局、图像）的可解释AI统一框架，适配现有XAI方法与评估指标，并在13种XAI方法、3个任务、8个数据集、4个模型、17个指标上系统基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态Transformer在多模态文档理解上性能突出，但其黑箱性阻碍高风险场景部署；现有XAI研究主要针对视觉和语言任务，缺少面向多模态文档理解的系统性可解释性方法与评测体系。

Method: 构建统一XAI框架，将现有解释方法与评估指标适配到文本-布局-图像文档理解场景，并针对语义实体识别等任务提出高效的多目标（multi-target）方法/指标适配；随后进行大规模系统评测与元评估（指标间一致性分析）及数据集级诊断分析。

Result: 13种XAI方法、3个任务、8个数据集、4个模型、17个指标的基准显示，仅少数方法在各评估标准下表现稳定一致，Occlusion整体在忠实性与可解释性上最优；元评估发现若干忠实性与复杂度指标间存在中等至强一致性，部分指标结果不可靠；诊断分析揭示各模态在不同任务与标签下的贡献、目标实体周围的空间归因模式以及潜在数据集偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作既是多模态文档理解XAI研究的基准，也是实践指南，并公开代码以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Multimodal transformer models have driven unprecedented performance breakthroughs in modern document understanding. However, the inherent black-box nature of these models remains a major obstacle to their safe deployment in high-stakes sectors. Despite significant progress in explainability for vision and language tasks, explainable AI (XAI) research tailored to multimodal document understanding has received little attention. To address this gap, we present \ourframework{}, the first comprehensive explainability framework that adapts existing XAI methods and evaluation metrics to the multimodal (text, layout, and image) document understanding context, while introducing efficient multi-target adaptations of both methods and metrics for domain-specific tasks such as semantic entity recognition. Using \ourframework{}, we systematically evaluate 13 XAI methods across 3 document understanding tasks, 8 datasets, 4 models, and 17 evaluation metrics, and find that only a few methods perform consistently across all evaluation criteria, with Occlusion emerging as the most faithful and interpretable method overall. Through a dedicated meta-evaluation, we observe moderate to strong agreement among several faithfulness and complexity metrics while identifying certain ones that produce unreliable results. Finally, through multiple dataset-wide diagnostic analyses, we uncover key insights into model behavior, including the contribution of individual modalities across tasks and labels, spatial attribution patterns around target entities, and potential dataset biases. Overall, this work serves as both a benchmark and a practical guide for future XAI research in multimodal document understanding. To support reproducibility, we publicly release our code at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Real-Time Smart Retail Analytics through Fog Computing using the RetailLightNet Lightweight Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35871)
*Mayank Atreya,Navin Chhibber,Shilpa Ajay,R Loshini,K Anitha*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出Fog架构下的超轻量检测/密度回归模型RetailLightNet（FRLN），配合运动门控抽帧与深度可分离卷积，在Jetson Nano类雾端设备上实现实时货架监测与排队评估，精度0.988/F1 0.976。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端视频分析存在通信时延高、原始视频传输隐私风险、重模型算力需求大等瓶颈，导致零售场景（客流追踪、货架盘点、排队）中需即时响应的决策被延迟。

Method: 雾计算端侧部署方案：图像归一化预处理＋运动门控帧选择剔除冗余帧；主干用深度可分离卷积做空间与通道特征学习；双预测头（存在性分类＋密度回归）分别支持货架监控与排队评估；在SKU110K与RPC公开数据集上训练与评测，面向NVIDIA Jetson Nano等受限硬件。

Result: FRLN精度0.988、召回0.976、F1 0.976，优于对比的常规CNN基线；摘要未给出时延、能耗、量化后精度与具体对比模型/数据集划分细节。

Conclusion: 轻量网络＋雾端推理可满足零售实时性要求，但属浅层骨干＋双头的通用轻量检测范式，方法新颖性有限，且缺少帧级时延、功耗与跨数据集泛化的量化证据。

Abstract: Smart retail systems now depend on the application of real-time video analytics, which can track customer behavior, manage shelf inventory, and enhance in-store processes. Currently cloud-based applications of video analytics face multiple challenges such as high latency of the communication itself, privacy concerns surrounding transmitting raw video, and the computational demands of the heavy vision models used on the often limited network infrastructure. These challenges lead to delays in decision making where prompt action is crucial. To address these short comings, this research proposes Fog-based analytics withRetailLightNet (F RLN), an ultra lightweight deep learning model which is specifically suited for the edge, resource constrained hardware capabilities of devices like the NVIDIA Jetson Nano. The approach utilizes publicly available retail datasets, SKU110K, and RPC, which contains an assortment of product, shelf, and customer interaction scenarios for this http URL processes include preprocessing images through normalization and employing motion-gated frame selection to improve efficiency by omitting redundant frames to ease the workload on the fog processing. Internal within Retail LightNet, image feature extraction is realized through depth wise separable convolutions which help support spatial and channel-wise learning without heavy computational costs. The model employs two prediction heads presence classification, and density regression, supporting shelf monitoring and queue assessment. The proposed F RLN framework attained better results in terms of precision (0.988), recall (0.976), and F1-score(0.976) when compared with existing convolutional neural network. Keywords: fog computing, motion-gated frame selection,NVIDIA jetson, retail lightnet, smart retail.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Compatibility and future-proof testing for multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36289)
*Yueshan Li,Lorenzo Fiaschi,Andrea Censi,Emilio Frazzoli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出"前瞻性(future-proofness)"监管框架：监管者发布种子智能体，各开发者独立测试其智能体与所有种子兼容，从而无需中心化规范或相互协调即可保证多智能体联合兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有独立开发的多智能体（如自动驾驶车辆）在共享环境中需要保证安全与任务完成等联合兼容性，但现有方法依赖部署前集中设计的规范或交互机制；当各开发者设计保密、且未来还会出现新智能体时，集中设计既不可行也不灵活。

Method: 采用双层结构：监管者仅发布种子智能体，开发者独立测试自家智能体与每个种子兼容。首先对一般多智能体系统形式化定义兼容性，论证种子兼容并不平凡地蕴含联合兼容；随后将前瞻性智能体刻画为种子的闭包（closure），并证明该闭包对种子具有单调性；最后研究仅通过与种子交互的可执行测试类，分析其区分前瞻性智能体的能力上界。

Result: 证明种子兼容不能自动推出联合兼容；给出前瞻性智能体的闭包刻画及闭包关于种子的单调性；刻画了仅与单个种子交互的可执行测试对前瞻性智能体的区分能力。

Conclusion: 通过监管者发布种子的去中心化机制可实现独立开发智能体间的联合兼容性，但需要借助种子的闭包（而非单一测试）来甄别真正前瞻性的智能体。

Abstract: Independently developed autonomous agents, such as autonomous vehicles, must guarantee joint compatibility, such as safety and task-completion requirements, when they operate in a shared environment. Existing methods require a centrally designed specification, or interaction mechanism before any agent is deployed. However, such centralized design is unrealistic when agent designs are confidential to their developers, and it is not flexible enough to accommodate future agent designs. We study a bi-level structure in which a regulator coordinates developers by publishing seed agents, and each developer independently tests its agent to ensure that it is compatible with every seed-compatible agent, a property we call future-proofness. Under this structure, joint compatibility is guaranteed for any agents that are designed and tested independently, without any coordination with each other. In this work, we first formulate compatibility for general multi-agent systems and show why it is not trivially implied by seed-compatibility. We then characterize the future-proof agents as the closure of the seed and show that this closure is monotone in the seed. Finally, we study a class of executable tests that involve interaction with the seed alone, and we ask to what extent such tests can distinguish the future-proof agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Calibrated for Which Policy? Conformal Uncertainty for Fixed World Models Under Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35877)
*Ayush Mittal,Dhruv Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作指出部署策略改变会使世界模型的不确定性失效，但在条件机制P(Y|X)不变假设下，可用前结果测度变换比w(X)=dQ_X/dP_X识别干预；策略控制动力学下该比可分解为状态占据与动作倾向项。作者用加权保形校准将边际覆盖率迁移到目标分布，并用域分类器的对数损失遗憾界给出比率误差界（E_P|w-\hat w|≤(1/(ργ))√(Δ(h)/2)），在DINO特征世界模型+Franka真实轨迹上验证（90%名义覆盖：普通55.0% vs 学习比率90.9%），并用Lean 4机器检验了代数与因果恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的不确定性/校准通常针对训练（源）部署策略成立；一旦部署策略改变，即使物理转移机制与预测器不变，覆盖率保证也会失效。作者希望在不重训模型的前提下，仅通过干预引起的测度变化把校准迁移到新策略，并提供可证明的误差界与因果识别条件。

Method: 1) 以可迁移性假设（条件机制P(Y|X)=Q(Y|X)）下的前结果测度变换对干预进行识别，权重w(X)=dQ_X/dP_X；2) 对策略控制动力学将w分解为状态占据项与动作倾向项的乘积；3) 采用加权保形校准（weighted conformal calibration）实现目标律下的边际覆盖；4) 用源-目标域分类器的总体对数损失遗憾Δ(h)来界定比率估计误差，得到E_P|w-\hat w_h|≤(1/(ργ))√(Δ(h)/2)，相比通用odds-map界γ^{-2}阶改进为(ργ)^{-1}阶；5) 实验使用已发布的DINO特征世界模型与真实Franka操作轨迹，在受控状态-动作分布偏移下评估覆盖与TV距离；6) 在Lean 4中机器检验有限支撑下的代数与因果恒等式及覆盖度复合步骤。

Result: 最强受控状态-动作偏移下，名义90%普通保形集实际覆盖55.0%，而oracle与学习比率校准分别为91.0%与90.9%；学习替代分布在总变差意义下与目标律偏差小于0.9%；域分类器遗憾界给出的比率误差尺度与经验表现一致；Lean 4形式化验证了分类器遗憾论证中所用恒等式与覆盖度复合步骤。

Conclusion: 校准应相对于部署律而非训练律：模型可以固定不变，而校准随干预迁移。该框架为策略偏移下的世界模型不确定性提供了可识别的、带显式误差界且可形式化验证的重校准路径，具有较强的理论严谨性与真实机器人证据，但依赖条件机制不变假设与可达支撑/重叠条件。

Abstract: Changing a deployment policy can invalidate a world model's uncertainty while leaving the physical transition mechanism and predictor unchanged. For one-step prediction, if source and target laws share the conditional mechanism $P(Y|X)=Q(Y|X)$, then the intervention is identified by the pre-outcome change of measure $w(X)=dQ_X/dP_X$. For policy-controlled dynamics this ratio factors into state-occupancy and action-propensity terms. Weighted conformal calibration transports marginal coverage to the target law; with an estimated ratio $\hat w$, $P_Q\{Y \in C_{\hat w}(X)\} \ge 1-\alpha-TV(Q,\hat Q) \ge 1-\alpha-E_P|w-\hat w|$. Population log-loss regret for a source-versus-target domain classifier bounds the same ratio error. If $h$ is its clipped target posterior, $\rho$ is the target-domain prior, and $\Delta(h)$ is population log-loss regret, then $E_P|w-\hat w_h| \le (1/(\rho\gamma))\sqrt{\Delta(h)/2}$. An exact cancellation improves the dependence from a generic odds-map bound of order $\gamma^{-2}$ to $(\rho\gamma)^{-1}$. Our experiments use a released DINO-feature world model and physical Franka trajectories. Under the strongest controlled state-action shift, ordinary 90% conformal sets cover 55.0%, whereas oracle and learned-ratio calibration cover 91.0% and 90.9%. The learned surrogate remains within 0.9% total variation of the target law. Calibration is relative to the deployment law: the model can remain fixed while calibration follows the intervention. We machine-check in Lean 4 the finite-support algebraic and causal identities used in the classifier-regret argument, including its coverage-composition step.

</details>


### [134] [From Slices to Volumes: Comparing 2D, 2.5D, and 3D Residual Networks for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy-Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35960)
*Bahodir Nazarov,Zineb Gharib,Fatima Zazoul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对比2D/2.5D/3D残差网络在肺结节恶性风险分类上的表现，并评估动量对比预训练、轻度增强与排除不确定标签的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中肺结节恶性风险评估的价值在于辅助早期诊断，但2D、2.5D、3D输入的表征选择及训练策略（自监督预训练、数据增强、标签质量）对性能的影响缺乏受控对比。

Method: 在LIDC-IDRI回顾性队列（2,654个结节，按放射科医师评分中位数划为良性/不确定/恶性）上按患者不重叠划分训练1,876/验证382/测试396。输入分别为单轴位切片（2D）、5张相邻切片（2.5D）和局部CT体块（3D）；每条轨道比较随机初始化基线、动量对比预训练+微调、轻度增强监督训练三种设置，按验证集Macro-F1选检查点；2D用3个随机种子，2.5D/3D为单次运行；另设排除不确定结节的二分类实验。

Result: 增强模型测试Macro-F1：2D 0.6134±0.0075、2.5D 0.6537、3D 0.5403，准确率相应为0.6128/0.6869/0.5455；增强在各轨道均提升Macro-F1，而所测动量对比预训练配方均导致下降。排除不确定标签的二分类Macro-F1为0.8227/0.8664/0.7554。

Conclusion: 增强的2.5D模型取得最佳观测性能；但预处理、优化、模型容量与单次重复的差异限制了对"维度"本身的归因。标签为放射科医师评估的恶性风险而非病理确诊癌症，结论外推需谨慎。

Abstract: Purpose: To compare 2D, 2.5D, and 3D residual networks for pulmonary nodule malignancy-risk classification and evaluate momentum-contrast pretraining, mild augmentation, and exclusion of indeterminate ratings. Methods: A retrospectively prepared LIDC-IDRI cohort contained 2,654 nodules labeled benign, indeterminate, or malignant using median radiologist ratings. Patient-disjoint splits comprised 1,876 training, 382 validation, and 396 test nodules. Representations included one axial slice, five neighboring slices, and a local CT volume. Each track evaluated a randomly initialized baseline, momentum-contrast pretraining followed by fine-tuning, and supervised training with mild augmentation. Checkpoints were selected by validation Macro-F1. The 2D experiments used three seeds; 2.5D and 3D results were single-run estimates. A separate binary experiment excluded indeterminate nodules. Results: Augmented models achieved test Macro-F1 values of $0.6134\pm0.0075$ for 2D, $0.6537$ for 2.5D, and $0.5403$ for 3D; the 2D uncertainty denotes population standard deviation across seeds. Corresponding accuracies were $0.6128$, $0.6869$, and $0.5455$. Augmentation improved Macro-F1 within every track, whereas the tested pretraining recipes reduced it. Binary Macro-F1 values were $0.8227$, $0.8664$, and $0.7554$, respectively. Conclusion: Augmented 2.5D achieved the highest observed performance. Differences in preprocessing, optimization, capacity, and replication limit attribution to dimensionality. Labels reflect radiologist-assessed malignancy risk rather than pathology-confirmed cancer. Code and experimental results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [135] [Knowledge distillation across teacher architectures for compact diabetic retinopathy grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35961)
*Mossa Manisa,Habiba Jahan Priya,A.K.M. Fakhrul Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在APTOS 2019五分类糖尿病视网膜病变分级任务上，系统评估离线response-based知识蒸馏：将ResNet-50、MobileNetV3-Large、Swin-T教师模型蒸馏进MobileNetV3-Small学生模型，得到1.52M参数、5.94MB的紧凑模型，三seed平均QWK 0.9024（基线0.8804）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜眼底图像分级精度要求高但部署受限于资源；需要验证知识蒸馏能否在医疗影像分级中压缩模型并保持可竞争性能，并厘清温度与硬标签权重等超参数的影响。

Method: 离线response-based蒸馏的受控研究：3×4×2网格（3种教师×4个温度×2个硬标签权重）单seed筛查，再对6组配置与无蒸馏基线做三seed评估；指标为二次加权kappa（QWK）与macro-F1，同时报告参数量与模型体积。

Result: 最佳单seed配置为Swin-T教师、温度4、硬标签权重0.7，QWK 0.9093（macro-F1 0.6851）；对应三seed均值0.9024，优于基线0.8804。学生模型1.52M参数、5.94MB，较Swin-T参数减少18.07倍。温度与硬标签权重效应随教师和配置而异，小差异对随机种子敏感。

Conclusion: 在给定协议下，知识蒸馏可产出性能可竞争的紧凑学生模型，但结论依赖单数据集与种子噪声，仍需独立外部验证；方法本身为通用蒸馏流程，无任务特定创新。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy grading from retinal fundus photographs is important but difficult to deploy on resource-constrained hardware. We conducted a controlled study of offline response-based knowledge distillation for five-class diabetic retinopathy grading on APTOS 2019, comparing ResNet-50, MobileNetV3-Large, and Swin Transformer-Tiny (Swin-T) teachers distilled into a MobileNetV3-Small student. A 3 x 4 x 2 grid of teacher, temperature, and hard-label weight was screened at one seed, while six configurations and the no-distillation baseline were evaluated across three seeds. The highest observed single-seed quadratic weighted kappa was 0.9093 (macro-F1 0.6851) for Swin-T at temperature 4 and hard-label weight 0.7; the corresponding three-seed mean was 0.9024, compared with 0.8804 for the baseline. The student used 1.52 M parameters and 5.94 MB, representing an 18.07x parameter reduction relative to Swin-T. Temperature and hard-label weight effects were teacher- and configuration-dependent, while small performance differences were sensitive to random seed. Knowledge distillation produced a compact student with competitive performance under the evaluated protocol, but independent external validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [136] [Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning in Dynamic Data-Driven Applications Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36190)
*Moises Ponce,Sarah Harkins Dayton,Ioannis Schizas,Vasileios Maroulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: B-EAGLe将图分类重构为序贯证据累积过程：GNN编码器+贝叶斯累积器，随观测逐步更新类别后验，达到足够置信度才决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN框架基本是静态的，难以适配DDDAS中观测序贯到达且存在不确定性的场景。

Method: 受CBGT-Net（皮层-基底核-丘脑网络）启发的概率框架：GNN编码器提取每个观测的图表示，贝叶斯累积器增量更新类标签后验分布，以置信度阈值触发决策提交。

Result: 在CIFAR10上取得有竞争力的分类性能，并在序贯观测下提供有原则的置信度估计。

Conclusion: 将图分类形式化为序贯证据累积，可实现不确定性感知的延迟决策，但仅在CIFAR10上验证，缺乏与物理AI/机器人/3D核心方向的关联。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used in modern machine learning, yet most existing frameworks remain fundamentally static, limiting their applicability in Dynamic Data Driven Application Systems (DDDAS) where observations arrive sequentially under uncertainty. We introduce B-EAGLe (Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning), a probabilistic framework inspired by CBGT-Net (cortico-basal ganglia-thalamic network) that reformulates graph classification as a sequential evidence accumulation process. B-EAGLe combines a GNN encoder with a Bayesian accumulator that incrementally updates posterior beliefs over class labels as new observations arrive, committing to a decision only when sufficient confidence is achieved. Experiments on CIFAR10 demonstrate competitive performance together with principled confidence estimation under sequential observations.

</details>


### [137] [Bridging Exploration and Loss of Plasticity in On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36309)
*Andrea Vasco Grieco,Evelyn D'Elia,Edoardo Caldarelli,Raffaello Camoriano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究PPO中"可塑性丧失"(Loss of Plasticity, LoP)现象，指出等向高斯策略的标准差σ显著影响LoP程度，并给出理论与实验解释，同时解释了常见缓解技术的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度RL在持续学习中会丧失从新数据适应学习的能力(LoP)，已有指标与之相关，但因果机制及产生LoP的几何条件尚不明确。作者希望在PPO+等向高斯策略这一连续动作空间主流配置下厘清LoP的成因。

Method: 在PPO与等向高斯策略框架下分析LoP，提出多个刻画策略标准差σ对LoP影响程度的度量指标，并辅以理论推导与实验验证；实验包括标准化LoP基准对比以及7自由度机械臂上的真实策略部署。

Result: 观察到σ对LoP程度有显著影响，理论与实验证据一致；并据此对若干主流LoP缓解技术的有效性给出解释，在7-DoF机械臂实机部署中完成验证。

Conclusion: 策略标准差σ是LoP的关键几何因素，可作为理解与缓解LoP的抓手；该结论为连续动作空间RL的持续学习稳定性提供机制性解释与实用指导。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have been observed to lose the ability to adaptively learn from new data, a phenomenon termed Loss of Plasticity (LoP). While many metrics have been shown to correlate with it, the causal mechanisms and the geometric conditions from which it can arise have not been clearly identified. We analyze LoP in Proximal Policy Optimization with isotropic Gaussian policies, the state-of-the-art formulation for many continuous action spaces in RL. We report multiple metrics which characterize the effect that the policy standard deviation $\sigma$ has on LoP, and we also suggest related explanations for the effectiveness of widely adopted LoP mitigation techniques. We observe a significant effect of $\sigma$ on the extent of LoP, and accompany this with both theoretical and experimental supporting arguments. Our experimental validation consists of LoP benchmark evaluations against standard baselines as well as real-world policy deployment on a 7-DoF robotic arm.

</details>


### [138] [PRISM: Pathology Reliability In Scarce-label Medicine. A Tile-level Benchmark of Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36517)
*Alicem Koyun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM：病理基础模型瓦片级可靠性基准，联合评估模型选择、标签比例、校准与跨机构OOD迁移，覆盖8个模型×6数据集×6标签比例×3随机种子共288个线性探针单元，并扩展到5家医院20个定向迁移对；揭示全监督下AUROC与校准排序一致（ρ=0.52）而1%标签下不一致、低标签下线性探针塌缩到单一类别、迁移AUROC<0.85时增加源域标签反而恶化目标ECE（86-88%组合）、温度缩放有49/288单元无法降低ECE且以欠自信为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型评测多聚焦全标签、同分布的分类性能，缺乏对校准、低标签预算行为与跨机构分布偏移鲁棒性的联合评估，导致实际部署时的可靠性结论不完整。

Method: 构建瓦片级基准PRISM（pip可安装包+参考结果）：8个病理基础模型（CLIP、PLIP、CONCH、UNI、Virchow2、GigaPath、H-Optimus-0、Midnight）×6个瓦片数据集×6档标签比例（1%–100%）×3随机种子的线性探针实验（共288单元/864次运行），外加以Camelyon17为基础的5家医院20个有向迁移对研究；分析温度缩放校准、ECH/ECE与AUROC的秩相关及元分析（Spearman ρ、I²）。

Result: 四类现象：(i) 全监督下AUROC秩稳定预测后温度校准秩（ρ=0.52, I²=0%），1%标签时关系变为数据依赖（ρ=-0.24, I²=67%）；(ii) 困难数据集在低于某标签比例时线性探针退化为单一类预测；(iii) 迁移AUROC低于约0.85时，增加源域标签在86-88%组合中反而升高目标域ECE；(iv) 温度缩放于49/288单元未能降低ECE，失校准以欠自信为主，源域拟合的温度在迁移失败时有害。

Conclusion: 病理基础模型的性能排序不等于可靠性排序：低标签与分布偏移下校准行为显著劣化，源域温度参数不可盲目迁移。PRISM提供可复现的288单元参考结果，支持在标签预算与机构偏移维度上做模型选择决策。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models are typically benchmarked on classification performance at full label availability and on in-distribution test sets, while calibration, behavior at low label budgets, and robustness under cross-institutional shift are seldom evaluated jointly. We introduce PRISM, a tile-level benchmark that measures the reliability of pathology foundation models along four axes: model choice, label fraction, calibration, and out-of-distribution transfer. PRISM evaluates eight foundation models (CLIP, PLIP, CONCH, UNI, Virchow2, GigaPath, H-Optimus-0, Midnight) on six tile-level datasets at six label fractions (1% to 100%) with three seeds (864 linear probe runs), extended with a five-hospital Camelyon17 study of 20 directed transfer pairs. Four phenomena emerge. (i) Under full supervision, AUROC rank predicts post-temperature calibration rank consistently across datasets (Spearman rho = 0.52, I^2 = 0%), whereas at 1% labels the relationship becomes dataset-specific (rho = -0.24, I^2 = 67%). (ii) On harder datasets the linear probe collapses to a single predicted class below a dataset-specific label fraction. (iii) Whether additional source-domain labels help or harm target calibration tracks how well the probe transfers: below a transfer AUROC of roughly 0.85, more labels worsen target ECE in 86-88% of combinations. (iv) Temperature scaling fails to reduce ECE in 49 of 288 cells, miscalibration is predominantly under-confidence, and a source-fitted temperature is harmful under failing transfer. PRISM is released as a pip-installable package with reference results for all 288 cells. Code and data: this https URL

</details>


### [139] [Tail Cues, Principal Corrections: Plug-and-Play Rectification for Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36870)
*Yingkai Yang,Chaoqi Chen,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TALOR：一种轻量、无反向传播的开放集测试时自适应校正模块，以源模型线性头诱导的子空间为几何锚，在主坐标子空间内用加权岭回归估计并扣除漂移分量，再用源分类头推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集测试时自适应（OSTTA）面临协变量漂移与语义漂移并存的非平稳目标流；现有方法依赖参数更新或可变更新的记忆库，计算/显存开销大，且易被未知类样本污染。

Method: 利用源分类器线性头的权重基做特征分解，将归一化特征拆为高增益主坐标与低增益尾部坐标；设计软路由的仿射校正，由"机制级主偏置"与"尾部条件斜率"（刻画样本级尾部-主部耦合）组合而成；通过加权岭回归闭式求解漂移量，从主坐标中减去该估计，重构特征后送入源头，全程无梯度反传。

Result: 在 ImageNet-C 搭配 6 个 csOOD 数据集上，H-score 比次优的 UniEnt 高 2.2%，速度提升 2.5 倍，GPU 显存仅为其 12%；作为后校正插件可将 COME 提升至 68.4% H-score，额外显存开销仅 2%。

Conclusion: 以源头几何结构为锚的闭式特征校正可在极低开销下有效抑制开放集测试时漂移，且具备良好插件兼容性；但验证仅限于图像分类基准，未涉及机器人、3D 或世界模型等具身场景。

Abstract: Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation (OSTTA) operates on non-stationary target streams where covariate and semantic shifts coexist. Existing TTA methods often obtain adaptation signals by updating model parameters or maintaining mutable memories, which can be costly and vulnerable to unknown samples. We present TALOR, a lightweight, backward-free rectification module that uses the source linear head as a geometric anchor. The head-induced basis decomposes each normalized feature into high-gain principal coordinates and low-gain tail coordinates. TALOR estimates a soft-routed affine correction in the principal subspace, combining a regime-level principal bias with a tail-conditioned slope that captures sample-level tail-to-principal coupling. Using weighted ridge regression, it subtracts this drift estimate from the principal coordinates, reconstructs the feature and feeds it into the source head. On the ImageNet-C benchmark with six csOOD datasets, TALOR outperforms the next-best UniEnt by 2.2% H-score while running 2.5x faster and using only 12% of its GPU memory; as a post-correction plug-in, it further boosts COME to 68.4% H-score with only 2% additional memory overhead.

</details>
