### [140] [V-LUMEN: Visual Lookup Memory for Embedding Scaling in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37019)
*Miso Choi,Daekeun Kim,Eunji Kim,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: V-LUMEN为视觉语言模型引入非参数化的“视觉查找记忆”：用冻结码本离散化视觉特征、以空间聚合哈希构造空间感知的memory key，并用文本条件相关性调制，通过门控残差注入主干。查找开销与表大小无关且仅在prefill发生一次，因此容量随存储而非逐token计算增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: embedding-level scaling（如产品键记忆式查找）已证明可作为独立于深度/宽度/稠密计算之外的容量轴，但视觉特征连续、具空间结构且依赖查询，无法直接套用离散token的n-gram查找，故需为VLM设计可寻址的视觉查找记忆。

Method: V-LUMEN：冻结码本离散化视觉特征→空间聚合哈希构建空间感知key→文本条件相关性信号调制→非参数寻址→门控残差融合进backbone。与LoRA/MoE适配器做等参数预算对照实验；在冻结Qwen3-VL-2B及另一backbone上验证。

Result: 在5个推理密集基准上显著提升冻结的Qwen3-VL-2B；对3个图像与训练集不相交的基准实现zero-shot迁移；等参数预算下将部分LoRA/MoE容量转移至V-LUMEN，两个backbone上精度基本保持，说明外部视觉记忆是独立于适配方法的容量维度。

Conclusion: 查找成本对表大小恒定、仅在prefill发生一次，性能提升来自存储扩展而非每token计算，为VLM提供了一个可扩展、与参数高效适配正交的容量轴。

Abstract: Embedding-level scaling expands the capacity of language models through lookup-based memory rather than by increasing model depth, width, or dense computation. Extending this idea to vision-language models (VLMs) is challenging because visual features are continuous, spatially structured, and query-dependent, unlike discrete text tokens that naturally support n-gram lookup. We introduce V-LUMEN, a visual lookup memory for VLMs. V-LUMEN discretizes visual features with a frozen codebook, builds spatially aware memory keys through spatial aggregation-based hashing, and modulates them with text-conditioned relevance signals; all addressing is non-parametric, and the retrieved embeddings are fused into the backbone through a gated residual pathway. On five reasoning-intensive benchmarks, V-LUMEN substantially improves a frozen Qwen3-VL-2B backbone and transfers zero-shot to three benchmarks whose images are disjoint from the training data. At a matched parameter budget, moving part of a LoRA or MoE adapter's capacity into V-LUMEN preserves accuracy on two backbones, indicating that external visual memory is an independent capacity axis rather than a stronger adaptation method. Because lookup cost is constant in table size and incurred only once at prefill, this capacity grows in storage rather than in per-token compute. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [141] [EmbeddingGemma 2: Unified and Efficient Multimodal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37364)
*Henrique Schechter Vera,Sahil Dua,Jovan Andonov,Sara Smoot,Sebastian Russo,Ivan Llanos,Max Matveev,Jae Hyeon Yoo,Kat Black,Shih-Cheng Huang,Josiah Aklilu,Maarten Grootendorst,Omar Sanseviero,Chandramouli Amarnath,Ian Ballantyne,Zhe Li,Daniel Salz,Ibrahim Alabdulmohsin,Ilya Philippov,Kristen Quan,Glenn Cameron,Edouard Yvinec,Luciano Martins,Doug Reid,Sindhu Raghuram Panyam,Weiyi Wang,Hengxuan Ying,Madhuri Shanbhogue,Tanmaya Dabral,Shanfeng Zhang,Juyeong Ji,Renjie Wu,Joe Zou,Xu Chen,Yunan Zhang,Yu-Hui Chen,Matthew Soulanille,Vinod Mamillapalli,Yi-Chun Kuo,Wai Hon Law,Matt Kreileder,Alice Zheng,Suril Shah,Mogan Shieh,Pooja Consul,Alberto Montes,Andreas Hess,Andres Sevilla,Babak Samari,Brendan McCloskey,Brian Potetz,Daniel Cer,Georgios Kathareios,Gustavo Hernández Ábrego,Hesen Zhang,Ji Ma,Jiaxing Wu,Keegan Mosley,Paul Suganthan,Rui Meng,Shen Gao,Shengcao Cao,Yi-Ting Chen,Yichang Chen,Zhongli Ding,Kendra Byrne,Cormac Brick,Olivier Lacombe,Qin Yin,Tris Warkentin,Armand Joulin,Tom Duerig,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EmbeddingGemma 2：开源原生多模态（文本/图像/音频/视频及组合）统一嵌入模型，提出逐层简化嵌入、多阶段课程（模态与目标渐进），在全部模态基准上取得新Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型多为单模态专用或需模态对齐拼接，缺乏在统一空间中同时处理文本、图像、音频、视频及混合模态的高质量开源方案；同时需兼顾资源受限部署。

Method: 统一多模态嵌入架构，采用简化的逐层嵌入（per-layer embeddings）设计；训练采用多阶段课程学习，逐步推进模态覆盖与训练目标；推理端支持选择性加载模态编码器、可调视觉token预算、可截断嵌入维度与量化权重；并给出关键设计消融。

Result: 在全模态综合嵌入基准上建立新的Pareto前沿，优于专用模型及规模为其2–12倍的模型；消融验证关键设计选择的有效性；模型开源。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了统一、可部署、开源的多模态嵌入基础模型，但主要面向检索/表示学习，未涉及具身智能、3D重建或机器人操作等具体任务。

Abstract: We present EmbeddingGemma 2, an open multimodal embedding model which natively represents text, images, audio, video, and combinations thereof in a unified space. Our approach introduces improvements such as simplified per-layer embeddings and a multi-stage curriculum with modality and objective progression which ensure strong quality across unimodal, cross-modal, and mixed-modal evaluations. Notably, in comprehensive embedding benchmarks across all modalities, EmbeddingGemma 2 establishes a new Pareto frontier, outperforming both specialist models and models two to twelve times larger. Furthermore, our design offers flexibility for resource-constrained environments, supporting selective modality encoder loading, adjustable vision token budgets, truncatable embedding dimensions, and quantized weights. We present ablations analyzing key decisions and release EmbeddingGemma 2 to promote further research.

</details>


### [142] [When Visual Evidence Is Insufficient: How Instructions, Post-Training, and Internal Activations Shape Refusal in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37430)
*Ananth K Suresh,Aveen Dayal,Gouthaman KV,Aditya Pola,Vineeth N Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统研究多模态大模型在视觉证据不足时"拒答"行为的三种控制来源：推理时指令（语义与位置）、后训练（SFT vs 偏好优化）、内部激活方向干预，并指出聚合拒答率会掩盖样本级差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM可靠部署需要"证据不足时拒答"，但拒答并不等于模型真正识别到缺失证据（可能源于无关安全约束）。因此需拆解拒答由外部指令、后训练与内部表示共同塑造的机制。

Method: 在MoHoBench上测试3个MLLM：(1) 操控拒答指令的含义（证据感知型 / 通用型 / 故意错配的安全指令）与提示位置；(2) 对比监督式与偏好式后训练；(3) 从自然回答/拒答样本中提取"拒答相关激活方向"并做激活干预，比较三者在样本级与聚合层面效果及对可回答样本的溢出影响。

Result: 拒答对指令含义与位置高度敏感，样本级变化被聚合拒答率掩盖；证据感知与通用拒答指令效果强于错配安全指令；后训练提升拒答最多但会产生"拒答措辞+尝试作答"的混合输出；激活干预幅度较小但在不同提示条件下可迁移，且未观察到对可回答样本的无差别拒答。

Conclusion: 拒答由外部指令、后训练与内部表示共同决定；评估需从"拒答倾向"中剥离"证据识别"，避免把拒答率当作视觉证据意识的代理指标。

Abstract: For reliable deployment, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) should recognize when an image provides insufficient evidence to answer a question and withhold unsupported answers. Yet refusal alone is not necessarily evidence of such recognition: models may refuse because of unrelated safety constraints or without identifying the missing evidence. We study how refusal on visually unanswerable questions is shaped by three sources of control: inference-time instructions, post-training, and internal activation interventions. Across three MLLMs on the MoHoBench benchmark, we systematically manipulate the meaning and placement of refusal instructions, compare supervised and preference-based post-training, and intervene on a refusal-related activation direction derived from naturally answered and refused examples. We find that refusal is highly sensitive to both the meaning and placement of instructions, with model-specific changes at the sample level that are obscured by aggregate refusal rates. Evidence-aware and general refusal instructions have stronger effects than an intentionally mismatched safety instruction. Post-training produces larger increases in refusal, but can also induce responses that combine refusal language with attempted answers. In contrast, activation intervention produces smaller yet transferable changes across prompt conditions, without evidence of indiscriminate refusal on tested visually answerable samples. These findings show that refusal is jointly shaped by external instructions, post-training, and internal representations, highlighting the need to disentangle evidence recognition from a model's learned propensity to refuse.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [When Slides Met Scans: Cross-Attention for Renal Cancer Prognosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36364)
*Anusha Krishnamoorthy,Basudha Pal*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文通过在冻结的病理与CT基础模型特征之间建立双向交叉注意力（16个组织学原型 × ≤32个肾脏中心CT token），融合肾癌的组织病理与影像信息用于疾病特异性生存预测，并在TCGA-KIRC内部评估与CPTAC-CCRCC外部测试中取得最优平均C-index，同时通过归因分析揭示采集伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织病理学与CT提供互补的肾癌信息，但两模态的尺度差异大（全切片图像 vs 三维体数据），已有融合方法多停留在简单拼接或单模态建模，缺乏跨尺度的显式交互机制，也缺少独立外部多模态验证。

Method: 采用冻结的病理与CT基础模型提取特征；将组织病理特征压缩为16个原型（prototype），CT则以肾脏为中心采样至多32个token；两者之间施加双向交叉注意力进行融合；下游用于DSS生存预测。评估采用5折交叉验证（TCGA-KIRC，210例/46事件），并在CPTAC-CCRCC（180例）上做外部测试（CT withheld）。附加归因分析定位预测相关图像区域。

Result: TCGA-KIRC上C-index为0.760±0.081，3年AUROC 0.773（95% CI 0.678–0.860），在评测的单模态与融合基线中平均表现最高；CPTAC-CCRCC外部测试（去除CT）DSS C-index为0.629；归因分析将预测关联到具体图像区域，同时暴露出采集伪影。

Conclusion: 结果支持跨尺度融合与独立多模态验证值得进一步研究；但外部测试性能明显下降（0.760→0.629）且仅含单模态外部验证，提示泛化性与模态鲁棒性仍是主要局限。

Abstract: Histopathology and computed tomography (CT) provide complementary views of renal cancer. We connect these modalities through bidirectional cross-attention between 16 histology prototypes and up to 32 kidney-centered CT tokens from frozen foundation models. In 210 TCGA-KIRC patients with 46 disease-specific survival (DSS) events, 5-fold evaluation yielded a C-index of $0.760\pm0.081$ and 3-year AUROC of 0.773 (95\% CI 0.678--0.860), the highest averages among evaluated unimodal and fusion baselines. External testing in 180 CPTAC-CCRCC patients yielded DSS C-index of 0.629 with CT withheld. Attribution analysis linked predictions to image regions while exposing acquisition artifacts. These findings support further study of cross-scale fusion and independent multimodal validation.

</details>


### [144] [Image Modeling: From Handcrafted Priors to Foundation Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36646)
*A. Murat Tekalp*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该综述将六十年来图像建模（从Tikhonov、TV、Non-local等手工先验，到PnP与扩散模型的隐式流形学习）统一到贝叶斯框架下，以log先验梯度即Stein score为共同数学引擎，贯通逆问题与条件生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号处理与机器学习社区长期各自发展逆问题求解与生成建模，路径看似平行；作者希望揭示二者实为同一图像建模演化脉络，用统一数学语言弥合学科鸿沟。

Method: 以贝叶斯框架将图像建模表述为对数据流形的隐式或显式概率刻画：早期方法显式定义log p(x)（Tikhonov、TV、Non-local），现代方法（PnP先验、去噪扩散模型）通过算法解耦与隐式流形学习得到去噪score；核心统一量是∇log p(x)，即Stein score，从而把MAP估计中的变分优化与基础模型中的后验采样串联起来。

Result: 为图像复原与条件生成提供统一的数学表述，说明去噪score是连接经典正则化优化与现代生成式基础模型的共同线索，并给出从手工解析模型到大数据学习模型的连续演化叙事。

Conclusion: 追求去噪score是贯通逆问题求解与条件生成的公共主线；该框架可作为跨社区的教学与研究组织范式。

Abstract: This paper unifies six decades of image modeling, evolving from handcrafted mathematical tractable priors to solve inverse problems in imaging to data-driven deep foundation image models for both image generation and solving inverse problems, under a common Bayesian framework that formulates image modeling as an implicit or explicit probabilistic characterization of a data manifold. Every image restoration or generative task fundamentally seeks to answer the same question: What makes an image look like a real image? In the Bayesian framework, this question is encapsulated by the prior probability density p(x), or its more optimization-friendly counterpart, the log-prior log p(x). While early works in solving inverse problems, such as Tikhonov, Total Variation (TV), and Non-local priors relied on explicit handcrafted definitions of log prior to model local smoothness, modern techniques, such as Plug-and-Play (PnP) priors and Denoising Diffusion models, are rooted in algorithmic uncoupling and implicit manifold learning that apply to both inverse problems and conditional image generation. By grounding imaging inverse problems and conditional image generation under a single mathematical engine: the gradient of the log-prior, or the Stein Score Function, we bridge the gap between signal processing and machine learning communities, turning what seems like parallel developments into a single, evolution of image modeling from handcrafted analytical models to models learned from big data, demonstrating that the pursuit of the denoising score is the common thread connecting variational optimization (in MAP estimation) to posterior sampling (in modern foundation image models).

</details>


### [145] [Quantized Edge Inference of a Self-Supervised SAR-Optical Encoder on Commodity x86 Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37056)
*Christian Sanchez-Leon,Dongming Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该文在三种商用x86节点（无AVX2的低功耗CPU到AVX2+VNNI参考平台）上部署预训练的SAR-光学Masked Autoencoder编码器，测试FP32/FP16/INT8及核显下的延迟、能耗与线性探测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督ViT已广泛作为对地观测的可复用编码器，但其在野外可用硬件上的推理成本鲜有刻画，缺少精度—延迟—能耗的系统性权衡数据。

Method: 将预训练SAR-optical MAE编码器部署于三个商品x86节点，在CPU与集成GPU上分别以FP32、FP16、INT8推理，用RAPL与插座功耗计测量延迟与能耗，平行以线性探测评估精度，并分析Pareto最优设备。

Result: 量化对精度近乎无影响：INT8使micro-mAP仅变化+0.0006，处于种子波动范围内；INT8在所有节点上降低CPU延迟（无AVX2节点1.45x，VNNI参考1.53x）并降低CPU能耗。Pareto最优设备随CPU代际提升由核显（最弱节点上快4.38x）转向CPU。

Conclusion: 在遥测编码器场景下，数值精度可视为纯成本决策项，INT8是稳健的默认选择；设备选型应随CPU类别演进重新评估，而非固定依赖集成GPU。

Abstract: Self-supervised vision transformers are increasingly used as reusable encoders for Earth observation, but their inference cost on the hardware available in the field is rarely characterized. We deploy a pretrained SAR-optical masked-autoencoder encoder on three commodity x86 nodes, from a low-power CPU without AVX2 to an AVX2+VNNI reference, at FP32, FP16 and INT8 on the CPU and, where present, the integrated GPU, and report latency, energy (RAPL and wall plug) and linear-probe accuracy. Quantization is accuracy-neutral: INT8 changes micro-mAP by only +0.0006, within the seed spread, so precision becomes a pure cost decision. INT8 lowers CPU latency on every node, by 1.45x even on the CPU without AVX2, and CPU energy wherever it is measured, and the VNNI reference (1.53x) suggests a further gain beyond AVX2. The Pareto-optimal device shifts from the integrated GPU, which is 4.38x faster than the CPU on the weakest node, to the CPU as the CPU class improves.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [E0.5: From Reconstructive to Future-Predictive 3D Foundations for Robotic World-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36011)
*X-Era AI Lab Team,Jianhao Lai,Zhihao Zhan,Zhiquan Liao,Qichang Li,Shubin Chen,Zhiyuan Liu,Hongsen Lei,Jianman Lin,Xiaoxin Lin,Ke Gong,Baiyue Gu,Shiming Chen,Junlin He,Longqi Yang,Junrong Xiao,Haolin Luo,Xiaojuan Su,Jianfeng Li,Yu Luo,Xu Sun,Likui Zhang,Jusheng Zhang,Qinhan Lyu,Tianshui Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E0.5：以3D基础模型（VGGT类）为主干的原生几何世界-动作模型，直接预测未来3D状态与动作，并在RoboTwin 2.0与真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）以视频基础模型或VLM为主干，3D几何仅作辅助输入或预测目标，未成为世界建模的原生表示，限制了空间动力学建模能力。

Method: 将3D基础模型作为主backbone，使3D几何成为世界建模与动作预测的共享原语表示；联合监督未来3D状态预测与动作预测；先在RoboTwin 2.0无具身预训练验证，再在AgiBotWorld-Beta（>160k轨迹）上于3D基础模型内联合预训练动作与未来几何。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0 clean/randomized成功率89.90%/50.09%（无具身预训练）；具身预训练后在AgileX Piper与Zerith H1真实操作平均成功率分别提升约15与7个百分点。

Conclusion: 几何原生基础模型可作为世界-动作建模的有效底座，具备获取机器人交互知识并迁移到真实操作的能力，属VWA系列世界-动作模型。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) offer a promising framework for embodied intelligence, yet mainstream approaches remain centered on video foundation models or vision-language models. Even when geometric features extracted by 3D models such as VGGT are incorporated, they are typically passed into a video model or VLM that serves as the primary backbone, leaving 3D geometry as an auxiliary input or prediction target rather than the native foundation for world modeling. We present E0.5, a geometry-native world-action model built directly on 3D foundation models. E0.5 introduces two key innovations. First, it adopts a 3D foundation model as the primary backbone, making 3D geometry the native representation for both world modeling and action prediction. Second, it explicitly predicts future 3D states together with actions, enabling the model to capture geometric world dynamics beyond action-only modeling or future video prediction. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 first demonstrate the effectiveness of E0.5 without embodied-data pretraining, achieving success rates of 89.90\% and 50.09\% under clean and randomized settings, respectively. Building on these results, we further pretrain E0.5 on more than 160k robot trajectories from AgiBotWorld-Beta, jointly learning robot actions and future geometry within the pretrained 3D foundation. This embodied pretraining improves mean real-world manipulation success rates by approximately 15 and 7 percentage points on AgileX Piper and Zerith H1, respectively. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of geometry-native foundations to acquire robot interaction knowledge and transfer it to physical manipulation. E0.5 embodies this geometry-native approach as a member of the VWA family of world-action models.

</details>


### [147] [Rollback: Experience Backtracking in World Models for Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36068)
*Jiayi Luo,Yang Zhang,Liliang Chen,Nan Wang,Chen Gao,Boxiang Qiu,Zhuoxuan Li,Baorui Peng,Chuankang Li,Yuxiang Yan,Si Liu,Qingyun Sun,Shuicheng Yan,Maoqing Yao,Jianxin Li,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Rollback范式，将动作条件世界模型从"前向模拟器"改造为"反事实引擎"：回滚到关键决策点、分支替代动作、对比结果以实现动作级信用分配，无需独立价值模型；并统一分布式世界模型搜索与异步真机执行，形成"真实经验精化世界模型↔世界模型驱动策略提升"的自增强闭环（test-time physics grounding，用LoRA在线校正动力学）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的方法仅把世界模型当作物理环境的单向代理，浪费了物理世界不具备的独特能力——回滚到过去决策点并探索反事实未来。同时，单靠想象改进策略缺乏细粒度信用分配，而真机交互成本高、难以规模化。

Method: Rollback：在世界模型中进行回滚式树搜索，识别关键决策点后分支替代动作，对比不同分支结果以将成功归因到单个动作，从而在关键区域集中"想象"并把反事实结果蒸馏为动作级监督；策略无需单独学习价值网络。系统层面构建分布式世界模型搜索与异步真机执行统一训练框架，真机轨迹通过轻量LoRA管线持续校正世界模型动力学。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务上，相对最强基线成功率分别绝对提升10.67%（积木抽取）、11.11%（抛球）、11.85%（桌面冰壶），且训练过程提升更稳定；虽由远低于人类水平的遥操作示范初始化，抛球与桌面冰壶在8小时训练内成功率超过人类玩家平均水平。

Conclusion: 将世界模型由被动模拟器转为反事实推理引擎，可把想象集中在关键决策上并实现细粒度动作级监督，配合test-time physics grounding闭环显著提升真实机器人策略，展示了世界模型作为可自校正训练环境的潜力。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models offer a scalable alternative to costly real-world interaction for improving robot policies. However, existing world-model-based methods largely treat world models as forward-only surrogates of the physical environment, leaving untapped a capability no physical environment affords: rewinding to past decision points to explore counterfactual futures. We introduce Rollback, a policy improvement paradigm that turns world models from passive simulators into counterfactual engines: it rolls back to critical decision points, branches into alternative actions, and contrasts their outcomes to attribute success to individual decisions. This concentrates imagination where it matters most and distills counterfactual outcomes into fine-grained, action-level supervision without a separately learned value model. We build a scalable training system that unifies distributed world-model search with asynchronous real-robot execution, forming a self-reinforcing cycle: real-world experience continually sharpens the world model, and a sharper world model in turn drives further policy improvement. We term the world-model side of this cycle test-time physics grounding, a capability that hand-built physics simulators lack: while the world model serves as the training environment, real transitions from the policy it trains correct its dynamics through a lightweight LoRA pipeline. Experiments on three challenging real-world tasks show that Rollback improves success rates over the strongest baseline by an absolute 10.67%, 11.11%, and 11.85% on block extraction, ball tossing, and tabletop curling, respectively, while achieving more stable improvement throughout training. Although initialized from teleoperated demonstrations far below human level, Rollback surpasses the average success rate of human players on both ball tossing and tabletop curling within 8 hours of training.

</details>


### [148] [Closed-Loop Aware Planning via Imitation-Free Speed Scoring for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36079)
*Emad Aghajanzadeh,Thomas Schwarzfischer,Marc Kaufeld,Moritz Thoma,Ryan Pégoud,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Pierpaolo Mori,Nael Fasfous,Johannes Betz,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CLAP-AD：路径用模仿学习、速度用基于物理安全约束监督的 learned scoring 对候选速度打分，作为即插即用规划头，首次实现无需速度条件训练的测试时速度可控端到端自动驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环模仿学习的两大缺陷：(1) 专家演示每个场景仅一条行为，模型无法学习其它合法行为，也无法在测试时适配用户偏好；(2) 演示中的噪声/歧义造成不一致监督。驾驶本身是多模态的，且多样性主要来自速度而非路径。

Method: 解耦规划：路径通过模仿学习得到；速度则对一组候选速度用学习到的打分函数按多准则评估，监督信号来自物理驱动的安全约束。实现为 CLAP-AD 插件式规划头，可接入现有端到端驾驶模型，无需 speed-conditioned 训练即可在推理时显式控制速度选择。

Result: Bench2Drive-Speed 上 Speed-Adherence Score 82.53（新 SOTA，比最强基线 TFv6 高 12.9），并保持强闭环性能；Bench2Drive 上 driving score 较 UniAD、VAD 分别提升 +28.5、+32.7；真实数据 NAVSIM v2 上 EPDMS 较 backbone 提升 +6.1。

Conclusion: 以“路径模仿 + 速度可打分”的解耦范式替代纯模仿，既提升闭环一致性与可靠性，又赋予端到端驾驶测试时可控的速度偏好调节能力，且以插件形式可广泛复用。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has predominantly relied on open-loop imitation learning for training, which presents two limitations. First, expert demonstrations show only one behavior per scenario, so the model cannot learn alternative valid behaviors and, in turn, cannot adapt to user-specified preferences at test time. Second, noise or ambiguity in these demonstrations can produce inconsistent, unreliable supervision for the end-to-end models. Driving is inherently multi-modal: multiple different behaviors can be valid responses to the same scene, and this diversity arises largely from speed rather than path. We introduce a planning paradigm that learns the path through imitation while scoring a set of candidate speeds across multiple criteria with a learned function supervised by physically grounded safety constraints. We realize this paradigm as Closed-Loop Aware Planning for Autonomous Driving (CLAP-AD), a plug-in planning head that integrates with existing end-to-end driving models and, to our knowledge, is the first to enable explicit test-time control over their speed selection without speed-conditioned training. On Bench2Drive-Speed, CLAP-AD sets a new state of the art Speed-Adherence Score of 82.53, topping TFv6, the strongest baseline on this metric, by 12.9 points while retaining strong closed-loop driving performance. On Bench2Drive, it improves driving score by +28.5 and +32.7 over UniAD and VAD, and on NAVSIM v2, built on real-world driving data, EPDMS by +6.1 over its backbone. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [149] [Program-Level Adversarial Co-Adaptive Self-Play for Robotic Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36143)
*Leo Benaharon,Chi Zhang,Wei Zheng,Weifan Zhang,Yifan Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出程序级对抗式协同自适应自对弈框架，让编码智能体基于对手代码与任务反馈生成攻击/防御程序，在自动驾驶与双足人形两类环境中进行8代共演化实验，揭示防御为局部任务恢复而非持续鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可改写机器人的可执行程序以适配任务，但面对持续演化的对手时，单次成功响应未必长期有效；作者希望在不微调模型的前提下，让攻击与防御在程序层面协同演化，并把这种适应过程与局限以可执行工件的形式暴露出来。

Method: 构建程序级对抗协同自适应自对弈框架：编码智能体读取对手已测代码与任务反馈，生成攻击与防御程序；为自动驾驶提供环境级接口以挂载外部程序，为人形运动提供SDK级防御接口；开展两个8代（generation）的案例研究。

Result: 自动驾驶防御方演化出“直接驱动恢复”（direct-actuation recovery）策略；人形防御方则改进对输入状态的校验。两者均实现了局部任务恢复，但未能建立持续鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 程序级对抗自对弈能产出具体可执行的软件响应，但其鲁棒性是局部且易被后续攻击代次击穿的；框架的价值在于通过可执行工件与任务级证据使适应过程及其局限可观测。目前属于方法探索性工作，缺乏真实部署与量化增益。

Abstract: Coding agents can adapt robotic software by revising executable programs, but successful responses to an evolving opponent may not remain effective. We present a framework for program-level adversarial co-adaptive self-play in which coding agents generate attacks and defenses from tested opponent code and mission feedback, without model fine-tuning. Environment-specific interfaces support external programs for autonomous driving and SDK-level defenses for humanoid locomotion. Two eight-generation case studies reveal concrete software responses: a driving defender introduces direct-actuation recovery, while a humanoid defender revises incoming-state validation. These responses provide local task recovery but do not establish sustained robustness. The framework makes such adaptation and limitations observable through executable artifacts and task-level evidence.

</details>


### [150] [cuArms: GPU-Accelerated Conflict-Based Planning for Multi-Arm Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36250)
*Philip Huang,Fabio Ramos,Jiaoyang Li,Caelan Garrett,Lars Johannsmeier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: cuArms: 面向多机械臂操作的GPU并行冲突搜索规划器，结合可复用多模态路线图与惰性冲突/死锁消解，并由高层调度器提出候选骨架，联合优化任务分配、调度、运动与操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂协同有望大幅提升吞吐、效率与灵巧性，但现有方法（共享工作区任务分配/调度、联合运动规划、端到端多臂策略学习）存在速度慢、依赖领域特定假设、随机器数与任务时域扩展性差的问题。

Method: 将冲突搜索（CBS类）与GPU并行协同设计：为各机器人构建可复用的多模态路线图以高效分解搜索空间；用惰性搜索（lazy search）延迟处理冲突与死锁；上层调度器生成候选骨架（skeleton），交由cuArms落地为协调操作计划，实现任务分配、调度、运动与技能的统一优化。

Result: 在实验中达到新SOTA：可实时给出初始解，并支持任意时间（anytime）在运动质量与调度上持续改进；无需学习类方法所需的数天领域特定训练。提供仿真与真实工业任务的视频。

Conclusion: 将经典冲突搜索与GPU并行、多模态路线图复用及高层骨架调度结合，可在不作领域特定训练的前提下高效求解多臂联合任务分配-调度-运动-技能问题，具备实时性与可扩展性优势。

Abstract: Multi-arm manipulation has the potential to dramatically improve the throughput, efficiency, and dexterity of many manipulation tasks. Recent work coordinates multiple robot arms in a shared workcell by allocating and scheduling tasks for greater parallelism, jointly planning multi-arm motions, or directly learning multi-arm policies. So far, these approaches are either too slow, rely on domain-specific assumptions, or scale poorly with the number of robots and task horizon. Thus, we introduce cuArms, a conflict-based planner co-designed with GPU parallelism that efficiently decomposes the search space using reusable multi-modal roadmaps for individual robots and resolves conflicts and deadlocks through a lazy search. To jointly optimize task assignment, schedules, motions, and manipulation skills, we integrate a high-level scheduler that proposes candidate skeletons for cuArms to ground into coordinated manipulation plans. Experimental results show that our method establishes a new state of the art, with real-time initial solutions and anytime improvements in both motion quality and scheduling, without days of domain-specific training required by learning approaches. Videos of both simulated and real-world industrial tasks are available on this https URL .

</details>


### [151] [Learning Multi-Phase Robot Loco-Manipulation via Phase-Conditioned Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36270)
*Simone Manni,Annika Wollschläger,Dario Bellicoso,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter,Simon Le Cleac'h,Jan Brüdigam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将PPO扩展为多阶段全身loco-manipulation单策略：相位条件critic、相位门控奖励、多阶段重置课程，配合解耦分层控制，在Unitree G1与Boston Dynamics Spot+机械臂真机上完成长时程多阶段任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂长时程全身loco-manipulation（下蹲、抓取、移动组合）难以用强化学习从零探索；现有方案依赖人类动捕数据与humanoid平台，牺牲平台无关性并带来大量数据处理开销。

Method: 提出相位感知的PPO变体：phase-conditioned critic用于价值分解/信用分配，phase-gated reward functions按阶段激活奖励，multi-phase reset curriculum引导探索；采用解耦分层控制架构，底层负责平衡动力学、高层执行相位决策，从而实现跨异构硬件（humanoid与四足+臂）共享同一训练框架。

Result: 在Unitree G1与搭载机械臂的Boston Dynamics Spot真机上演示多阶段loco-manipulation技能，并通过消融实验验证相位条件critic、门控奖励、重置课程与分层架构各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 不依赖人类运动数据，仅通过环境交互即可习得复杂全身loco-manipulation行为；将高层任务结构（相位）显式注入策略学习，兼顾长时程任务可解性与跨平台通用性。

Abstract: Complex whole-body loco-manipulation requires executing diverse maneuvers, such as crouching, grasping, and locomotion, within a single long-horizon task. While performing simple pick-and-place behaviors is well-established, scaling to complex, long-horizon tasks remains difficult. Currently, this challenge is addressed by relying on human motion data and correspondingly humanoid platforms, which limits platform independence and introduces substantial data processing overhead. Instead, we extend proximal policy optimization (PPO) to train a single policy for multi-phase tasks across diverse robotic hardware, leveraging prior task structure to shape behavior. Our method adapts PPO using a phase-conditioned critic, phase-gated reward functions, and a multi-phase reset curriculum to guide exploration. To support varied hardware, we employ a decoupled hierarchical control architecture that separates lower-level balancing dynamics from high-level phase execution. We demonstrate multi-stage loco-manipulation skills on hardware on both the Unitree G1 and a Boston Dynamics Spot equipped with a manipulator arm, and validate our design choices through ablations. Our approach enables the acquisition of complex loco-manipulation behaviors purely through environment interaction while directly incorporating high-level task structure into policy learning.

</details>


### [152] [ASPEN: Adaptive Semantic Reasoning via Command Persistence Prediction for Efficient Navigation VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36285)
*Vieri N. Akustia,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASPEN 是一个事件触发的调度器,通过预测冻结的导航 VLA 策略是否会重复上一条指令,来决定在指令边界处复用命令还是重新调用 VLA 语义推理,从而降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导航 VLA 策略在每个指令边界都调用昂贵的语义推理,即使下一条指令与之前相同;简单复用命令虽能省算力,却可能漏掉依赖指令的转向或停止。

Method: 轻量多模态预测器融合时序观测、语言指令、执行历史和缓存的 VLA 置信度,估计"命令持续性";在可触发边界上,预测持续性足够则复用命令,否则触发 VLA 重新推理。

Result: 在 RxR-CE val-unseen 上与冻结 NaVILA 评测:VLA 调用减少 9.5%,估算推理时间减少 8.1%(含调度开销),导航性能相当,92.1% 回合动作序列与基线完全一致。

Conclusion: 指令级缓存调度可在几乎不损失导航质量的前提下降低导航 VLA 的推理成本,但收益幅度有限且局限于冻结策略的推理加速。

Abstract: Navigation vision-language-action (VLA) policies repeatedly invoke costly semantic reasoning even when the next command remains unchanged. Command reuse can reduce computation but risks missing instruction-dependent turns or stops. We introduce ASPEN, an event-triggered scheduler based on command persistence prediction: estimating whether the frozen base policy would reissue its previous command. A lightweight multimodal predictor combines temporal observations, language instructions, execution history, and cached VLA confidence. At eligible command boundaries, sufficient predicted persistence permits command reuse. Otherwise, ASPEN triggers fresh semantic reasoning from the VLA. Evaluated with frozen NaVILA on cross-dataset RxR-CE val-unseen, ASPEN reduces VLA calls by 9.5% and estimated inference time by 8.1%, including scheduling overhead. It maintains comparable navigation performance and preserves identical baseline action sequences on 92.1% of episodes.

</details>


### [153] [SG^2SLAM: Spatial Geometry-Informed 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Monocular Outdoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36312)
*Ziang Guo,Didar Seyidov,Yixiao Zhou,Xuefeng Zhang,Ali Alridha Abdulkarim,Chen Min,Zufeng Zhang,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG^2SLAM：用块级度量先验（streaming spatial geometry）替代逐帧2D-3D对应，结合视角方向子空间（ray-conditioned 3DGS kernel），联合优化几何与位姿，提升单目户外SLAM一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目户外导航需要鲁棒的相机运动估计与稠密可导航地图；3DGS SLAM虽具显式可微参数化与闭式投影优势，但依赖逐帧2D像素-3D点关联，模块化系统会把跟踪误差吸收进地图，造成级联退化。

Method: 提出SG^2SLAM：1) Streaming spatial geometry——以chunk级度量先验监督跟踪与建图，共享同一几何源，替代逐帧跨维对应；2) Ray-conditioned 3DGS kernel——为每个primitive扩展视角方向子空间以感知单目视点；3) 对空间几何与位姿进行联合优化以获得全局一致的建图与轨迹。

Result: 在KITTI与Waymo Open数据集上，单目户外导航任务中优于逐帧关联的基线方法；代码将在录用后开源。

Conclusion: 以chunk级几何先验与视角条件化核缓解单目3DGS SLAM的误差级联问题，实现跟踪-建图联合一致优化，但仅提供数据集实验，缺乏实机部署与泛化性验证。

Abstract: Monocular outdoor navigation requires robust camera motion estimation and a dense, navigable map, where 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM serves this task through an explicit differentiable parameterization and closed-form projection. However, typically, the geometry is recovered by associating 2D pixels with 3D points, so a modular SLAM system absorbs tracking error into the map, leading to cascading degradation. To address these gaps, we present SG^2SLAM, which couples streaming spatial geometry with a ray-conditioned 3DGS kernel. Streaming spatial geometry replaces the per- frame cross-dimensional correspondence with a chunk-level metric prior that supervises tracking and mapping from one source. A ray-conditioned kernel extends each primitive with a viewing-direction subspace that senses the monocular camera viewpoints. The joint optimization over spatial geometry and poses ensures a globally consistent map and trajectory outcome. Experiments on the KITTI and Waymo Open datasets show that our SG^2SLAM surpasses the per-frame association baselines in the monocular outdoor navigation task. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [154] [HIDRA: Hierarchical Dual-Routing Attention for Replay-Free Lifelong Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36374)
*Fanqi Yu,Matteo Tiezzi,Cigdem Beyan,Tommaso Apicella,Vittorio Murino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HIDRA：面向无回放单遍终身模仿学习的分层双注意力路由框架，用指令级专家检索与上下文细化解耦，并结合键级几何正则稳定路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人需从多模态示范流中持续学习，且常无法存储或回放历史数据；单遍无回放的终身模仿学习中，顺序更新导致表征漂移，使基于专家混合（MoE）的路由出现干扰，专家选择能力随时间退化。

Method: 提出HIDRA结构化路由框架：①分层双注意力机制，将指令级专家检索与上下文相关细化两阶段解耦，降低路由干扰；②键级几何正则，约束任务内表征对齐与任务间分离，抵抗表征漂移下的路由失稳；③支持专家集合可扩展的持续适配。

Result: 在LIBERO多个基准套件（含异构任务序列与改写语言指令的更难变体）上，AUC与前向迁移均优于无回放和有回放基线，遗忘率低；在高干扰与语言变异条件下增益更大。

Conclusion: 通过分层路由解耦与几何正则约束，可在无回放单遍设定下实现稳定的专家选择与可扩展的终身模仿学习，具备较好的抗干扰与语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotic agents operating in real-world environments must adapt continuously from a stream of multimodal demonstrations, often under constraints that preclude storing or revisiting past data. In this replay-free, single-pass lifelong imitation learning setting, sequential updates can progressively distort the representation space, making expert-based approaches particularly sensitive to routing interference, causing expert selection to degrade over time. We propose HIDRA, a structured routing framework that mitigates this issue through a hierarchical dual-attention mechanism, which decouples instruction-level expert retrieval from context-dependent refinement. To further stabilize routing under representation drift, HIDRA introduces a key-level geometric regularization that enforces both alignment within tasks and separation across tasks. The resulting approach enables reliable expert selection while supporting an expandable set of experts for continual adaptation. We evaluate HIDRA on several LIBERO benchmark suites, including more challenging variants with heterogeneous task sequences and paraphrased language instructions. Our approach consistently outperforms both replay-free and replay-based baselines, improving AUC and forward transfer while maintaining low forgetting, with stronger gains under high interference and language variation. The code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [155] [A 2-DOF MultiBody Track Joint for Race Car Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36407)
*Srikanth Sivaramakrishnan,Behzad Danaei,Adwait Verulkar,Ryan Trinter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrackJoint：用Modelica多体二自由度关节描述三维赛道曲面，以达朗贝尔原理闭合约束，同一FMI 3.0 FMU可同时用于时域仿真与CasADi/IPOPT最小圈速优化，并以弧长为状态、发布其时间导数实现"以距离为自变量"的重写，在新加坡F1赛道给出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有赛车多体仿真与最优控制通常需要两套模型（仿真模型与优化模型），且沿赛道几何约束的闭合往往依赖拉格朗日乘子或人工约束，导致模型难以在仿真与最小圈速优化之间直接复用。作者希望用一种统一的、以弧长为自然坐标的关节组件消除这种分裂。

Method: 将三维路面几何预先拟合为一小组周期性分段五次样条，构造Modelica MultiBody二自由度关节（弧长s与横向偏移n），由二者给出frame_b连接器的位姿与角速度；以达朗贝尔原理闭合道路约束，令两个广义力为零从而避免拉格朗日乘子；关节与赛车多体模型在Dymola中装配，导出为单个FMI 3.0 FMU；因关节保留弧长作为实数状态并输出其时间导数，可直接改写为以距离为自变量的最优控制问题，同一FMU既做时域仿真又做CasADi/IPOPT最小圈速优化。

Result: 在F1新加坡街道赛道上完成了最小圈速仿真，验证了同一FMU在时域仿真与优化两种用途下的可行性；未提及与实测数据或其他方法的定量对比。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一种建模层面的轻量方案：用弧长参数化样条路面＋二自由度关节＋达朗贝尔闭合，使多体赛车模型无需修改即可在两套工具链间复用，并可推广到任意预先拟合样条的赛道；实际价值取决于公开对比基准与优化数值表现。

Abstract: TrackJoint is a Modelica MultiBody component that turns a three-dimensional road surface into a joint with two degrees of freedom. The two joint coordinates are the distance travelled along a reference line and the sideways offset from that line. Together they give the position, orientation and angular velocity of a single frame_b connector. All road geometry reaches the joint as a small set of periodic piecewise-quintic splines, so one component can represent any circuit whose splines have been fitted beforehand. The road constraint is closed with d'Alembert's principle. The two generalized forces that belong to the joint coordinates are set to zero, so no Lagrange multipliers are needed. The joint is connected to a multibody race car in Dymola and the pair is exported as one FMI~3.0 FMU. The same FMU is then used for time-domain simulation and for CasADi/IPOPT minimum lap-time optimization, with no change to the model in between. This works because the joint keeps the arc-length coordinate as a real state and publishes its time derivative, which is the quantity that lets the problem be rewritten with distance We report results for minimum lap time simulations on the Singapore Formula~1 circuit.

</details>


### [156] [A Singularity Avoiding Track Model for Race Car Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36430)
*Adwait Verulkar,Behzad Danaei,Srikanth Sivaramakrishnan,Ryan Trinter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出赛道点云→闭合 C⁴ 可微三维 Monge 曲面（MCL 弧长坐标+侧向偏移+高程多项式）的拟合流程，用于 Modelica 高保真赛车优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以几何中心线为参考线时，急弯处相邻法线在可行驶区域内相交，导致参数化非单射、法线映射退化，进而产生无效道路几何，影响仿真与优化。

Method: 用二次规划在赛道边界、周期性、jerk 正则约束下最小化积分曲率，求取最小曲率线 MCL 作为参考线；以其弧长与带符号侧向偏移参数化 Monge patch，高程用可选阶数多项式；曲面与赛道宽度导出为周期分段五次 C⁴ B 样条并作为非线性规划求解。

Result: 在所有测试赛道上，MCL 将参数化失效区域推至可行驶带之外，法线映射在全赛道宽度上保持单射；弧长-时间变换更接近线性，利于最小圈时问题的收敛与求解。

Conclusion: 参考线选择是曲面可微建模的关键：MCL 相比中心线给出全局单射、C⁴ 光滑且良态的赛道表示，可作为车辆动力学与轨迹优化的统一几何前端。

Abstract: A track fitting pipeline is presented that converts a measured racetrack point cloud into a closed, $C^4$ differentiable, three-dimensional surface model suitable for high-fidelity Modelica race-car optimization. The surface is a Monge patch, parametrised by a curvilinear arc-length coordinate measured along the minimum-curvature line (MCL) and a signed lateral offset, with a height polynomial of user-selectable degree capturing the cross-sectional elevation profile. The choice of MCL as the reference line is deliberate since anchoring the same surface on the geometric centre-line can cause adjacent normals to cross inside the drivable region on tight corners. The resulting non-injective parametrisation assigns multiple curvilinear addresses to the same three-dimensional point and returns invalid road geometry. The MCL is obtained from a quadratic program that minimises integrated curvature subject to track-boundary, periodicity, and jerk-regularisation constraints. By reducing total curvature while remaining within the track bounds, the MCL moves the region of parametrisation breakdown outside the drivable strip on all circuits studied, keeps the normal map injective across the full track width, and yields a more linear time-to-arc-length transformation for minimum lap-time problems. The fitted surface and the associated track widths are exported as periodic piecewise-quintic $C^4$ B-splines, solved as a nonlinear program.

</details>


### [157] [Ground Sample Distance Constrained k-Coverage Viewpoint Planning via Set Multi-Cover Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36450)
*Waleed Bin Khalid,Chul Min Yeum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对体素化结构的GSD约束k覆盖视点选择，提出动态加权贪心算法，比ILP多3.00–16.39%视点即可实现近满覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机3D重建与检测需要满足图像质量约束且对每个表面区域多次观测的视点，而基于重叠率的规划启发式无法保证这一约束。

Method: 沿表面外法向生成候选视点，经距离、视角、FOV、遮挡测试筛选，对可见性不足体素补采样；将选择问题建模为带逐体素覆盖需求的集合多覆盖（set multi-cover）；提出动态加权贪心启发式，优先剩余覆盖缺口大且可视替代方案稀缺的体素；与顺序重叠剪枝、ILP同池对比。

Result: 在2种结构、4种体素分辨率、3种覆盖需求下实现完整或接近完整的k覆盖，视点集紧凑，仅比ILP多用3.00–16.39%视点，并具可解释的贪心选择过程。

Conclusion: 动态加权贪心为GSD约束k覆盖视点选择提供了接近最优且可解释的实用方案，适用于无人机结构巡检与重建的覆盖规划。

Abstract: Drone-based 3D reconstruction and inspection require viewpoints that satisfy image-quality constraints while observing each surface region multiple times, which overlap-based planning heuristics do not guarantee. We study ground sample distance (GSD)-constrained $k$-coverage viewpoint selection for structures represented by surface voxels. Candidate viewpoints are generated along outward surface directions, screened with distance, viewing-angle, field-of-view, and occlusion tests, and augmented for voxels with insufficient visibility. Selection is then posed as set multi-cover with per-voxel coverage demands. We propose a dynamically weighted greedy heuristic that prioritizes remaining coverage deficits and scarce viewing alternatives, and compare it with sequential overlap-based pruning and an integer linear programming (ILP) formulation on the same candidate pools. Across two structures, four voxel resolutions, and three coverage requirements, the proposed approach achieves full or near-full $k$-coverage with compact viewpoint sets, using only 3.00--16.39\% more viewpoints than ILP while offering an interpretable selection process based on coverage deficits and viewpoint availability.

</details>


### [158] [Whole-Body Humanoid Control via Stochastic Motion Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36452)
*Michael Drolet,Oleg Arenz,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出简洁的离散运动表示（随机运动tokenizer），由高层控制器按行为标签或文本指令生成，直接作为人形机器人全身运动跟踪策略的条件输入，并在Unitree G1实体上完成舞蹈、格斗、行走及35秒格斗到舞蹈的连续切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 速度指令接口对需要复杂全身运动的人形机器人过于受限；现有全身运动跟踪策略对未来期望运动信息掌握有限，难以在行为切换前做受控偏离，从而影响长时任务成功率。

Method: 用简洁运动表示（可由高层控制器在给定行为标签或文本命令下生成）作为跟踪策略的详细运动参考；采用随机运动tokenizer将运动离散化为token接口，并与FSQ、VQ-VAE在重建质量上同容量对比。

Result: 同容量下随机运动tokenizer对Unitree G1动作的重建优于FSQ和VQ-VAE；离散接口驱动的全身跟踪策略在实体G1上执行各16.8秒的舞蹈、格斗、行走，以及35秒的格斗到舞蹈序列。

Conclusion: 离散运动token作为高层指令与全身跟踪策略之间的接口，可实现灵活、可切换的真实人形机器人全身运动控制，为长时组合任务提供可行范式。

Abstract: While velocity-commanded RL policies are effective for controlling quadrupeds, they are a limited interface for humanoids that need to perform complex whole-body motions. Furthermore, RL tracking policies for whole-body motions typically have limited information about the desired future motion, preventing them from performing controlled deviations to achieve long-term task success; such information is particularly relevant when a controller must prepare for transitions between behaviors. We address these challenges using concise motion representations that can be generated by a high-level controller, given a behavior label or text command. These representations can be fed directly into a tracking policy to provide a detailed motion reference. Our method exhibits a high degree of flexibility in performing real-world robot motions such as dancing, fighting, or walking based on conditional information. At matched capacity, our adopted stochastic motion tokenizer reconstructs motions from the Unitree G1 at a level that exceeds state-of-the-art approaches such as finite scalar quantization (FSQ) and the vector-quantized autoencoder (VQ-VAE). The resulting discrete interface conditions whole-body tracking policies that execute generated dance, fight, and walk motions lasting 16.8 s each and a 35 s fight-to-dance sequence on a physical Unitree G1.

</details>


### [159] [SVIn+: Multi-Modal Framework for Robust Underwater State Estimation using 3D Sonar, Visual-Inertial, Water-Pressure, and DVL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36454)
*Chinmay Burgul,Xiaoming Zhao,Yewei Huang,Michalis Chatzispyrou,Marios Xanthidis,Ioannis Rekleitis,Alberto Quattrini Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SVIn+ 是一个面向 AUV 的多传感器鲁棒状态估计框架，在开源视觉-惯性 SLAM（SVIn）基础上将水压传感器与 DVL 紧耦合进非线性优化，并以层次化架构引入 3D 声呐（KISS-ICP 解耦前端输出相对位姿因子）以低成本利用声学结构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下作业规模扩大对可靠自主性需求提升，而状态估计（尤其在低能见度下）是控制、安全导航与建图的基础难题；单一视觉-惯性在退化场景中易漂移，需要多模态融合。

Method: 扩展 SVIn 视觉-惯性 SLAM：将水压（深度）与 DVL 测量以紧耦合方式写入因子图/非线性优化；3D 声呐采用层次化设计，解耦前端用 KISS-ICP 做配准，输出相对位姿因子注入因子图，兼顾声学结构利用与计算效率。

Result: 在沉船与珊瑚礁实地部署验证，报告漂移抑制与全局一致性提升；同时开源代码与数据集。

Conclusion: 多模态紧耦合（视觉+惯性+压力+DVL）加声呐相对位姿因子的层次化融合，可显著提升低能见度水下 AUV 状态估计的鲁棒性与一致性；开源资源利于后续水下多模态融合研究。

Abstract: As expansion and scalability in underwater oper- ations becomes more important and challenging, autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) become more relevant and the necessity for reliable autonomy more critical. A fundamental challenge in any autonomous underwater operation, from control to safe navigation and mapping, is state estimation; a notorious hard problem to address, especially in low visibility conditions. In this paper, we tackle this problem, by presenting SVIn+, a multi-sensor framework for robust state estimation of AUVs equipped with an IMU, cameras, a water-pressure sensor, a Doppler Velocity Log (DVL), and a compact 3D sonar. This framework extends an established open-source state-of-the-art visual-inertial SLAM, SVIn, by integrating water-pressure and DVL measurements into a tightly-coupled nonlinear optimiza- tion formulation resulting in improved estimates. To leverage the structural information of recently available 3D acoustic sonars while maintaining computational efficiency, we introduce a hierarchical architecture that incorporates estimates from a decoupled front-end via KISS-ICP, treated as relative pose factors within the factor graph. The proposed framework is validated through field deployments in a shipwreck and coral reef, demonstrating drift mitigation and global consistency. To support further research in underwater multi-modal fusion, the code and the datasets can be found on project website this https URL this https URL

</details>


### [160] [GRAFT: Geometry-Aware Residual Adaptation for Foundation Trackers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36507)
*Liang Li,Guoyao Zhang,Xingyu Chen,Sikai Wu,Qianyang Wu,Yuhang Lin,Renjing Xu,Mingliang Zhou,Diyun Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRAFT：冻结行为基础模型（BFM），在不动整体参数与运动参考接口的前提下，用轻量适配器对下肢执行目标施加地形条件化残差修正，并用任务+足部几何奖励训练、平地正则化、特权地形蒸馏到机载深度；4个冻结跟踪器仿真验证，Unitree G1真机以机载深度通过台阶/沟壑/平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形行为基础模型（BFM）主要基于平地运动训练，缺乏显式调整落脚点、摆动离地高度与触地时序以适应局部地形的机制；现有地形感知做法需重训或微调跟踪器并耦合感知通路，数据与算力代价高，且使普通跟踪能力与感知深度绑定。

Method: 保持BFM参数与运动参考接口不变，仅在下肢执行目标上接入轻量残差适配器，输出地形条件化修正；用任务奖励与足部几何奖励训练，加入平地正则项抑制多余修正；将特权地形观测蒸馏至机载深度估计，使部署时仅需深度输入。框架实例化于四个冻结跟踪器。

Result: 仿真中在台阶、沟壑、平台三类地形上验证；真机Unitree G1上，经适配的SONIC跟踪器使用机载深度、未修改的运动参考完成地形通过。

Conclusion: 将地形适应从"重训基础模型"重构为"聚焦的下肢执行层残差适配"问题，可在保留BFM复用性与运动参考接口的前提下以低代价获得地形感知能力，具备跨跟踪器的即插即用性与真机可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid behavior foundation models (BFMs) provide reusable whole-body motion priors: given a kinematic reference, they generate coordinated joint commands for motion replay, teleoperation, and VLA control. However, trained primarily on flat-ground motion, BFMs lack an explicit mechanism to adapt foot placement, swing clearance, and contact timing to local terrain. Many terrain-aware BFM approaches address this limitation by retraining or fine-tuning the tracker together with perception, incurring substantial data and computation costs while coupling ordinary tracking to the perception pathway. To bridge this gap, we propose GRAFT, a geometry-aware residual adaptation framework for frozen behavior trackers. GRAFT preserves the BFM parameters and motion-reference interface, while a lightweight adapter adds terrain-conditioned corrections only to lower-body execution targets. The adapter is trained with task and foot-geometry rewards, regularized to suppress unnecessary corrections on flat ground, and distilled from privileged terrain observations to onboard depth. Because the frozen BFM already provides whole-body reference tracking and nominal locomotion, GRAFT reduces terrain learning to a focused execution-adaptation problem. We instantiate the framework on four frozen trackers and evaluate stairs, gaps, and platforms in simulation. We further validate it through real-world deployment on a Unitree G1, where the adapted SONIC tracker traverses terrain with onboard depth and unmodified references.

</details>


### [161] [RoboAuto: Autonomous Robot Program Search through Multi-Level Physical Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36533)
*Buzhao Liu,Jianing Ye,Xiu Yuan,Yangtong Feng,Chengsong Huang,Tyler Dinh,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Ning Zhang,Chongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAuto: 在代码即策略(Code-as-Policy)框架中显式组织对象、场景、交互三层物理理解,通过分层知识库、场景落地与多阶段自设计实验规划提升机器人程序搜索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体通过执行反馈迭代精化机器人程序,但缺乏对可操作性、场景几何约束及交互成败原因的物理理解,物理疑问长期未被解决。

Method: 三组件设计:(1) 分层知识库,将可执行技能与以对象为中心的可供性、空间关系、动作效果知识结合,来源于具身数据与在线任务求解;(2) 场景落地,识别相关对象并解析动作关键量;(3) 带自设计实验的多阶段规划,验证各阶段预期物理效果并主动检验竞争假设以消解物理不确定性。

Result: 在五个基准族上表现优异:LIBERO-PRO 短程任务成功率 89.1%;在 LIBERO-PRO Long 与 LIBERO-Plus 上平均成功率超过 ASPIRE 两倍以上;在三个需精细操作的长程真机任务上优于 ASPIRE 与 π0.5。知识库可跨仿真器与本体迁移,程序搜索性能随知识库规模扩展。

Conclusion: 显式分层物理理解 + 主动实验式假设检验是提升机器人程序搜索与长程精细操作的有效途径,且知识可迁移、可规模化。

Abstract: Recently, LLM-based autonomous agents have shown strong capabilities in iteratively generating, evaluating, and refining executable solutions. In robotic manipulation, Code-as-Policy lets these agents search over modular and editable robot programs. However, effective program search requires physical understanding: agents must understand how objects can be manipulated, how scene geometry constrains actions, and why interactions succeed or fail. Existing methods rely mainly on execution feedback for program refinement, often leaving these physical questions unresolved. We introduce RoboAuto, a framework for robot program search that explicitly organizes physical understanding at the object, scene, and interaction levels through three components. First, a hierarchical knowledge base combines executable skills with object-centric knowledge of affordances, spatial relations, and action effects, learned from embodied data and online task solving. Second, scene grounding connects this knowledge to the current environment by identifying relevant objects and resolving action-critical quantities. Third, multi-stage planning with self-designed experiments checks whether each stage produces its expected physical effects and actively tests competing hypotheses to resolve physical uncertainties. RoboAuto achieves superior performance across five benchmark families, attaining an 89.1% success rate on short-horizon LIBERO-PRO tasks and more than twice the mean success rate of ASPIRE on LIBERO-PRO Long and LIBERO-Plus. It also outperforms ASPIRE and $\pi_{0.5}$ on three challenging long-horizon real-robot tasks requiring precise manipulation. We further show that the knowledge base transfers across simulators and embodiments, and that program search performance scales with its size. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [162] [A generalist policy for muscle-driven control across individual musculoskeletal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36535)
*Bozhao Wang,Yinglei Zhu,Zhengqing Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Weiming Kang,Yiheng He,XinJun Liu,Senhao Hou,Huichan Zhao,Xiaoqiang Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MyoAcross 是一个肌骨形态条件化的控制框架，结合个体化肌肉骨骼模型构建、形态条件策略学习与虚拟模型增广，使肌肉驱动策略无需逐个体微调即可复用于31个未见个体模型，完成率99.24%，跟踪误差较无条件MLP降低22.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同个体的肌肉骨骼形态差异导致控制策略难以跨模型复用，传统做法需为每个个体单独微调，成本高。作者希望建立一个通用的、形态感知的控制框架，支持跨个体的运动比较仿真。

Method: 构建个体化肌肉骨骼模型 + 形态条件（morphology-conditioned）策略学习 + 虚拟模型增广（含非对称增广，覆盖单侧下肢缩短模型）；并与单图体型估计、身高体重构建的个体模型配合实现零微调迁移。

Result: 31个留出个体模型上完成率99.24%、跟踪误差降22.60%；策略可迁移至22个由单张图像形态估计+身高体重构建的个体模型；垂直地面反作用力波形与120名额外受试者实测一致，肌肉激活与EMG呈通道依赖的一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作为跨个体肌肉骨骼模型的运动比较仿真提供了统一控制框架，显著降低个体化微调需求，并具备一定生物力学真实性验证。

Abstract: Differences in musculoskeletal morphology challenge policy reuse across individual models. Here we present MyoAcross, a framework that combines individual musculoskeletal model construction, morphology-conditioned policy learning and virtual model augmentation to enable muscle-driven reference-motion tracking without individual fine-tuning. Across 31 held-out individual models, MyoAcross achieves 99.24% completion and reduces tracking error by 22.60% relative to an unconditioned multilayer perceptron. Policy reuse extends to models of 22 held-out individuals constructed from single-image shape estimates, measured height and body mass, and, with asymmetry augmentation, to models with unilateral lower-limb shortening. Comparisons with measurements from 120 additional individuals show agreement in vertical ground reaction force waveforms and channel-dependent agreement between simulated muscle activations and electromyography. These results establish a common control framework for comparative simulations of movement across individual musculoskeletal models.

</details>


### [163] [A Trainable CPG Framework for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36537)
*Xiyan Liu,Yide Liu,Guoyong Mao,Shaoxing Qu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CPG-ENN：用对称八神经元CPG产生步态节律，预训练腿部共享执行网络直接映射为关节角参考，单一反馈策略调制6个可解释轨迹变量，实现五种步态及任意步态切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步态四足运动通常依赖各步态专用学习控制器或切换专用奖励，难以统一且缺乏可解释性；作者希望用显式神经协调(CPG)加学习式运动实现(ENN)结合，实现单控制器覆盖多步态及无缝切换。

Method: 结构上分两层：对称性约束的8神经元CPG生成步态节律；预训练、腿间共享的ENN将节律信号与轨迹参数映射为关节角参考，避免在线逆运动学；单一反馈策略调制六个物理可解释的轨迹变量以跟踪平面速度与偏航率指令。无步态专用控制器与切换专用奖励，并做跨步态切换、暴露消融及与AllGaits、Walk These Ways的对比。

Result: 仿真中20组有向步态对、源/目标速度各取{0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0} m/s的组合全部切换成功率达99.39%；消融显示即使无切换训练暴露跨步态切换仍高度可行，暴露主要改善步态习得与速度过渡；速度-步态调节精度优于AllGaits和Walk These Ways；Unitree Go2实机验证五种步态及其切换。

Conclusion: 显式CPG节律与学习式执行网络解耦，可让单一反馈策略统一实现多步态运动与平滑切换，兼具可解释性与实机可用性。

Abstract: We present CPG-ENN, a trainable quadruped locomotion framework that combines explicit neural coordination with learned motion realization. A symmetry-based eight-neuron central pattern generator (CPG) supplies gait rhythms, while a pretrained, leg-shared execution neural network (ENN) maps rhythmic signals and trajectory parameters to joint-angle references without online inverse kinematics. A single feedback policy modulates six physically interpretable trajectory variables. The controller tracks planar velocity and yaw-rate commands across walk, trot, pace, bound, and pronk without gait-specific learned controllers or transitionspecific rewards. Simulation yields 99.39% transition success across all 20 directed gait pairs and all source-target speed combinations from {0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0} m/s. Ablations show high cross-gait transition feasibility without transition exposure; exposure mainly refines gait acquisition and velocity transients. Comparisons with AllGaits and Walk These Ways demonstrate accurate gait-speed regulation, and Unitree Go2 experiments validate five gaits and their transitions.

</details>


### [164] [CARD: Contact-Anchored Retargeting for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36539)
*Cunxi Dai,Botao He,Francois R Hogan,Wei Pan,Rao Fu,Wen-Han Hsieh,Ruoshi Liu,Jitendra Malik,Guanya Shi,Akash Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARD：以“接触力目标”为核心的人到灵巧手重定向框架，统一视频/动捕/触觉手套输入，配合触觉跟踪控制器在硬件开环回放下维持接触，并给出视频到接触预测模型 Vid-HACO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据采集（遥操作、动觉示教、外骨骼）迫使人类通过机器人本体动作，压制了人的灵巧性；重定向可规避此问题，但现有方法只迁移运动学而忽略决定任务成败的接触动力学。

Method: 提出 Contact-Anchored Retargeting（CARD）：(1) 用共享的手–接触表示统一视频、动捕与触觉手套三类数据源，除运动学外显式生成接触力目标；(2) 引入触觉跟踪控制器，在硬件开环回放下维持接触目标；(3) 针对无触觉源的视频，提出视频到接触模型 Vid-HACO。

Result: 在视频、手套、动捕数据及 GRAB 数据集上，CARD 在 43–78% 的接触帧上保持接触，而纯运动学方法与物体感知重定向分别 <10% 和 22.5%；将该形式化融入物理仿真重定向后，GRAB 上严格任务成功率由 12% 提升至 32%；硬件回放实验表明触觉跟踪器对复现示范力至关重要。

Conclusion: 显式接触保持可作为将人类灵巧性可扩展地迁移至机器人的接口。

Abstract: Humans are dexterous, but traditional data collection paradigms such as teleoperation, kinesthetic teaching, and wearable exoskeletons force humans to act through the robot embodiment, bottlenecking their dexterity. Retargeting can avoid this. However, existing methods purely translate kinematics and ignore the real-world contact dynamics essential for task success. We present CARD: Contact-Anchored Retargeting for Dexterous Manipulation, a simulator and object-free framework that retargets human demonstrations to robot actions by explicitly preserving contact force targets. CARD unifies human data sources including video, motion capture, and tactile gloves via a shared hand--contact representation and generates explicit contact force targets in addition to kinematics. To maintain these contact targets during hardware open-loop replay, CARD also introduces a tactile tracking controller. For video which does not contain an explicit tactile source, we present Vid-HACO, our video-to-contact model that sets a new state of the art. Across our video, glove, motion-capture data and the GRAB dataset, CARD maintains contact on $43$--$78\%$ of contact frames, while kinematic and object-aware retargeting methods maintain contact on $<10\%$ and $22.5\%$ of the frames respectively. Further, integrating our formulation into physics-based retargeting improves strict task success from 12\% to 32\% on GRAB dataset. Finally, we show that our tactile tracker is critical to reproduce demonstrated forces during hardware replay. These results establish that contact preservation via CARD is a scalable interface for transferring human dexterity to robots.

</details>


### [165] [REACH: An RL-based Hierarchical Humanoid Crawling Framework for Adaptable Confined Space Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36546)
*Shengzhang Xu,Yuning Guo,Weiyi Hu,Yifeng Xu,Zhongyu Li,He Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REACH：基于RL的分层框架，让Unitree G1人形机器人通过变高度双足行走（1.27–0.50m）与爬行（0.50–0.20m）在受限高度空间连续通行；采用历史条件潜在表征处理富接触爬行动力学，并用双向过渡策略衔接蹲行与爬行，配合LiDAR规划器生成无碰撞三维路径与主动高度指令，完成真机实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限空间中的人形运动需跨多种运动模式连续调节身高，而现有方法多局限于高度可调的双足行走，蹲行受刚度与净空下限约束，难以进入更低矮空间。

Method: 分层RL框架：底层为分模式变高度运动策略（双足行走覆盖1.27–0.50m，爬行覆盖0.50–0.20m）；引入历史条件潜在表征从本体感受观测中编码地形与接触交互上下文以应对富接触爬行动力学；设计双向过渡策略跨越蹲行与爬行间的大幅运动学与接触不连续；上层LiDAR规划器将点云转换为无碰撞3D路径与含前瞻高度目标的运动指令。

Result: 真机实验实现了较精确的高度与速度跟踪、鲁棒地形通行、可靠模式切换，并完成完整管线的受限空间穿越，将可通行净空下限延伸至蹲行极限以下。

Conclusion: 多模式变高度运动（行走+爬行）与感知规划的分层整合可显著扩展人形机器人在高度受限环境中的作业范围，历史条件潜在表征与过渡策略是处理模式切换与富接触运动的关键。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion in confined spaces requires continuous height adaptation across multiple locomotion modes, yet most existing approaches remain limited to height-adjustable bipedal walking. We present REACH, an RL-based hierarchical framework that enables a Unitree G1 humanoid to traverse height-constrained environments through variable-height bipedal walking and crawling. The learned policies regulate body height over complementary command ranges of 1.27--0.50m in bipedal locomotion and 0.50--0.20m in crawling, extending traversal beyond the clearance limits of crouched walking. To address contact-rich crawling dynamics, we employ a history-conditioned latent representation that captures terrain and interaction context from proprioceptive observations. A bidirectional transition policy further connects crouched bipedal locomotion and crawling across large kinematic and contact discontinuities. These skills are integrated with a LiDAR-based planner that converts point clouds into collision-free three-dimensional paths and locomotion commands, including proactive height targets. Real-world experiments demonstrate accurate height and velocity tracking, robust terrain traversal, reliable mode transitions and confined-space traversal with the proposed pipeline. Code and videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [166] [OpenJaws: An Open Hardware UMI Framework with Stereo Localization for Generalist Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36598)
*Adithyavairavan Murali,Yash Oza,Karanbir Chahal,Neel Jawale,Silas De Vries,Hesam Rabeti,Erwin Coumans,Balakumar Sundaralingam,Stan Birchfield*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenJaws：全开源硬件+软件的手持UMI数据采集框架，用3D打印夹爪配低成本双目相机恢复度量尺度轨迹，并提出UMI-Loc-Bench-v1基准证明双目定位显著优于单目，策略可迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMI类手持采集设备可在无机器人参与下野外采集操作演示，但动作标签质量受限于视觉定位精度：现有单目VIO存在尺度歧义与漂移，依赖闭源模块或外部基础设施则带来成本与部署负担。

Method: 模块化3D打印夹爪+标准化ISO适配器+低成本双目相机，构建全开源软硬件框架；引入UMI-Loc-Bench-v1量化真实场景定位精度并与单目方案对比；在真实机器人上部署由所采数据训练的策略。

Result: 双目定位效果显著优于单目替代方案；基于OpenJaws采集数据训练的策略可在真实机器人上完成多类任务部署。

Conclusion: 开源、低成本、易部署的传感器化UMI方案可提升手持演示的动作标签保真度，并降低跨机器人平台的数据采集门槛。

Abstract: Handheld data-collection interfaces such as the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) allow manipulation demonstrations to be gathered in the wild without a robot in the loop, but the quality of the resulting action labels is bounded by the accuracy of vision-based localization. Existing systems recover end-effector pose from monocular visual-inertial odometry, which is subject to scale ambiguity and drift, or delegate tracking to closed-source modules and external infrastructure that reintroduce cost and setup burden. We present OpenJaws, a fully open hardware and software framework for sensorized UMI that improves localization and simplifies deployment across robot platforms. OpenJaws pairs a modular, 3D-printed gripper exposing a standardized ISO adapter with a low-cost stereo camera to recover metric camera trajectories. We further introduce UMI-Loc-Bench-v1, a benchmark quantifying real-world localization accuracy for handheld data-collection devices, and show that stereo localization substantially outperforms monocular alternatives. Finally, we demonstrate that policies trained on data collected with OpenJaws can be deployed on a real robot across multiple tasks. Hardware, software, and data are released under a commercially permissive license. Code, data, and videos are available at this https URL

</details>


### [167] [Camera-to-Quadruped Robot Pose Estimation from Sequential Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36599)
*Seobin Hwang,Ji-Seong Beom,Yeong-Jun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QuadPose从单目视频估计四足机器人全身18自由度位姿（6-DoF基座+12-DoF关节），并提出首个相机到四足机器人位姿基准QuadSyn。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在灾害响应、设施巡检等危险环境中依赖外部相机监控，但其关节时变、腿部对称、频繁自遮挡导致关键点对应歧义，且缺乏公开基准无法定量比较。

Method: 单目视频序列输入，统一回归基座位姿与12个腿部关节角，利用结构先验处理腿对称与自遮挡歧义；同时构建含真值标注的QuadSyn合成基准数据集。

Result: QuadSyn上达到92.18% PCK@2.5px、95.54% PCK-AUC、85.94% ADD@20cm、66.98% ADD-AUC，平均关节角误差3.65°，约33 FPS实时推理。

Conclusion: 为外部相机下的四足全身位姿估计提供可行方案与首个标准化评测基准，具备实时性；但验证限于合成数据，真实场景泛化与实机部署证据仍待补充。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are increasingly deployed in hazardous environments such as disaster response and facility inspection, where external-camera monitoring is essential for safe operation. This setting is challenging because quadrupeds are articulated systems with time-varying leg joints, leg symmetry, and frequent self-occlusions, which make keypoint correspondence ambiguous. Moreover, no public benchmark exists, making quantitative comparison difficult. To address these challenges, we propose QuadPose, which estimates the full-body 18-DoF pose (6-DoF base pose + 12-DoF joint angles) from monocular video sequences, and introduce QuadSyn, the first camera-to-quadruped robot pose benchmark. On QuadSyn, QuadPose achieves 92.18% PCK@2.5px, 95.54% PCK-AUC, 85.94% ADD@20cm, 66.98% ADD-AUC, and 3.65 degree mean joint-angle error at approximately 33 FPS.

</details>


### [168] [Mimetic Embodiment Transfer of Human-Humanoid Interaction from In-the-Wild Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36615)
*Nghia Le,Khang Nguyen,Ba Long Tran,Thi Quynh Nhu Pham,Binh Nguyen,Tuong Do,Erman Tjiputra,Hoan Nguyen,Tung D. Ta,Baoru Huang,Hai-Nguyen,Nguyen,Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumiTransfer将野外人人交互视频转换为物理可行的人-人形机器人交互，并构建HumiMimic数据集（约50万帧，覆盖26-DoF Unitree R1与23-DoF Mini-hi）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人与自然交互需保留个体动作及空间、语义、接触关系，且方法需跨不同形态与运动学约束的人形本体泛化。现有动作重定向方法难以处理交互中的身体间关系与物理可行性。

Method: 先重建带一致足-地接触的3D人体运动；再通过交互建模将其中一方迁移至目标人形本体，使用加权关节相对方向与解剖学偏移以优先保持交互关键的身体间关系；最后以post-retargeting优化施加平衡、接触与碰撞避免约束。

Result: 在两款机器人上，运动保真度、身体间关系保持与接触精度优于现有基线；HumiMimic可支撑以人类伙伴为条件的人形交互动作生成；真机部署验证了迁移动作的物理可执行性。

Conclusion: 提出通用的拟人本体交互迁移框架，实现从野外视频到可执行人-人形交互动作的pipeline，并开源风格的大规模交互数据集与人形动作生成训练支撑。

Abstract: Enabling a humanoid to interact naturally with human partners requires preserving not only individual motions but also the spatial, semantic, and contact relationships underlying the interaction (i.e., hugging, dancing, hand-shaking, etc.). Furthermore, a practical solution should generalize across various humanoid embodiments with distinct morphologies and kinematic constraints. To tackle this challenge, we propose HumiTransfer, a mimetic embodiment transfer framework that converts in-the-wild human-human interaction videos into physically plausible human-humanoid interactions. HumiTransfer first reconstructs 3D human motion with consistent foot-ground contact, then transfers one participant to a target humanoid embodiment via interaction modeling, using weighted joint-relative directions and anatomical offsets to prioritize interaction-critical inter-body relationships, while post-retargeting optimization enforces balance, contact, and collision avoidance. Subsequently, we apply HumiTransfer to 1,200 in-the-wild videos, producing approximately 500,000 frames of diverse interactions for the 26-DoF Unitree R1 Edu and the 23-DoF HighTorque Mini-hi humanoid robot, resulting in our HumiMimic dataset. Across both robots, HumiTransfer achieves superior motion fidelity, preserves inter-body relationships, and maintains contact accuracy compared with existing baselines. Moreover, we demonstrate that HumiMimic supports motion generation methods for synthesizing interactive humanoid motions following a human partner. Finally, our real robot deployments confirm the physical executability of the transferred motions in real-world settings.

</details>


### [169] [SWIM: Learning What Matters from Behaviors for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36662)
*Jiaming Hu,Shi Bo,Yan Zheng,Tian Wang,Florian Dubost,Alejandro Mottini,Junze Liu,Arvind Srinivasan,Kai Zhong,Kun Qian,Sharon Gao,Vianne Gao,Houyu Zhang,Qingjun Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWIM通过离线轨迹训练逆动力学模型，学习状态特征对动作预测的贡献权重，构建加权状态度量用于JEPA世界模型的状态监督，提升五类机器人操作任务的规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA类世界模型以预测未来嵌入为目标，未显式纳入任务相关的物理状态信息，导致决定任务进展的状态可能未被可靠编码。用仿真状态直接监督时，又面临特征选择问题：人工挑选或均匀加权会让冗余坐标重复计量同一变化、无关特征淹没任务相关信息。

Method: 提出SWIM（State Weighting with Inverse-Dynamics Model）：在离线轨迹上训练逆动力学模型，由当前状态与观测到的未来位移预测记录的动作，以各状态特征对动作预测的贡献作为权重；将权重用于定义加权状态度量（文中通过对齐潜变量距离与加权状态距离实现状态监督训练）。逆动力学模型仅在训练期使用，规划阶段只需图像输入，无额外计算开销。

Result: 在五个仿真机器人操作任务上，SWIM相较均匀状态加权持续提升规划成功率。

Conclusion: 以动作预测为代理目标可自动学习状态特征重要性，为状态监督式世界模型提供比人工/均匀加权更合理的度量；但验证仅限仿真，缺少真实机器人实验与对权重可解释性、泛化性的进一步分析。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) enable world models to plan from images by predicting future embeddings. However, their visual prediction objectives do not explicitly incorporate task-relevant state information, so the physical state that determines task progress may not be reliably captured in the latent space. Supervising world models with simulator state can make planning more informative, but it requires deciding which state features matter. Features are typically selected by hand or weighted uniformly, and a uniform weighting gives every standardized state feature equal influence, so redundant coordinates can overcount the same variation, while irrelevant ones can obscure task-relevant differences. To address this limitation, we propose SWIM (State Weighting with Inverse-Dynamics Model), which learns state weights from offline trajectories by predicting recorded actions from current states and observed future displacements. The learned weights quantify each feature's contribution to action prediction and define a weighted state metric for state-supervised training, which we demonstrate by aligning latent and state distances. The inverse-dynamics model is used only during training, so the resulting world model plans from images with no additional planning-time computation. Across five simulated robotic manipulation tasks, SWIM consistently improves planning success over uniform state weighting.

</details>


### [170] [From Biological Curves to Soft Modular Robots: Learning-Based Inverse Design with Uncertainty-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36681)
*Dixiang Wang,Minghao Zha,Arifur Rahman,Yinhong Qin,Yitao Jiang,Shiyang Lu,Zebing Mao,Muhao Chen,Luyang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出学习驱动的real-to-sim-to-real框架，结合代理模型辅助的逆向设计（前向代理评估+学习式逆向预测）与不确定性引导细化，将生物曲线高效转换为缆驱动模块化软体机器人构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆驱动模块化软体机器人复现生物形变（如鳗鱼波动、尺蠖拱形）时，逆设计依赖昂贵的物理建模与组合搜索，难以高效复现多样目标形状。

Method: real-to-sim-to-real框架：学习式逆预测给出候选构型，用快速前向代理模型（surrogate）评估候选，并通过不确定性引导的定向候选探索与迭代细化来筛选可行配置。

Result: 前向代理将形变预测时间从1.53×10^5 ms降至0.0008 ms（约1.9×10^8倍加速）；逆设计时间从1.99×10^6 ms降至4.18 ms。在5000个未见穿线模式的测试样本上，基于代理评估的形状重建成功率达93%；并以四个仿生机器人进行了物理演示。

Conclusion: 学习式代理辅助逆设计可大幅降低软体机器人形状复现的计算成本，并在仿真评估与实物演示中验证了可行性。

Abstract: Animals exhibit diverse shape-changing behaviors, from the undulatory swimming of eels to the arching movements of inchworms. Reproducing these biological shapes with modular soft robots offers a promising approach to developing versatile bioinspired robotic systems. However, translating a desired shape into a feasible robotic configuration remains challenging. Existing cable-driven modular soft robots rely on computationally expensive physics-based modeling and combinatorial search for inverse design, making it difficult to efficiently reproduce diverse target shapes. To address this challenge, we present a learning-based real-to-sim-to-real framework that translates biological curves into robotic configurations through surrogate-assisted inverse design and uncertainty-guided refinement. The proposed method combines learned inverse prediction with targeted candidate exploration, using a fast forward surrogate to evaluate and select suitable designs. The learned forward surrogate reduces deformation prediction time from $1.53 \times 10^5$ ms to 0.0008 ms, achieving approximately a $1.9 \times 10^8$-fold speedup. At the same time, the proposed inverse design method reduces design time from $1.99 \times 10^6$ ms to 4.18 ms. On 5,000 test samples generated from previously unseen threading patterns, the method achieves a 93% shape reconstruction success rate under surrogate-based evaluation. Physical demonstrations with four bioinspired robots further illustrate the translation of biological curves into realizable robotic shapes.

</details>


### [171] [RoboSTD: Zero-Shot Single-to-Dual Transfer via Sagittal Mirroring for Bimanual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36685)
*Hongyu Wu,Chong Liu,Shaoxuan Xie,Shaqi Luo,Hanyu Feng,Shihan Wu,Guocai Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSTD：利用矢状面镜像生成对侧伪示范，并用 LLM 生成时空协调约束，实现零样本单臂→双臂数据构建，无需采集双臂示范即可训练双臂策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂示范采集成本高，限制双臂策略学习的可扩展性；单臂数据因动作空间、协调需求与左右臂空间偏好差异（Single-to-Dual gap）难以直接迁移。

Method: 观测、本体感知与动作作矢状面镜像得到可执行的对侧伪示范；再用 LLM 生成结构化时空协调约束，按工作空间区域与任务阶段重建伪双臂监督信号。

Result: 在 AgileX ALOHA 平台的 RoboTwin 仿真与真机实验中验证有效性；无需额外双臂示范，抓杯任务平均收集杯子数为单臂基线的 2.11 倍。

Conclusion: 镜像+LLM约束的数据构建可在零样本条件下缓解双臂数据瓶颈，但方法仅覆盖对称/可镜像任务结构，协调生成依赖语言模型先验，缺少更广泛任务与细粒度操作的验证。

Abstract: Bimanual robot manipulation plays an important role in real-world robotic applications, but the high cost of collecting bimanual demonstrations limits the scalable development of bimanual policy learning. Meanwhile, existing single-arm manipulation data are difficult to directly transfer to bimanual systems due to the Single-to-Dual gap in action spaces, coordination requirements, and arm-side spatial preference. To address this issue, we propose RoboSTD, a zero-shot Single-to-Dual data construction method for bimanual robot learning. RoboSTD first applies sagittal-plane mirroring to observations, proprioception, and actions to construct executable contralateral pseudo-demonstrations. It then uses an LLM to generate structured spatio-temporal coordination constraints that guide the reconstruction of pseudo-bimanual supervision across workspace regions and task stages. We evaluate RoboSTD on the AgileX ALOHA platform in both RoboTwin simulation and real-world settings. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of RoboSTD for bimanual policy learning. Without requiring additional bimanual demonstrations, RoboSTD increases the average number of collected cups to 2.11x that of the single-arm baseline in the cup collection task. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [172] [TOPS: Reshaping Flow Source and Transport for Multi-Task Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36694)
*Tuo An,Chuhao Zhou,Jiahua Dong,Jingliang Li,Jiaqi Bai,Jindou Jia,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TOPS于不改动网络结构的前提下，为语言条件多任务操作中基于流匹配的策略构造任务相关的多模态源先验，并依据源-目标协方差错配自适应调整去噪插值调度，提升多任务成功率和数据/推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配策略在多个任务间共享单一高斯源先验与统一线性传输路径，难以适配不同任务差异化的运动需求，导致共享策略在多任务上可靠性受限。

Method: 1）利用反向映射的专家动作序列构造多模态任务源分布，使语言指令可决定生成起点；2）以源-目标的协方差错配为指导，在去噪早期减速（更贴近源结构）、在需要扩张的方向上后期加速；3）在高斯端点模型下给出速度预测风险上界的理论分析，证明该调度可降低风险；4）方法为即插即用式，可迁移至π0.5等大规模流策略。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus与CALVIN上，分布内、扰动及跨环境设置均取得提升且数据效率更好；单步积分下LIBERO-10成功率由53.44%（十步标准FM）升至69.67%，推理时间约为基线1/6；迁移到π0.5在四个设置上均有一致增益；真机实验中上抽屉打开任务成功率由20%提升至80%。

Conclusion: 任务导向的源先验与插值调度是低成本、可迁移的改进，能在不改变架构的情况下显著提升流式VLA策略的多任务性能与采样效率，并有真机验证支撑。

Abstract: Flow-based policies for language-conditioned multi-task manipulation commonly share a single $\textit{Gaussian}$ source prior and a common linear transport path across tasks. However, reliably executing multiple tasks with distinct motion requirements remains challenging for a shared policy. We introduce $\textbf{TOPS}$ ($\textbf{T}$ask-$\textbf{O}$riented $\textbf{P}$riors and $\textbf{S}$chedules), which adapts the flow source prior and interpolation schedule to each task without modifying the network architecture. TOPS first constructs multimodal task sources from reverse-mapped expert action sequences, allowing language instructions to select where generation begins. Guided by source-target covariance mismatch, it then slows the transition toward expert actions early in denoising and accelerates it later along directions requiring expansion. Under a $\textit{Gaussian}$ endpoint model, our analysis shows that this schedule adjustment can lower an upper bound on velocity-prediction risk. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and CALVIN demonstrate improved performance across in-distribution, perturbation and cross-environment settings, alongside better data efficiency. Using one integration step, TOPS raises LIBERO-10 success from $53.44\%$ to $69.67\%$ compared with ten-step Standard Flow Matching (FM), while reducing inference time to approximately one-sixth of the baseline. TOPS also yields consistent gains when transferred to the large-scale flow-based $\pi_{0.5}$ policy across all four evaluated settings. Real-world experiments further demonstrate improved task success, including an increase from $20\%$ with standard FM to $80\%$ with TOPS on the upper-drawer opening task.

</details>


### [173] [On Shaky Ground: Characterising Non-Rigid Base Effects in Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36725)
*Zongyuan Zhang,Christopher Lehnert,Will N. Browne,Jonathan M. Roberts*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 移动机械臂在轻量化、非刚性基座上的持续接触任务（砂磨）中，基座运动与支撑柔顺会显著削弱力实现（100 N指令仅实现54-70%）并产生最大100 mm的TCP误差；提高基座-地面阻力可改善力实现并抑制工具位移，而基座振动与力跟踪误差无一致关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机械臂拓展了接触任务的工作空间，但轻量平台上基座运动与支撑柔顺会改变实际工件的力与运动；现有工作对"非刚性基座效应在持续接触中如何表现"刻画不足。

Method: 以砂磨为案例，结合探索性仿真与两台轻量化、无驱动轮式测试平台的实验；同步测量接触力、工具运动与基座振动，并改变基座-地面阻力条件。

Result: 低基座-地面阻力下，100 N指令仅实现54-70%，TCP误差达100 mm；接触过程中提高阻力可改善力实现并阻止工具进一步位移；基座振动与力跟踪误差在测试条件下无一致相关性。

Conclusion: 非刚性基座行为会实质影响接触任务执行，说明表征轻量移动操作时需同时考察力、运动与振动三者的联合测量。

Abstract: Mobile robot manipulators extend contact-task capability beyond the workspace of fixed robot installations, but their bases must also sustain the forces generated by the task. On lightweight platforms, base motion and support compliance can change the force and motion realised at the workpiece. How these non-rigid base effects manifest during sustained contact remains insufficiently characterised. This paper investigates non-rigid base effects in mobile robot manipulators through a sanding case study combining exploratory simulation with experiments on two lightweight, unactuated wheeled testbeds. Joint measurements of contact force, tool motion, and base vibration show that sustained contact coexisted with substantial task error: under low base-ground resistance, only 54-70% of a 100 N command was realised, with tool-centre-point errors reaching 100 mm. Increasing base-ground resistance during contact improved force realisation and arrested further tool displacement, while base vibration did not vary consistently with force-tracking error in the tested conditions. These observations illustrate how non-rigid base behaviour can affect contact-task execution and highlight the value of examining force, motion, and vibration together when characterising lightweight mobile manipulation.

</details>


### [174] [Learning Side-by-Side Navigation for Personal Mobility Vehicles from Human Drivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36726)
*Luis Yoichi Morales,Florent Ferreri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一对个人移动载具(PMV)用解耦的采样MPC+博弈式效用矩阵协同保持并排社交队形，行为参数由IRL从人驾数据拟合，含全身制动安全层，仿真中队形保持率显著高于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让两辆个人移动载具并排行驶以便乘客交谈，同时保证安全平滑，并希望行为风格从人类驾驶数据中学习而非手工设定。

Method: 每车独立运行采样式模型预测规划器；规划器仅交换各自可观测的对方里程计信息，用候选轨迹对的效用矩阵表达联合决策并由共同规则裁决；行为参数通过IRL从10对驾驶员409分钟数据拟合；可行车道由走廊几何离线推导；安全层基于全身几何的可制动距离，不可覆盖；卡住则沿自身路径倒车。

Result: 24+24次闭环仿真无碰撞；障碍场景队形保持率60.5%/68.9%，空旷通道81.2%/91.5%，人类为34.0%/36.0%与52.9%/56.0%；速度与人类相当，横向队形单一且方差小（人类在近对角与宽并排间切换）。

Conclusion: 仅靠里程计级信息交换的分布式规划即可实现稳定社交队形，IRL参数化风格更一致且显著优于人类，但验证仅在差速驱动ROS 2仿真中完成，缺少真实平台与真人交互实验。

Abstract: This paper presents an approach to achieve side-by-side autonomous motion with a pair of personal mobility vehicles (PMVs). Given a global path, two PMVs plan cooperatively so as to hold a social formation that lets the passengers hold a conversation, while keeping the ride safe and smooth. Each vehicle runs its own sampling-based model-predictive planner; the planners exchange nothing but the odometry each vehicle could observe of the other, and the joint decision is expressed as a pair of utility matrices over pairs of candidate trajectories, resolved by a common rule. The behavioural parameters are fitted by inverse reinforcement learning from 409 minutes of driving by ten pairs of people in human-driven PMVs in an indoor environment, and the lanes the pair can use are derived offline from the corridor itself rather than fixed by hand. A safety layer the cost cannot override executes a move only if the vehicle can still brake clear, measured from its whole body, and a vehicle that is stuck reverses along its own path and continues. In closed-loop simulation over 24 runs in the conditions the drivers were recorded in and 24 in four generated obstacle layouts, no vehicle entered a wall or a cone. The pair holds conversational formation for 60.5 and 68.9 percent of the time among obstacles (anticlockwise and clockwise) and 81.2 and 91.5 percent in a clear aisle, against the drivers' 34.0 and 36.0 percent, and 52.9 and 56.0 percent, and it matches their speed. It keeps one lateral formation where the drivers alternated between a close diagonal and a wide abreast one, and it varies far less. Experimental results are based on a differential drive kinematic simulator running on ROS 2.

</details>


### [175] [Making Self-Driving Laboratories Auditable:A Unified Framework for Hybrid Actions,Measurements, and Safe Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36761)
*Zihan Zhang,Zeyu Wu,Xin Zhang,Mingyu Luo,Haohui Que,Tong Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可追溯的自主实验室（SDL）执行框架，含LAM（测量条件化的VLA式策略，同时输出连续机器人轨迹与类型化仪器API调用）、OpenSDL（执行底座，角色级操作映射到异构设备并记录带时间戳的因果事件）、Safe-SDL（派发前schema/资源校验+运行时STL监控）、L3-Bench（回合级评测协议，覆盖物理结果、时序、可复现性、安全违规与故障恢复）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDL需将实验规划、自动化执行与测量反馈闭环耦合，但柔性机器人控制与仪器暴露的类型化API、连续测量之间缺乏统一的执行接口，且异构设备协调与全链路可追溯性难以保证。

Method: 设计四组件框架：LAM将视觉-语言-动作范式扩展为测量条件化策略，联合生成连续轨迹与类型化API调用；OpenSDL作为执行基质完成角色级到设备的映射、协调与因果事件时序记录；Safe-SDL以派发前schema与资源检查结合含长等待段的信号时序逻辑（STL）在线监控构成运行门；L3-Bench定义回合级评测协议，度量物理结果、时序、可复现性、安全违规与故障恢复。

Result: 摘要为设计性/立场性描述，提出端到端设计以联结混合动作生成、受监控的设备执行、测量反馈与实验评测，未给出定量实验结果、数据集规模或部署验证。

Conclusion: 该工作以“可追溯性”为主线，将混合动作生成、受监控执行与评测协议统一，为SDL工作流的协调与可追溯难题提供了概念框架，但尚缺实证支撑，属框架/基准提案类工作。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) are laboratory systems that couple experimental planning, automated execution, and measurement feedback in a closed loop. Integrating flexible robot control with the typed application programming interfaces (APIs) and continuous measurements exposed by laboratory instruments remains an execution-interface challenge. To address this challenge, we propose a traceable framework comprising four components: LAM (Lab Action Model), OpenSDL, Safe-SDL, and L3-Bench.
