LAM is a proposed measurement-conditioned policy architecture that extends vision-language-action paradigms to generate both continuous robot trajectories and typed instrument API calls. OpenSDL is an execution substrate with an initial control implementation, designed to map role-level operations to heterogeneous devices, coordinate execution, and record timestamped causal events. The proposed Safe-SDL runtime gate combines pre-dispatch schema and resource checks with signal temporal logic monitoring during execution, including extended waiting periods. L3-Bench (Level-3 Laboratory Benchmark) specifies an episode-level evaluation protocol covering physical outcomes, timing, reproducibility, safety violations, and fault recovery. Together, these components provide an end-to-end design for linking hybrid action generation, monitored device execution, measurement feedback, and experimental evaluation, addressing coordination and traceability challenges in SDL workflows.

</details>


### [176] [A fully articulated humanoid dexterous hand with human-level joint structure enabled by linkage-based actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36789)
*Jihao Li,Jianguo Wang,Haotian Guo,Yanzhe Wang,Guodong Lu,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Torch-Hand：21自由度全连杆传动仿人灵巧手，含5自由度差异化拇指，掌内串联式布局实现运动学直映射，兼顾抓取力、负载与控制简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手在「仿生运动学复杂度—机械鲁棒性—负载能力—控制简洁性」之间存在固有折衷；腱驱动方案存在磨损、耦合与标定困难，难以支撑非结构化环境中的灵巧操作。

Method: 全关节式21自由度设计（拇指5 DOF含多轴CMC运动，其余四指各4 DOF）；采用纯连杆传动（螺旋连杆机构）替代腱驱动/混合驱动；所有执行器内置于掌部，按类串联机器人构型排布，使电机运动与关节运动学一一对应；通过机械解耦实现功能DOF，同时在复杂操作中可主动协调为耦合多关节运动模式。

Result: 实验覆盖可达工作空间、协同指尖轨迹、拇指对掌能力、33种抓取分类覆盖率、抓取力、负载能力及任务级操作测试；结果表明在紧凑仿人形态下同时获得较高抓取力与负载容量。

Conclusion: Torch-Hand以连杆传动与掌内串联驱动架构，在仿生形态、结构强度与控制可解性之间取得较好平衡，可作为灵巧具身操作的硬件基础平台。

Abstract: Humanoid dexterous hands are a core component of embodied intelligence, enabling robots to translate high-level intent into rich and adaptive physical interaction. However, existing designs remain constrained by a persistent trade-off among anthropomorphic kinematic complexity, mechanical robustness, load capacity and control simplicity, limiting dexterous manipulation in unstructured environments. Here we present Torch-Hand, a fully articulated humanoid dexterous hand that replicates the joint structure and motion hierarchy of the human hand with 21 degrees of freedom (DOF), comprising a highly articulated five-DOF thumb and four DOF in each of the remaining fingers. The differentiated thumb integrates multi-axis CMC motion to provide broad opposition and flexible contact orientation relative to the four fingers. Unlike tendon-driven or hybrid actuation approaches, Torch-Hand adopts an entirely linkage-based transmission architecture. By integrating screw-linkage actuation, the hand achieves high grasping force and substantial payload capacity while maintaining a compact anthropomorphic form. Its actuation architecture is optimized for control tractability, with all actuators integrated within the palm and arranged in a serial-robot-like configuration that establishes a direct correspondence between motor motion and joint kinematics. This architecture mechanically decouples functional DOF to reduce unintended inter-joint motion propagation, while allowing independently addressable DOF to be actively coordinated into coupled multi-joint motion patterns during complex manipulation. Comprehensive experiments assess reachable workspace, coordinated fingertip trajectories, thumb opposition, 33-grasp taxonomy coverage, grasping force, payload capacity and task-level manipulation. These results establish Torch-Hand as a robust hardware foundation for dexterous embodied manipulation.

</details>


### [177] [RynnWorld-Latent: A Cross-Embodiment World Model Conditioned on Universal Latent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36791)
*Haoyu Zhao,Yinzhou Tang,Zixuan Wang,Minghao Zhu,Dongchi Huang,Xingyue Zhao,Qin Zhao,Kehan Li,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Zhenyu Zeng,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnWorld-Latent：以3D-grounded latent action为跨具身共享条件接口的视频世界模型，联合预训练人类/机器人/仿真数据（23数据集、约5.535亿帧），支持遥操作与latent action合成两条数据生成路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人具身动作空间互不兼容，人类视频缺少原生控制标签，导致异构交互视频难以联合学习可控世界动态。

Method: 基于RynnLAM的3D-grounded latent action作为统一条件表示；在预训练视频Transformer上叠加全局action token与identity初始化的局部分支，使动作与未来视频latent对齐；大规模混合预训练后做后训练适配目标机器人；两条数据生成路径：数字遥操作生成动作-视频示范，latent action合成将源动作迁移至编辑后初始帧并配以重定向伪机器人动作。

Result: 公开基准与两个真机平台视频预测指标提升；相同初始观测下不同动作序列呈现可区分的动作跟随；合成mid-training提升LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus策略成功率；两条生成路线在评测设置下均优于纯真机示范训练。

Conclusion: universal latent action可作为跨异构具身学习可控视觉动态的可扩展接口。

Abstract: Learning controllable world dynamics from heterogeneous interaction videos is challenging because robot embodiments use incompatible action spaces, while human videos lack native control labels. We present RynnWorld-Latent, a cross-embodiment video world model that uses the 3D-grounded latent actions introduced by RynnLAM as a shared conditioning interface. This representation enables joint pretraining on human interaction videos, robot demonstrations, and simulation rollouts without requiring a common native action space. Built on a pretrained video transformer, RynnWorld-Latent combines global action tokens with an identity-initialized local pathway that aligns actions with future video latents. We pretrain on 23 datasets comprising approximately 553.5M frames and adapt the model to target robots through post-training. The framework supports two data-generation routes: digital teleoperation uses target robot commands to generate action-video demonstrations, while latent-action synthesis transfers source motion to an edited initial frame and pairs the resulting rollout with retargeted pseudo robot actions. Experiments show improved video prediction metrics on public benchmarks and two real-robot platforms, alongside qualitative action following when different action sequences share the same initial observation. Synthetic mid-training improves policy success rates on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, and both generation routes improve real-world policy success rates over real-demonstration-only training in the evaluated settings. These results support universal latent actions as a scalable interface for learning controllable visual dynamics across heterogeneous embodiments.

</details>


### [178] [Gradient flow of magnetic inclination and intensity as a GPS-free guidance mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36812)
*Zayed Herma,Philippe Cardin,Andréa Vassilev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将仿生地磁导航(BIMN)建模为梯度流控制与制导问题，利用磁倾角与磁感应强度构成的人工势场引导系统经中间航点到达目的地，实现无GPS导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星导航系统脆弱，而海龟等动物可利用地磁进行长距离导航，启发发展低成本的仿生地磁导航；但地磁坐标与地理坐标非一一对应，存在磁异常歧义，使BIMN本质困难。

Method: 在预定区域内构造由磁倾角与磁感应强度定义的目标函数，将其梯度流视为"携带"系统的洋流式速度场，通过梯度流控制/制导实现航点约束下的路径规划与闭环导航。

Result: 论证在预定义区域内磁倾角与强度足以提供导航至任意目的地所需信息，梯度流可作为可控"电流"将系统经中间航点引导至目标。

Conclusion: 提出无GPS导航框架，工程上可用于构建地磁导航基础设施并执行预设航线，生物学上可分析依赖磁感受的动物迁徙路径。

Abstract: It is well established that some animals, such as sea turtles, sense the Earth's magnetic field and use it as a primary cue for long-distance navigation across the vast and featureless ocean. This remarkable ability has inspired the development of bio-inspired magnetic navigation (BIMN), which seeks to mimic these animals to develop robust and frugal navigation methods. BIMN exploits the Earth's magnetic field, sometimes described as ''Nature's GPS'', as a natural navigation infrastructure, providing a potential alternative to vulnerable satellite navigation systems. However, unlike geographic coordinates, magnetic coordinates extracted from the Earth's magnetic field do not provide a one-to-one mapping to geographic locations. Different locations can exhibit similar magnetic signatures, creating ambiguity and making BIMN inherently more challenging. Despite this fundamental challenge, we demonstrate that, within predefined regions of the Earth, magnetic inclination and intensity provide sufficient information to navigate to any destination. By formulating BIMN as a gradient flow control and guidance problem, we show that the resulting gradient flow act as current that carries the system and can be controlled to guide it through intermediate waypoints toward the desired destination. The resulting framework provides a GPS-free navigation mechanism with potential applications in both engineering and biology. In engineering, it can be used to establish a geomagnetic navigation infrastructure through which prescribed routes can be defined and followed, while in biology, it can be used to analyse the migration routes of animals known to rely on magnetoreception.

</details>


### [179] [Integrated Design and Reproducible Feasibility Analysis of a Four-Wheel Rover with Packet-Commanded Imaging, LiDAR Ranging, and Learning-Assisted Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36816)
*Sabik Bin Sultan,Shafi Bin Sultan,Safwan Sadad,Aranya Abeer Khan Prapya,Maria Sultan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向2027 NASA人类探索漫游车挑战赛高中遥控组，构建可复现的四轮教学漫游车集成设计评估：商用差速驱动+独立悬挂轮架+双电池+Raspberry Pi视觉测距子系统，量化质量、车速、爬坡扭矩与传感器几何约束，并用合成基准与mock硬件验证软件链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育类比赛（NASA Rover Challenge 高中组）参赛设计多依赖粗略经验，缺乏把部件选型、失效机理与定量设计限值（8 km/h 速度上限、爬坡扭矩、感知分辨率）联系起来、且可复现的评估流程。

Method: 以CAD尺寸图与BOM对齐，推导运动学与电气约束：10.919 kg 质量、215 mm 轮径、200 RPM 得理论车速 8.105 km/h；20° 定常爬坡在滚动阻力系数 0.10 与等扭矩分配假设下需≥1.255 N·m/轮；50 ft 处 12 英寸特征在全分辨率 Camera Module 3 Standard 图像中约 71 像素，1° 指向误差导致测距射线偏移约 266 mm。软件侧：在 2,400 张合成板裁剪图上以场景不交叉划分，对比颜色-几何规则与两种线性SVM；另用mock硬件检查参考指令执行器。

Result: 分析显示电机铭牌额定值不足以证明满足 8 km/h 上限；给出爬坡扭矩下限、感知分辨率与指向误差间的定量权衡；合成基准中规则法与线性SVM分类器完成对比（摘要未给出具体准确率数值），指令执行链在mock环境下通过检查。无实车试验数据。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是建立部件选型—失效机理—定量设计限值—可复现验证任务之间的可追溯链条，并给出可测试的后续开发计划；局限在于仅有解析与合成/模拟验证，缺少物理样机实测。

Abstract: This study develops an integrated, reproducible design assessment for a four-wheel educational rover intended for the high-school remote-control division of the 2027 NASA Human Exploration Rover Challenge. The architecture combines commercial differential drive control, independently sprung wheel carriers, separate drive and task batteries, and a Raspberry Pi-based subsystem that executes received imaging and ranging commands. The analysis reconciles component lists with dimensioned computer-aided design views, derives mobility and electrical constraints, and connects sensor geometry to command execution. At the estimated mass of 10.919 kg, a 215 mm wheel rotating at 200 RPM produces a theoretical rolling speed of 8.105 km/h; consequently, the nominal motor label does not establish compliance with an 8 km/h design ceiling. A steady 20-degree climb requires at least 1.255 newton-meters per wheel under an assumed rolling-resistance coefficient of 0.10 and equal torque sharing. At 50 ft, an ideal 12-inch feature spans approximately 71 pixels across a full-resolution Camera Module 3 Standard image, while a 1-degree pointing error displaces the ranging ray by approximately 266 mm. An executed synthetic benchmark compares color-geometry rules and two linear support-vector classifiers on 2,400 generated board crops with scene-disjoint evaluation. A separate reference command executor is checked using mocked hardware. These software results and analytical sensitivities support a testable development plan; the study does not report physical rover trials. The principal contribution is a traceable connection between component choices, failure mechanisms, quantitative design limits, and reproducible verification tasks.

</details>


### [180] [TAPESTRY: A Single Backbone for Video Generation and Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36823)
*Jeremy A. Collins,Seungjae Lee,Rachanon Wachakorn,Krishnan Srinivasan,Animesh Garg,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Paarth Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAPESTRY 将机器人动作编码为视频扩散 Transformer 隐空间中的单 patch token，与视频共享同一 patchify 层和主干，用三种扩散目标混合训练，推理时单步 DDIM 直接输出动作块，无需生成视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频生成模型蕴含丰富的物理交互先验，但用于机器人控制时要么在测试时做昂贵的视频生成，要么从头训练动作头、未充分利用预训练主干。

Method: 把每个动作时间步经轻量线性投影映射到预训练 VAE 的 patch 维度，作为单 patch token 与视频 token 共用 patchify 与 Transformer 主干。每个 minibatch 元素随机采样三种扩散目标之一：视频扩散（机器人+人类视频的 VAE 隐变量，注入动力学先验）、视频+动作联合扩散（耦合扩散时间步 + 非对称注意力掩码）、动作单独扩散（以干净图像隐变量为条件的动作去噪）。

Result: 测试时从纯噪声经单次 DDIM 步即可生成紧凑动作 token 序列，在消费级硬件上每动作块 84 ms，输出平滑动作块；仿真基准与真机实验中性能强劲，推理成本显著低于同类联合视频-动作策略。

Conclusion: 共享主干、单步动作生成的"世界-动作模型"范式可在不生成视频的前提下复用视频预训练先验，兼顾真机表现与推理效率。

Abstract: Pretrained video generation models encode rich priors about physical interactions, but leveraging them for robot control typically requires either resource-intensive video generation at test time or action heads trained from scratch that do not fully utilize the pretrained backbone. We introduce Tokenizing Actions as Video Patches in a Shared Transformer Policy (TAPESTRY), a world action model that repurposes a pretrained video diffusion transformer into a robotic policy without requiring video generation at inference. Our key idea is to treat robot actions as single-patch tokens in the video model's latent space. A lightweight linear projection maps each action timestep to the patch dimensions of the pretrained VAE, and the resulting tokens are processed by the same patchifying layers and transformer backbone used for video. The shared backbone is optimized through three diffusion objectives sampled per minibatch element: Video diffusion uses VAE latents from robot and human videos to establish dynamics priors. Joint video+action diffusion trains on robot demonstrations using a coupled diffusion timestep and an asymmetric attention mask. Action-only diffusion denoises actions conditioned on clean image latents. At test time, the model generates a compact action token sequence in a single DDIM step from pure noise, producing smooth action chunks at 84 ms per action chunk on consumer hardware and avoiding the cost of full video generation. Across simulation benchmarks and real-robot experiments, TAPESTRY achieves strong performance with substantially lower inference cost than comparable joint video--action policies. More information can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [181] [HC2Robot: Hyper-Connection Transformers with Predicted Foresight for Multi-Terrain Humanoid Parkour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36825)
*Wenhao Xu,Yanheng Mai,Yifei Fu,Nan Zheng,Yanbo Zhou,Zirui Huang,Mingsheng Ling,Xinjue Wang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HC2Robot：用可部署的"预测前瞻模块"(PFM)从机载感知预测每只脚下一次落足的时刻、位置与支撑质量，加超连接Transformer提升时序推理，实现感知式人形机器人跑酷，仿真平均通过率97.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称actor-critic中critic依赖特权仿真信号，actor仅靠机载感知，存在训练-部署信息鸿沟。作者主张关键不是把特权信息喂给actor，而是让actor学习其可部署的近似表示。

Method: PFM：从可部署观测预测策略条件下的下一次落足事件（时间、位置、支撑质量），将特权监督转化为可部署潜变量；HC Transformer用可学习多流路由增强时序推理；未来运动预测头提供短时运动先验作正则。整体为感知式人形跑酷策略框架。

Result: 在楼梯、沟壑、高台、斜坡上仿真平均通过率97.60%，安全落足率持续优于强感知式运动基线。

Conclusion: 以"预测特权信息替代特权输入"弥合信息鸿沟，是感知式人形全身运动控制的有效思路；但证据仅为仿真，缺真机部署验证。

Abstract: Humanoid parkour requires robots to reason about future footholds and whole-body motions over highly discontinuous terrain. We revisit the information asymmetry in asymmetric actor-critic learning for perceptive humanoid locomotion: the critic exploits privileged simulator signals during training, whereas the deployable actor operates solely from onboard perception. We argue that the key is not exposing privileged information to the actor, but learning a deployable approximation of it to narrow the information gap between training and deployment. We present HC2Robot, a deployable perceptive humanoid parkour framework consisting of a Predicted Foresight Module (PFM) and a Hyper-Connection (HC) Transformer. PFM predicts from deployable observations the policy-conditioned timing, location, and support quality of each foot's next touchdown event, converting privileged supervision into a deployable latent representation that provides the actor with predicted touchdown information without privileged runtime sensing. The HC Transformer further enhances temporal reasoning through learnable multi-stream routing, while a future motion prediction head provides short-horizon motion priors for regularization. Across stairs, gaps, high platforms, and slopes, HC2Robot achieves a mean simulated traversal success rate of 97.60% and consistently higher safe foothold rates than strong perceptive locomotion baselines, supporting improved traversal performance and geometric foothold support under deployable sensing.

</details>


### [182] [ExecDiffuser: Closing the Sample-to-Execution Gap in Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36837)
*Shuo Pei,Bosun Liang,Saihui Zha,Chen Sun,Huachun Tan,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ExecDiffuser，一个递归式的"提案到执行"框架，通过共享执行语义将候选生成、轨迹选择与重规划耦合；在nuPlan/InterPlan/NAVSIM上取得领先闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式规划器通常以能生成多少轨迹来评价，但闭环驾驶真正依赖的是被提交控制的唯一轨迹，二者存在"样本-执行鸿沟"（sample-to-execution gap），单纯提升提案质量反而可能使执行决策变差。

Method: 两模块：(1) Intent-Guided Diffusion Proposal（IGD）为各假设分配责任，并用执行条件监督塑形轨迹；(2) Execution-Grounded Selection（EGS）学习候选条件化的发布效用（candidate-conditioned publication utility），提交单条轨迹至控制，并将其作为下一规划周期的上下文，形成递归闭环。

Result: 在nuPlan、InterPlan、NAVSIM上均优于强SOTA；统一五种子协议平均分85.62，较Diffusion Planner与FlowDrive分别提升4.02与1.31分；NAVSIM官方流程下PDMS达92.6。反事实分析显示其更可靠地将强候选转为已承诺决策，前缀干预验证该优势在执行与重规划后仍保持。

Conclusion: 执行语义对齐与选择器设计是弥合样本-执行鸿沟的关键；闭环性能提升来自提案-选择-重规划的联合优化而非单纯更好的生成。

Abstract: Generative planners are often evaluated by the trajectories they can generate, although closed-loop driving ultimately depends on the single candidate committed to control. We identify this mismatch as the sample-to-execution gap and show that improving proposal quality alone can worsen the executed decision. Here, we introduce ExecDiffuser, a recurrent proposal-to-execution framework that couples candidate generation, trajectory selection, and subsequent replanning through shared execution semantics. Intent-Guided Diffusion Proposal (IGD) assigns hypothesis responsibility and shapes trajectories with execution-conditioned supervision. Execution-Grounded Selection (EGS) learns a candidate-conditioned publication utility, commits one trajectory to control, and returns it as context for the next planning cycle. Across nuPlan, InterPlan, and NAVSIM, ExecDiffuser achieves leading closed-loop performance against strong state-of-the-art baselines. Under a unified five-seed protocol, it obtains an average score of 85.62, improving over Diffusion Planner and FlowDrive by 4.02 and 1.31 points, respectively. On NAVSIM, it achieves 92.6 PDMS under the official evaluation pipeline. Matched counterfactual analysis further shows that ExecDiffuser more reliably converts strong candidates into committed decisions, while prefix interventions confirm that this advantage persists after physical execution and subsequent replanning.

</details>


### [183] [Trace-WAM: Learning to Act via 3D Motion Prediction for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36840)
*Xu Hu,Changjie Wu,Linzhe Shi,Hang Zhang,Chen Yang,Lingjun Zhang,Guowen Zhang,Mu Xu,Junran Peng,Qing Li,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Trace-WAM在视频-动作学习框架中引入未来3D运动预测作为辅助目标，通过三分支MoTransformer联合学习视频预测、运动预测和动作生成，提升有限示教与分布偏移下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操纵成功主要取决于机器人与任务相关物体的演化，而未来视频预测还需重建整幅场景的外观与背景，监督信号存在冗余与失焦，因此需要更直接刻画交互动力学的辅助表征学习目标。

Method: 提出Tri-MoT三分支架构：视频预测专家、运动专家、动作专家。运动专家从当前视觉特征预测未来3D轨迹，监督时间点对应、像平面位移与深度变化，使运动监督塑造动作生成所用表征；动作专家不消费预测轨迹，推理保持单次视觉前向+动作去噪的当前观测策略路径。

Result: 在RoboCasa-GR1、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus上取得强分布内性能与分布外泛化，在示教数据有限与分布偏移条件下相对Fast-WAM提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 以3D运动预测作为辅助目标可在不增加推理开销的前提下改进WAM表征质量，为世界模型与VLA结合提供轻量且有效的训练范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) learn representations for robot control by coupling future visual prediction with action generation. Successful manipulation depends primarily on how the robot and task-relevant objects evolve, whereas future-video prediction must also reconstruct the appearance and background of the entire scene. We therefore propose Trace-WAM, which augments video-action learning with future 3D motion prediction as an auxiliary objective. This objective supervises temporal point correspondence together with image-plane displacement and depth change, directly describing the interaction dynamics and spatial changes involved in manipulation. To this end, we adopt a three-branch Mixture-of-Transformer (Tri-MoT) architecture to jointly learn video prediction, motion prediction, and action generation. The motion expert predicts future trajectories from current visual features, allowing motion supervision to shape the representations used for action generation. Because the action expert does not consume predicted trajectories, inference retains the current-observation policy path: a single visual forward pass followed by action denoising. Experiments on RoboCasa-GR1, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate strong in-distribution performance and out-of-distribution generalization, with particularly clear gains over Fast-WAM under limited demonstrations and distribution shifts.

</details>


### [184] [When Black-Box Robot Policies Fail: Online Sensor Diagnosis and Repair with a Transparent Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36852)
*Marc Ziegler,Batu Özen,Sami Mentes,Thomas Waschulzik,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用一个可解释的结构化代理模型（分解周期步态与传感器相关修正）在冻结的Go2运动策略外部进行离线诊断与在线输入修复，离线将行走成功率从259/640提升至429/640，在线达382/640，但对照组boosted-tree代理无修复收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型常依赖可能失效的传感器，而策略被冻结或不可访问时，缺乏无需内部信息的输入诊断与修复手段。作者检验「外部代理模型可解释输入-动作依赖」这一思路是否真能支撑修复，还是仅是一种事后归因。

Method: 在仿真Go2上，为冻结运动策略拟合结构化surrogate：将动作分解为周期步态项与传感器依赖的修正项（相位感知）。据此做离线诊断（定位受污染传感器通道）与在线输入修复，并与匹配的boosted-tree surrogate（预测精度相近但无结构分解）作消融对照；另在Go1与ANYmal C上验证跨具身可迁移性。

Result: 离线诊断使行走成功从259/640升至429/640，在线修复为382/640；64个无故障留出回合无误触发。匹配的boosted-tree代理预测干净动作效果相近，但相对不修复无显著提升；Go1与ANYmal C上独立拟合亦有改善，相位感知梯度效果更佳。

Conclusion: 输入修复的增益关键来自结构先验（周期步态+传感器修正分解）而非代理精度本身；但仅验证单一策略、部分筛选的仿真故障集，未涉及机器人基础模型与真机，结论外部效度有限。

Abstract: Robot foundation models may generalize across tasks and embodiments, but their actions still depend on sensors that can fail. When the policy is frozen or inaccessible, can an external monitor diagnose and repair corrupted inputs without access to its internals? We test a structured surrogate that separates periodic gait from sensor-dependent corrections to guide input repair around a frozen locomotion policy. On simulated Go2, offline diagnosis raises walking success from 259/640 to 429/640 episodes; online repair reaches 382/640, with no triggers in 64 held-out fault-free episodes. A matched boosted-tree surrogate predicts clean actions similarly well but yields no detected repair improvement over no repair. Separate fits improve walking on Go1 and ANYmal C, although phase-aware gradients perform better. Evidence remains limited to one policy and a partly screened simulated fault suite; robot foundation model and hardware validation remain open.

</details>


### [185] [ActiveAlign: Aligning Egocentric Manipulation via Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36859)
*Ri-Zhao Qiu,Bowen Wen,Yifeng Jiang,Chen Tessler,Eric Heiden,Xue Bin Peng,Xiaolong Wang,Stan Birchfield,Erwin Coumans*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ActiveAlign 框架，用"主动感知"（挥手动作做手眼标定 + 触觉反馈做物体对齐）弥合 Real2Sim2Real 的间隙，并在 Unitree G1 真机上实现毫米级精细操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Real2Sim2Real 多依赖被动感知，观测不完整导致 real2sim 间隙（几何/位姿误差）；人类通过动作主动感知环境，故应引入主动交互来对齐仿真与真实。

Method: 两个互补模块：(1) ActiveAlign-Waving——利用主动手部挥动运动结合可微渲染完成手眼标定，无需标定板；(2) ActiveAlign-Touching——利用触觉反馈迭代精化物体位姿对齐，达到毫米级精度。二者联合实现较强的 real2sim 对齐，并可用于为开源未标定机器人操作数据集自动整备仿真环境。

Result: ActiveAlign-Waving 可用于为开源未标定操作数据集构建仿真环境；真机 Unitree G1 上实现按钮按压、滑块推动等毫米级物体精细操作。

Conclusion: 主动感知（运动+触觉）是弥合 Real2Sim2Real 间隙的有效手段，标定与触觉对齐结合可支撑人形机器人毫米级接触丰富操作；但实验规模偏小、定量指标与开源情况不明确。

Abstract: Real-to-Sim-to-Real (Real2Sim2Real) enables structured world representations for planning, interpretability, and safe execution. Most existing approaches rely on passive perception, which creates real2sim gaps due to imperfect observation. Inspired by how humans perceive actively through actions, we propose ActiveAlign, a framework that leverages active perception to align the Real2Sim2Real gap from interactions. ActiveAlign introduces two complementary modules: (i) ActiveAlign-Waving, which performs hand-eye calibration by exploiting active hand motions and differentiable rendering, and (ii) ActiveAlign-Touching, which refines object alignment through tactile feedback to achieve millimeter-level precision. Together, these capabilities enable strong real2sim alignment. We demonstrate that ActiveAlign-Waving can be used to curate simulation environments for open-sourced uncalibrated robot manipulation datasets, and demonstrate on a real Unitree G1 robot that ActiveAlign enables fine manipulation of millimeter-scale objects such as pressing buttons and moving sliders.

</details>


### [186] [Keeping Contact: Task-Adaptive Friction Margins for Bimanual Non-Prehensile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36961)
*Qian Ying Lee,Lin Yang,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用"带符号摩擦距离"度量力空间中的摩擦裕度，并从双臂倾斜任务中平衡枢轴力矩偏差所需的力修正推导任务自适应裕度，嵌入一系列SOCP求解全局最优接触力，实现非抓握双臂倾斜中维持接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统摩擦锥可行性判据无法区分"刚好在锥内"与"深在锥内"的力，二者抗滑移与抗接触丢失的裕度差异显著；固定裕度或零裕度也难以适配任务需求变化。

Method: 以力空间中的signed friction distance（锥边界有符号距离）定义摩擦裕度；通过双臂倾斜时平衡枢轴力矩偏差所需的力修正量，推导任务自适应裕度；将裕度约束写入一系列二阶锥规划(SOCP)，在每个倾斜角求解全局最优接触力，同时使物体绕环境枢轴平衡并使机器人/枢轴接触力远离名义摩擦边界。

Result: 优化层面呈现可解释的力重分配及对物体属性变化的适应；实物实验在物体属性变化与模型失配下成功执行任务；与固定裕度、无裕度对照表明自适应裕度的必要性。

Conclusion: 论证带符号摩擦距离裕度可作为双臂非抓握操作中维持接触的力规划机制，兼具可解释性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Maintaining contact in non-prehensile manipulation requires more than nominal friction cone feasibility: a force barely inside the cone and one well inside it are both feasible, but they provide different margins against slip and contact loss. This paper uses signed friction distance to measure friction margin in force space and derives a task-adaptive margin from the force correction needed to balance pivot moment deviations in bimanual tilting. The resulting contact margins are embedded in a sequence of second-order cone programs (SOCPs), each giving a globally optimal contact force solution at a tilt angle. The optimizer balances the object about an environmental pivot while keeping robot and pivot contact forces away from the nominal friction boundary. Optimizer-level results show interpretable force redistribution and adaptation to object property changes. Real-world experiments demonstrate successful execution under object property variations and model mismatch, while fixed margin and no-margin comparisons illustrate the importance of adapting the margin to task demand. These results support signed friction distance margins as a force planning mechanism for maintaining contact in bimanual non-prehensile manipulation. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [187] [Omni-IGN: Onboard Image-Goal Navigation for Drones with Omnidirectional Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36968)
*Zichen Yan,Rui Huang,Shenao Wang,Yucong Wang,Nuthasith Gerdpratoom,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Omni-IGN 是一个全机载无人机 ImageNav 系统，融合四目鱼眼感知、基于注意力的 RL 策略与碰撞感知安全层，在 Habitat 上优于连续动作基线，并在 IsaacSim 与真机实验中验证零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中 ImageNav 方法基于单视角观测，需要额外旋转与探索才能找到视野外的目标相关线索；同时无人机机载算力有限，需同时兼顾探索、目标导航与避障。

Method: 1) 四目鱼眼 RGB 环视感知，同时支撑 ImageNav 与避障，无需专用深度传感器；2) 基于注意力的 RL 策略，跨多视角自适应融合目标相关特征，输出连续导航指引；3) 碰撞感知安全层，结合学习式深度估计对动作进行安全修正。

Result: Habitat 基准上在连续动作基线中取得最佳性能；IsaacSim 飞行测试验证学习式深度估计与动作安全层结合对避障的有效性；真机实验实现无需微调、无需外部定位的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。

Conclusion: 多视角鱼眼融合是提升空中 ImageNav 效率与鲁棒性的有效途径，且全机载系统具备直接部署到真实无人机的潜力。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation (ImageNav) requires an agent to reach a location specified by a goal image in an unknown environment. For drones, this task demands exploration, goal- directed navigation, and collision avoidance under limited onboard computing resources. Existing aerial ImageNav methods are built on single-view observations, requiring additional rotation and exploration to search for goal-relevant cues outside the field of view. In this paper, we present Omni-IGN, a fully onboard ImageNav system that integrates quad-fisheye perception, an attention-based reinforcement learning (RL) policy, and a collision-aware safety layer. The policy adaptively fuses goal-relevant features across multiple views to provide directional guidance for continuous navigation. The quad-fisheye RGB images support both ImageNav and obstacle avoidance without a dedicated depth sensor. On Habitat benchmarks, Omni-IGN achieves the best performance among continuous-action baselines. Moreover, flight tests in IsaacSim validate the effectiveness of integrating learning-based depth estimation with the action safety layer for collision avoidance. Finally, real-world experiments demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer without any fine-tuning or external localization. The code and model checkpoints will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [188] [Structure-Actuation Co-Design of All-Electric Heavy-Duty Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36996)
*Mohammad Bahari,Amir Hossein Barjini,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合离散-连续多目标双层优化框架，用于全电重载机械臂的"结构-驱动"协同设计：上层优化几何与驱动拓扑，下层为各关节构建电机/减速器/丝杠离散方案库，系统级协调器按质量、成本、损耗感知供电能量选型，并将执行器质量反馈至机器人模型迭代至质量一致不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全电重载机械臂中，结构几何与驱动选型强耦合（执行器质量影响动力学与结构负载），且电机/减速器/丝杠本质为离散目录选型，与连续几何变量混合；现有设计流程多为串行、单目标，难以生成兼顾硬件质量、成本与能耗的Pareto方案。

Method: 混合离散-连续多目标双层优化。上层：连续几何+离散驱动拓扑；下层：关节级搜索构造电机、减速器、丝杠的可行方案档案；系统级协调器按硬件质量、协同设计成本、损耗感知供电能量进行多目标选型；执行器质量回馈机器人模型，迭代求解质量一致不动点。并做固定设计消融（串行单次选型对比）与负载筛选分析。

Result: 在100 kg工具负载的全位姿SE(3)基准上得到8个可行非支配设计，全部满足12个指定位姿；所有设计均在3次迭代内完成质量耦合收敛。消融显示：单次串行选型使硬件质量最大变化2.13%、成本10.20%、供电能量9.40%（量级）。负载筛选表明随载荷增加质量-成本-能量需求上升，且竞争性机构拓扑发生变化。

Conclusion: 双层混合变量+质量不动点反馈的多目标框架可有效求解重载机械臂结构与驱动协同设计，串行设计会带来可测量的次优性；载荷显著影响优选驱动拓扑，说明协同设计需面向任务载荷尺度进行。

Abstract: This paper presents a mixed discrete-continuous multiobjective bilevel framework for the structure-actuation co-design of all-electric heavy-duty robotic manipulators. The upper level optimizes manipulator geometry and actuation topology, while joint-specific lower-level searches construct feasible archives of discrete motor, gearbox, and screw configurations. A system-level coordinator selects actuator packages according to hardware mass, co-design cost, and loss-aware supply energy, and the resulting actuator masses are fed back to the robot model until a mass-consistent fixed point is obtained. Evaluation on a full-pose SE(3) benchmark with a 100-kg tool payload yields eight feasible nondominated designs, all satisfying the 12 prescribed poses. Actuator-mass coupling converges in three iterations for every reported design. A fixed-design ablation shows that one-pass sequential sizing changes hardware mass by up to 2.13%, co-design cost by 10.20%, and supply energy by 9.40% in magnitude. Payload screening further reveals increasing mass-cost-energy demand and changes in competitive mechanism topology with carried load.

</details>


### [189] [State estimation for a hybrid rigid-continuum space manipulator system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37043)
*Lorenzo Ticozzi,Patricio A. Vela,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于混合软- rigid空间机械臂（末端为绳驱动连续体机器人）的状态重构管线：将配置估计建模为MAP推断，用因子图融合关节编码器与视觉管道测量，并采用Geometric Variable Strain（GVS）获得最低阶参数化；在仿真中以合成真实感图像模拟视觉测量，于闭环轨迹上验证精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨组装、维修、加注等任务需要长寿命航天器机械臂；引入连续体/软体机构可降低收纳体积与质量，并借本征柔顺性提升作业安全性。但软-刚性混合结构的配置状态难以直接从离散、含噪的编码器与视觉测量中恢复，缺少统一、紧凑且低阶的估计框架。

Method: 末端连杆为绳驱动连续体；以混合刚-软运动学模型为约束，将状态估计写成对配置的MAP推断；离散噪声测量（关节编码器+视觉感知）与运动学模型统一转写为因子图，实现异构测量融合与软约束优化；配置采用GVS（几何可变应变）最低阶参数化，便于后续基于模型的反馈控制设计。验证为仿真：闭环轨迹，连续体位置测量由合成真实感图像驱动的模拟视觉管道给出。

Result: 仿真闭环轨迹上的状态重构精度与计算性能均满足要求，验证了因子图+GVS框架融合异构测量的有效性；但仅有仿真结果，无实物或真实图像验证。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了因子图MAP推断与GVS参数化在混合软-刚空间机械臂状态估计中的潜力，为在轨软体机器人应用提供了低阶、可扩展的估计基础；后续需真实硬件与实测视觉数据验证，并衔接基于模型的反馈控制。

Abstract: Spacecraft-mounted robotic manipulators can support long-term operability of orbiting assets by conducting tasks such as on-orbit assembly, repair, upgrade, and refueling. In this context, the integration of continuum/soft robotic mechanisms into space manipulator designs promises to reduce stowage volume and mass, as well as to improve operational safety through the inherent mechanical compliance of soft structures. This work presents a novel state reconstruction pipeline for a hybrid soft-rigid space manipulator, in which the distal link is a tendon-driven continuum robot. The estimation problem is formulated as a maximum a posteriori (MAP) inference problem over the hybrid manipulator's configuration, conditioned on discrete, noisy measurements from joint encoders and a vision-based sensing pipeline. The resulting soft-constrained optimization problem is transcribed as a factor graph, providing a compact representation for fusing heterogeneous measurements with the rigid-soft kinematic model. Furthermore, the adoption of the Geometric Variable Strain (GVS) approach yields a minimum-order parameterization of the configuration, a property expected to facilitate model-based feedback control design in future work. The proposed implementation is validated in simulation on a closed-loop trajectory where, for better realism, position measurements for the continuum link are obtained by means of a simulated visual pipeline leveraging synthetic photorealistic images. The state reconstruction accuracy and computational performance confirm the potential of the proposed framework for hybrid soft-rigid robotic applications in orbit.

</details>


### [190] [GAWM: A Compact Latent World-Action Model for Multi-Task Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37091)
*Yang Liu,Xiaoli Liu,Liuzhao Li,Yuduo Song,Muyuan Liu,Xiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAWM: a compact (<100M) multi-task world-action model that predicts future DINOv3 latent tokens and regresses action chunks, outperforming billion-parameter WAMs/VLAs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）依赖十亿参数级视觉语言或视频生成骨干，训练成本与推理延迟高；作者希望验证：在冻结的强视觉编码器潜空间中做紧凑世界建模是否足够。

Method: 以冻结 DINOv3 编码器提取每路相机特征并压缩为少量 token；轻量预测器以任务（而非动作）为条件，在两个未来时间尺度上预测这些 token，并以真实未来帧的稠密 patch 特征监督（迫使编码未来场景空间布局）；动作解码器由当前与预测 token 直接回归动作块（action chunking）。无机器人/视频预训练。

Result: <100M 参数下，RoboTwin 2.0 上超过十亿参数 WAM，LIBERO 上与十亿参数 VLA 相当；双臂真机上未见演示的动作预测误差低于微调的十亿参数 VLA，推理延迟显著更低；消融显示数据或训练时间受限时潜空间世界模型收益显著。

Conclusion: 潜空间（而非像素/视频生成）世界建模配合任务条件预测，可在极小规模下获得强多任务策略性能，是降低 WAM 成本的有效路径；局限在于仅在桌面/双臂操作基准与少量真机演示上验证，缺乏长程与开放世界泛化证据。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) incorporate the prediction of future observations into robot policy learning. However, most WAMs build on billion-parameter vision-language or video-generation backbones, which incur high training cost and inference latency. In this letter, we present GAWM (Geometry-Aware World Model), a compact multi-task WAM that predicts future observations in the latent space of a frozen DINOv3 encoder. GAWM compresses the features of each camera view into a small set of tokens. A lightweight predictor, conditioned on the task rather than on actions, forecasts these tokens at two future horizons. The forecast is supervised with the dense patch features of future frames, which requires it to encode the spatial layout of the future scene. An action decoder directly regresses a chunk of future actions from the current and predicted tokens. With fewer than 100M parameters and no robot or video pretraining, GAWM outperforms billion-parameter WAMs on RoboTwin 2.0 and performs comparably to billion-parameter VLAs on LIBERO. On a dual-arm robot, it achieves lower action-prediction error on held-out demonstrations than a fine-tuned billion-parameter VLA, with substantially lower inference latency. Ablation studies show that the latent world model substantially improves performance when training data or training time is limited. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [191] [RAVEN: A Unified Active SLAM System for Rapid Autonomous Visual Exploration with Aerial Robots under Localization Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37111)
*Xinyi Chen,Yichen Zhang,Chenxing Jiang,Shaojie Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEN：面向四旋翼无人机的统一主动SLAM框架，融合前沿与主动回环候选的全局路径生成，用分别量化探索效率与定位精度的效用向量加优先级参数选择路径，并以基于在线Fisher信息场的感知感知导航规划器生成平滑轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人敏捷性高、传感器受限，定位漂移更严重；同时存在"探索未知空间扩展覆盖"与"在已访问区域主动回环以抑制漂移"的两难。已有工作多针对地面机器人，且缺乏可依任务优先级自适应调整表现的统一策略。

Method: 1) 识别前沿(frontier)与主动回环候选点；2) 由候选生成全局巡游(tour)方案；3) 用分别量化探索效率与定位精度的效用向量评估，并按[0,1]的优先级参数挑选最终巡游；4) 感知感知导航规划器基于实时在线Fisher信息场最小化视觉状态估计误差，输出平滑执行轨迹。

Result: 基准测试中优于现有先进主动SLAM方法，在多种优先级设置下呈现连续的性能谱；在定制双目-惯性四旋翼上完成全机载实地实验，验证在复杂环境中的实用性。

Conclusion: 提出统一的、显式考虑定位不确定性的主动SLAM框架，实现探索-回环的可调权衡并具备机载实飞可行性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Active simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) addresses the problem of planning robot motion to build complete and accurate maps of unknown environments through efficient exploration while maintaining high localization accuracy. This raises a dilemma between exploring unknown spaces to expand coverage and exploiting visited regions to perform active loop closures. Despite numerous prior works on ground robots, aerial robots with high agility and constrained sensor suites face more significant localization drift and thereby demand dedicated active SLAM planning. Moreover, a unified strategy that adapts performance based on task-specific priorities remains an open question. In this article, we introduce RAVEN, a unified active SLAM framework for Rapid Autonomous Visual ExploratioN using quadrotors, which systematically accounts for localization uncertainty. The framework starts with identifying frontiers and active loop closure candidates, from which global tour candidates are generated. These candidates are evaluated using a utility vector that separately quantifies exploration efficiency and localization accuracy, and the final tour is selected according to a prioritization parameter ranging from $[0,1]$. Finally, a perception-aware navigation planner generates smooth execution trajectories that minimize visual state estimation error based on the online Fisher information field that provides visual localization information in real-time. Benchmark evaluations demonstrate that RAVEN outperforms state-of-the-art active SLAM methods, highlighting its advantages in producing a spectrum of performance across prioritizations. Fully onboard field experiments with a customized stereo-inertial quadrotor further validate its practical applicability in challenging environments. The source code will be released to benefit the community.

</details>


### [192] [From 12 to 200 Demonstrations: How ACT, Diffusion Policy, SmolVLA and $π_{0.5}$ Scale on a Low-Cost Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37170)
*Simon Cowling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在SO-101真机上系统比较四种模仿学习策略(ACT/Diffusion Policy/SmolVLA/π0.5)在12–200条示教下的数据效率，发现ACT从零训练随数据近似爆发式提升、π0.5在少数据下最优但受微调配方主导，并对"启动失败"、失败阶段分布与评测噪声做了量化审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向真机部署时，工程界缺乏对主流模仿学习策略在不同数据量下的可靠经验规律；同时现有报告的成败率常受数据规模、微调配方、起始延迟、评测噪声等因素混淆，需要一个受控的实测对照。

Method: 单一含干扰物的pick-and-place任务，示教集嵌套子集(12/25/50/100/200)，每种策略固定一套训练配方；全部模型在真实SO-101机械臂固定物体摆放集合上评测，共1340个计分episode、126个block。基于关节与相机录像将每个episode映射到四阶段阶梯(启动/接触球/抓取搬运/送达)。对π0.5比较LoRA与action-expert微调；用重复测同一checkpoint估计十episode block的测量噪声，并分离seed间方差；人工评分与录像交叉审计。训练步数在配方内固定，数据量与遍历次数耦合。

Result: ACT(从零训练)成功率由12条示教时1/40升至100条时37/40。π0.5(action-expert微调)在少数据下最佳(12条:16/40；25条:28/40)，此后稳定在约70%；100条起ACT领先，但该差距本研究未能完全定论。SmolVLA与Diffusion Policy峰值约50%：SmolVLA在47%episode中机械臂从未启动，Diffusion Policy在示教中空闲lead-in未被裁剪前几乎不启动。π0.5的微调配方比数据量更关键：LoRA 4/110 vs action-expert 130/200。失败阶段分析：ACT的数据增加使失败由"接触"转为"抓取"再至无失败；π0.5残余失败集中于抓取。测量上同一checkpoint两次评测平均差2/10个成功。

Conclusion: 模仿学习策略的真机数据效率高度依赖具体配方与可启动性，而非单纯数据量；不同策略的失败阶段谱系不同，可作为诊断与改进的针对性依据。工作同时指出数据量与训练步数耦合的混杂、demo block测量噪声与seed方差，明确了哪些结论在该混杂下仍稳健、哪些不成立。

Abstract: We trained four widely used imitation-learning policies, ACT, Diffusion Policy, SmolVLA and $\pi_{0.5}$, on nested subsets of 12, 25, 50, 100 and 200 teleoperated demonstrations of one pick-and-place task with distractor objects, using one fixed training recipe per policy. Every trained model was then run on a physical SO-101 arm on a fixed set of object placements: 1,340 scored episodes in 126 blocks. ACT, trained from scratch, rose from 1 success in 40 episodes at 12 demonstrations to 37 in 40 at 100. $\pi_{0.5}$ with its action expert fine-tuned was the best policy when data was scarce (16 of 40 at 12 demonstrations, 28 of 40 at 25) and then stayed near 70%; from 100 demonstrations ACT was ahead, by a margin this study does not fully resolve. SmolVLA and Diffusion Policy peaked near 50%, and both were affected by a failure to start: SmolVLA's arm never started moving in 47% of episodes, and Diffusion Policy hardly ever started until an idle lead-in was trimmed from the demonstrations. For $\pi_{0.5}$ the fine-tuning recipe mattered more than the amount of data: low-rank adaptation succeeded in 4 of 110 episodes, against 130 of 200 for action-expert fine-tuning. From the joint and camera recordings we place every episode on a four-stage ladder (started, reached the ball, carried it, delivered it) and show that for ACT more data moves failures from reaching, to grasping, to none, while the failures $\pi_{0.5}$ retains are in grasping. We also measure how weak a ten-episode block is as a measurement (the same checkpoint tested twice differs by 2 successes in 10 on average), separate seed-to-seed variation from that noise, and audit the human scoring against the recordings. Training length was fixed within each recipe, so the amount of data and the number of times it was seen change together; we state which conclusions survive that confound and which do not.

</details>


### [193] [Acoustic Signature Optimisation for Bioinspired Flapping-Wing Micro Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37190)
*Yi Lun Duh,Krispin C. V. Broers,Sophie F. Armanini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过优化翅脉布局抑制扑翼膜在翻转瞬间的snap-through咔哒声（最高降8 dB），并用仿蜂鸟羽毛的增材后缘流苏降低宽带啸声3-4 dB且不损失升力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼微型飞行器以安静著称，但实测人工翼比其仿生对象噪声高15-30 dB，缺乏系统性的被动降噪几何设计方法；主要噪声源之一是行程反转时松弛膜在两曲率间突弹产生的脉冲"咔哒"声。

Method: 1) 建立膜反转撞击的动力学snap-through解析模型，据此优化翅脉（venation）布局并制作样件，实测其降噪效果并做参数敏感性分析（取向 vs. 厚度）；2) 采用增材制造的后缘流苏（trailing-edge fringe），借鉴蜂鸟羽毛结构，与切割锯齿翼对比，在保持升力面积的前提下降低宽带噪声。

Result: 优化翅脉在实验中于反转突弹阶段最高降低8 dB；翅脉方向比厚度更敏感。后缘流苏进一步将宽带啸声降低3-4 dB，且无升力代价，效果可媲美切割锯齿翼但不损失有效升力面积。

Conclusion: 解析模型引导的翅脉布局优化与仿生后缘流苏是两种有效的被动降噪手段，分别针对脉冲咔哒声与宽带啸声，可在不牺牲气动性能的条件下显著压低扑翼膜噪声。

Abstract: Flapping-wing micro air vehicles are prized for quiet flight, yet the few existing measurements show engineered wings to be 15-30 dB louder than the animals they imitate. This paper investigates two passive geometric approaches to reduce the acoustic signature of a flapping membrane wing, and applies these to an exemplary wing. A significant sound source in flapping wings is the impulsive click radiated at stroke reversal, when a slack membrane snaps between two curvatures. Our first noise-reduction approach optimises the wing's venation layout, based on an analytical model of the reversal click as a dynamic snap-through of the membrane. The optimised venation pattern was manufactured and resulted in a reduction of up to 8 dB during the reversal snap in experiments. During testing, the venation was found to be sensitive to orientation rather than thickness. Secondly, an additive trailing-edge fringe inspired by hummingbird feathers was shown to further reduce the broadband swoosh by 3-4 dB at no lift cost, matching a cut serration wing without removing lifting area.

</details>


### [194] [How Much Can Best-of-N Help a World Action Model? Measuring Selectable Headroom and Realizing It in Closed Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37196)
*Haojia Gao,Yue Cao,Yumin Liu,Haohua Que,Mingkai Liu,Guanqiu Guo,Zhuo Zou,Bokui Chen,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出去噪反事实协议（比特级可复现分支、重复续段、留出oracle、共同随机数）来测量世界动作模型（WAM）测试时选择的可实现"选择余量"，并据此构建失败门控选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM预测动作的视觉后果，因而测试时可"想象多个候选动作的未来并选最优"。但"每个候选只执行一次取最好"的标准估计把候选质量与后续随机性混为一谈，高估了选择可带来的增益（headroom）。

Method: 提出denoised counterfactual protocol：比特级可复现的分支、重复续段、留出oracle、共同随机数，测量选择器能实现的选择余量；基于测得的结构化动作扰动余量与失败预测读出，构建fail-gated selector，仅在WAM预测失败时偏离原policy chunk。

Result: 4个WAM、2个基准、10万+分支rollout、预注册检验：(i) 标准估计高估原生样本的可选余量，每候选17次续段下有3个WAM的真实可选份额仅24%–66%；(ii) 结构化动作扰动可产生最多16个百分点可选余量；(iii) 动作条件想象中，想象未来与真实未来携带相当的候选结果信息，而Cosmos Policy的value head未利用这一信息。闭环任务成功率：训练任务留出集76.9%→81.6%（policy易失败处增益最大），未见任务62.6%→69.8%。

Conclusion: 候选选择的真实增益被标准估计严重高估；可靠性来自结构化扰动而非原生采样，失败门控选择器可显著提升闭环成功率，且WAM想象情报未被现有value head充分利用。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict the visual consequences of the actions they generate, which makes test-time selection a natural way to improve a fixed policy: imagine the futures of candidate actions and execute the best one. The benefit of selection is bounded by the selectable headroom, how much better the best candidate truly is, but the standard estimate, which executes each candidate once and keeps the best outcome, conflates candidate quality with the randomness of everything that follows. We introduce a denoised counterfactual protocol, based on bitwise-reproducible branching, repeated continuations, held-out oracles, and common random numbers, that measures the headroom a selector can realize, and use its measurements to build a fail-gated selector. Across four WAMs, two benchmarks, over 100,000 branched rollouts, and pre-registered tests, we find that (i) the standard estimate overstates the selectable headroom of native samples: bounds from 17 continuations per candidate place its selectable share between 24% and 66% for three WAMs; (ii) structured action perturbations create up to 16 points of selectable headroom; and (iii) in both WAMs with action-conditioned imagination, imagined futures carry candidate-outcome information with no detectable loss relative to real futures, information that the value head of Cosmos Policy leaves unused. Deviating from the policy chunk only when a readout of the WAM predicts failure, the selector raises closed-loop task success from 76.9% to 81.6% on held-out episodes of training tasks, with the largest gain where the policy fails most often, and from 62.6% to 69.8% on unseen tasks.

</details>


### [195] [A Bi-level Co-design Framework for Lunar Monoped with 2D Jump and Landing Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37199)
*Sanjay Ramkumar,Suraj Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双层协同设计框架，用差分进化搜索单腿机构连杆长度，内层分别用QP任务空间控制与直接配点轨迹优化评估月面重力垂直跳，并扩展到2D跳跃与LQR落地缓冲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月面低重力下接触力减小、飞行相变长，腿部形态与控制器的耦合关系成为设计瓶颈，需研究形态与控制的联合优化。

Method: 双层co-design：外层差分进化搜索连杆长度；内层以垂直跳跃任务评估候选，控制器为QP-TSC或直接配点轨迹优化。进一步在2D跳跃中引入质心角动量调节，并用LQR吸收触地冲击。

Result: 两种控制器给出不同的最优连杆长度；轨迹优化在大部分设计空间优于QP-TSC，在各自最优点处COT低17%。2D原地/前向跳跃稳定，0.3 m连杆基线形态上完成落地稳定演示。

Conclusion: 低重力跳跃任务中控制器选择显著影响最优形态，优化型控制器更高效；该框架可推广到更复杂多腿形态（论文为平面/简化模型，无实物验证）。

Abstract: Legged robots for lunar exploration must be designed for low gravity, where reduced contact forces and long flight phases make leg morphology and controller choice interdependent. We study this for a planar monoped in lunar gravity ($1.625$ m/s$^2$) using a bi-level co-design framework: a differential evolution outer loop searches over link lengths, and each candidate is evaluated on a vertical jump by either a quadratic program based task-space controller (QP-TSC) or a direct-collocation based trajectory optimization. The two controllers yield different optimal link lengths, and trajectory optimization outperforms the QP-TSC across most of the design space, with 17% lower Cost of Transport (COT) at their respective optima. We then extend to a 2D jump, where trajectory optimization with centroidal angular momentum regulation produces stable in-place and forward jumps, and an LQR controller absorbs impact and stabilizes the robot after touchdown. This is demonstrated on a baseline morphology with both links 0.3 m long.

</details>


### [196] [Remember Where You Walk: Learning Spatial Memory for End-to-End Legged Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37203)
*Leon Magnus,Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出局部空间矩阵记忆（LSMM），一种基于Hadamard记忆的网络结构，让强化学习智能体将任务相关信息存储在与位置绑定的记忆单元中，用于机器人导航；在自主迷宫探索任务中，其覆盖率与强循环基线持平，且是唯一能记住迷宫布局的结构，重建误差降低三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境中作业的足式/人形机器人需要记住已离开视野、但很久之后才有用的信息。基于LSTM等从端到端训练的记忆网络往往难以在长时序上保留足够信息。作者希望为导航任务设计一种显式、空间落地的记忆机制。

Method: 提出LSMM（Local Spatial Matrix Memory）：采用Hadamard记忆式网络架构，将通用的任务相关信息写入按位置索引的局部记忆单元，实现与空间位置对齐（spatially grounded）的灵活记忆。集成到RL策略中，在自主迷宫探索任务上评测，与强循环网络基线及有限历史基线对比，并加入迷宫布局重建误差指标。

Result: 在迷宫探索任务中，LSMM的迷宫覆盖率与强循环基线和有限历史基线相当，是唯一能记住迷宫布局的架构，重建误差相比基线降低约三倍。

Conclusion: 显式、位置索引的Hadamard记忆比端到端LSTM式记忆更适合长时序导航中的空间信息保持；但作者仅在仿真迷宫探索中验证，尚未展示真实机器人部署或更复杂场景下的长程导航效果。

Abstract: Humanoids and other legged robots operating in complex environments must remember information that leaves their field of view and only becomes relevant much later. Reinforcement learning policies address this using memory networks such as LSTMs that are trained end-to-end with the policy to memorize such task-relevant aspects. Yet, such architectures often fail to preserve sufficient information over long horizons. In this work, we propose a network architecture based on Hadamard memory that specifically targets robot navigation. Our Local Spatial Matrix Memory (LSMM) enables RL agents to store generic task-relevant information at specific, position-based memory cells, enabling flexible and spatially grounded memorization. We evaluate LSMM on the task of autonomous maze exploration, where it matches the maze coverage of strong recurrent and finite-history baselines while being the only architecture that remembers the maze layout, reducing the reconstruction error threefold.

</details>


### [197] [Retrieve to Act: Motion Primitive Graph Retrieval for Robust VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37215)
*Pham Tri Quang,Bao-Ngoc Dao,Minh Duc Nguyen,Duc-Duy Nguyen,Hung T. Ho,Linh Dang Le,Trong-Bao Ho,Thien-Loc Ha,Tan Q. Nguyen,Tuan Dam,Vu N. Duong,Daniel Sonntag,Ngan Le,Khoa D. Doan,Jan Peters,An Thai Le,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Vien Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R2A: 将 VLA 的 OOD 鲁棒性转化为“几何运动基元检索”问题，用离线时空运动基元图 + 查询相关 GNN 在执行时定位当前状态并检索结构兼容的未来运动基元，通过逐维门控与冻结 VLA 的预测动作融合，无需微调即提升多种扰动的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略语义泛化强但对执行期 OOD 视觉偏移脆弱；已有测试时方法只压制伪视觉线索，忽视了策略训练经验中蕴含的、未被视觉损坏的运动学先验。

Method: 离线将成功演示结构化为时空运动基元图；推理时用查询相关 GNN 动态定位机器人当前执行位置并检索结构兼容的未来运动基元；通过可学习的逐维门控将检索到的几何先验与冻结 VLA 的预测动作调和，自适应修正空间轨迹同时保留局部反应性。

Result: 在 GR00T N1.7、π0.5、StableVLA 三个 SOTA 架构上验证：仿真基准（LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin2.0-Plus）成功率绝对提升最高 +12%，真实世界部署最高 +40%，且不微调基座策略。

Conclusion: 测试时检索训练经验中的几何运动先验并与冻结 VLA 动作门控融合，是一种通用、免微调、跨架构有效的 OOD 鲁棒性增强范式，但依赖高质量成功演示图覆盖，遮挡/非几何型扰动下的泛化与在线计算开销待评估。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies demonstrate remarkable semantic generalization, yet remain highly vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) visual shifts during execution. While existing test-time methods attempt to suppress spurious visual cues, they fundamentally overlook the rich, uncorrupted kinematic priors embedded in the policy's own training experience. We introduce Retrieve to Act (R2A), a test-time scaling framework that formulates OOD robustness purely as a geometric motion primitive retrieval problem. R2A bypasses fragile visual features by structuring successful training demonstrations into an offline spatiotemporal motion primitive graph. During inference, a query-dependent Graph Neural Network dynamically localizes the robot's ongoing execution within this graph, retrieving structurally compatible future motion primitives. These retrieved geometric priors are then seamlessly harmonized with the frozen VLA's predicted actions via a learned, dimension-wise gating mechanism, adaptively rectifying erroneous spatial trajectories while preserving the policy's local reactivity. Extensive experiments across three state-of-the-art VLA architectures, GR00T N1.7, $\pi_{0.5}$, and StableVLA, demonstrate that R2A consistently and significantly enhances robustness against diverse contextual perturbations. R2A achieves absolute success rate improvements of up to +12% in simulation benchmarks (LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin2.0-Plus) and +40% in real-world deployments, without requiring any finetuning of the base policies. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [198] [KEPT: Conformal Keepouts Kept in Planning for Enclosed-Space Navigation in Degraded Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37217)
*Eric Minwoo Kim,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将保形预测(conformal prediction)的保持带裕度条件化于无标签的退化严重度估计，并在规划时与控制命令处统一执行单一制动距离约束，从而保证感知退化下的覆盖率且不牺牲窄通道可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隧道、矿井等封闭空间中烟雾/粉尘/雾使距离图失真，传统"膨胀障碍"式keepout在窄通道会因裕度+规划缓冲而封死路径，现有做法靠放松覆盖率换取可达性，牺牲安全性。

Method: 1) 用无标签的图像/感知退化严重度估计对conformal margin做条件校准，使覆盖概率随退化程度动态保持在目标附近；2) 规划-控制层仅施加制动距离条件（plan-time与每条指令均校验），以实测跟踪残差为唯一裕度，不改动keepout本身；3) 真机地面机器人在烟雾数据上验证keepout，仿真四旋翼雾中隧道闭环对比标准部署。

Result: 闭环仿真中显著减少进入标定keepout的次数，达成目标数在两个校准区间均更多，合并增益3.2个百分点（各区间置信区间含零）；留出录音上分类keepout覆盖率保持在目标附近。

Conclusion: 将覆盖率校准与制动可行性约束解耦，可在感知退化下同时获得安全性与窄通道可达性，但闭环端到端收益统计上尚不显著，需更多实机验证。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles in enclosed spaces such as tunnels and mines rely on range maps that smoke, dust and fog make inaccurate. Conformal prediction calibrates a keepout around the mapped walls that covers the true unsafe set at a chosen probability. Typically an unmodified planner treats the keepout as an inflated obstacle, and nothing ensures that the vehicle can stop before the keepout as it moves when sensing degrades. In narrow passages this margin and the planner's buffer close the route, and existing remedies recover reach by relaxing the coverage level. This paper conditions the conformal margin on a label-free severity estimate to hold coverage near its target as degradation varies. A planning and control layer enforces one braking-distance condition on the keepout at plan time and at every command, takes the measured tracking residual as its only margin and never alters the keepout. The keepout is validated on ground-robot smoke recordings and the closed loop in simulated quadrotor flights in a foggy tunnel against the standard deployment. In closed-loop simulation the proposed system substantially reduces entries into the calibrated keepout and reaches numerically more goals than the deployment pattern in both calibration regimes, an observed gain of 3.2 percentage points pooled whose per-regime confidence intervals include zero. The per-class keepout holds its coverage near the target on held-out recordings.

</details>


### [199] [Spiking Neuromorphic Control for Periodic Orbits Stabilization in Underactuated Robots: A Greedy Lyapunov-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37310)
*Maksim Surov,Oleg Sumenkov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将横向线性化(periodic LTV)与脉冲神经网络(SNN)的Lyapunov贪心设计结合，实现欠驱动机器人周期轨道的轨道镇定，并在butterfly robot上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件驱动/脉冲控制器具有低功耗、事件式采样优势，但缺乏针对非线性欠驱动机器人轨道镇定(而非点镇定)的系统设计方法；同时原有Lyapunov贪心脉冲控制设计仅针对LTI系统。

Method: 对机器人周期轨迹邻域做横向线性化，得到周期系数的线性时变(LTV)系统，将轨道镇定转化为该LTV系统平凡解的镇定；把面向LTI的Lyapunov贪心设计推广到周期LTV横向动力学，构造脉冲神经网络事件式反馈控制器。

Result: 给出周期LTV系统下脉冲控制器的构造性反馈设计，并在butterfly robot的轨迹镇定任务上展示了方法的有效性（摘要未报告定量指标或实机实验）。

Conclusion: 横向线性化+Lyapunov贪心SNN设计可扩展到周期LTV系统，为欠驱动机器人轨道镇定提供事件式脉冲控制方案；证据主要为仿真层面。

Abstract: This work investigates the use of event-based spiking controllers, implemented as spiking neural networks, for trajectory tracking in underactuated robots. Specifically, we address the problem of stabilizing a prescribed periodic orbit. It is well known that, by applying the method of transverse linearization to the robot dynamics in a neighborhood of a periodic trajectory, the dynamics can be approximately described by a linear time-varying system with periodic coefficients (a periodic LTV system). The problem of orbital stabilization can therefore be reduced to stabilizing the trivial solution of this LTV system. We show that a previously proposed Lyapunov-based greedy design, originally developed for LTI systems, can also be extended to the corresponding linearized transverse dynamics, which take the form of a periodic LTV system. This enables the construction of a feedback controller for orbital stabilization of the robot. The effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated on the trajectory stabilization problem for the butterfly robot.

</details>


### [200] [Tokens of Motion: Primitive Motion Learning for Human-Humanoid Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37330)
*Hanwen Ren,Meng Zheng,Wenzhe Cui,Benjamin Planche,Zhongpai Gao,Anwesa Choudhuri,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AFIL利用预训练VLA在线生成失败轨迹，与成功轨迹共享视觉-语言骨干联合训练双动作生成器（DAG），采样时按逐步分布距离自适应地以负引导将动作推离失败区域，提升VLA的鲁棒性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA仅依赖成功演示的行为克隆，缺乏纠错性训练信号，微小执行误差会迅速累积成不可恢复的分布外失败。失败数据虽含信息但难以利用，手工设计失败模式或人工介入恢复代价高。

Method: 端到端框架：(1) 用预训练VLA在线rollout生成失败轨迹，免去人工失败模式设计与人在环恢复；(2) 在共享VLM骨干上联合训练成功/失败两个动作生成器（DAG），参数开销小；(3) 推理采样时用失败生成器做自适应负引导，引导强度由每个扩散步上成功与失败分布的距离决定。

Result: 在域内与域外机器人操作任务（短时程与长时程）上，相比现有VLA基线持续提升任务成功率与鲁棒性，体现出有效性、效率与通用性。

Conclusion: 将失败轨迹转化为自适应负引导是提升VLA策略鲁棒性的可行高效路径，无需额外人工标注即可实现失败感知的策略学习。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a promising paradigm for scalable robotic manipulation, yet their reliance on success-only behavioral cloning leaves them brittle; lacking corrective training signals, minor execution errors rapidly compound into unrecoverable, out-of-distribution failures. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Failure-Informed Learning (AFIL), an end-to-end framework that leverages failure trajectories as adaptive negative guidance for diffusion- and flow-based VLA policies. AFIL uses a pretrained VLA to generate failure rollouts online, avoiding the need for handcrafted failure-mode design or human-in-the-loop recovery. It then jointly trains Dual Action Generators (DAGs) for successful and failed behaviors while sharing a common vision-language backbone, enabling efficient failure-aware policy learning with limited parameter overhead. During sampling, the failure generator adaptively steers action generation away from failure-prone regions and toward more reliable success modes, with guidance strength determined by the per-diffusion-step distance between success and failure distributions. Experiments across in-domain and out-of-domain robotic manipulation tasks, covering both short- and long-horizon settings, show that AFIL consistently improves task success rates and robustness over existing VLA baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness, efficiency, and generality.

</details>


### [201] [Generalizable Target-Less Joint Intrinsic-Extrinsic Camera-LiDAR Calibration using Deep Pixel-Point Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37350)
*Simon Bultmann,Daniele Cattaneo,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出全自动无标定板框架，从原始图像与LiDAR点云联合估计相机内参、径向-切向畸变与相机-LiDAR外参，通过合成增强实现内参/畸变感知的像素-点对应学习，并迭代耦合对应预测与非线性优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习像素-点对应的无靶标外参标定方法精度高、跨环境泛化好，但只能在已知内参的校正图像上运行，要求相机已单独标定，限制了实际部署。作者希望将此类方法推广到未标定的原始相机。

Method: 用合成增强（模拟不同内参与畸变）训练内参与畸变感知的对应网络，使其可直接处理原始畸变图像；标定时迭代交替进行像素-点对应预测与非线性优化，联合精化相机内参、畸变参数与传感器间相对位姿。

Result: 在KITTI一个未见过的相机-LiDAR配置及三个真实机器人平台上零样本评估：联合标定的外参精度优于使用预标定相机的对应方法，同时恢复出准确的内参。

Conclusion: 内参与外参联合标定可在无靶标、零样本条件下完成，且外参精度不降反升，说明脱离预标定相机的假设是可行且有益的。

Abstract: Accurate camera-LiDAR calibration is an essential prerequisite for robust multi-modal perception in robotics. Recent target-less methods based on learned pixel-point correspondences achieve high extrinsic calibration accuracy and generalize across environments. However, they operate on rectified images with known camera intrinsics, restricting their applicability to systems whose cameras have already been individually calibrated. We present a fully automatic, target-less framework that jointly estimates camera intrinsics, radial-tangential distortion, and camera-LiDAR extrinsics from raw images and LiDAR scans. Our method generalizes deep correspondence-based calibration from rectified images to raw cameras through intrinsic- and distortion-aware correspondence learning via synthetic augmentations. During calibration, we iteratively couple correspondence prediction and nonlinear optimization to jointly refine the camera parameters and relative sensor transformation. We evaluate our approach on an unseen camera-LiDAR configuration from the KITTI dataset and on three real robotic platforms in a zero-shot manner. The results demonstrate that joint calibration with our generalized pixel-point correspondence network improves extrinsic accuracy over correspondence-based methods using pre-calibrated cameras, while additionally recovering accurate intrinsics.

</details>


### [202] [VLA-Stem: A Lightweight Safety Mechanism for Vision-Language-Action Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37378)
*Bojun Zou,Wenhao Yu,Jiaohao Li,Jiaxuan Lin,Guanting Ye,Haohan Geng,Weihao Yu,Yanyong Zhang,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Stem：无需微调底层VLA策略的轻量强化学习"安全层"，将VLA输出的动作块转为轨迹级运动参考，在推理时结合本体感受与局部障碍信息自适应修正执行，实现静态约束与动态避障下的安全执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流VLA模型面向无约束静态操作，忽视空间约束与动态障碍；直接在避障数据上微调VLA成本高（尤其动态场景），且会损害原有任务能力。

Method: 提出VLA-Stem作为VLA外挂式安全机制：(1) 将原VLA输出的action chunk转换为轨迹级运动参考，保留其空间意图但允许灵活调整运动与时间节奏；(2) 以该参考、机器人本体感受和局部障碍信息为条件，用RL训练一个轻量Stem策略，在推理时改写动作执行；(3) 用教师-学生蒸馏结合课程学习提升样本效率与鲁棒性。

Result: 在参考跟随、静态空间约束、动态避障三类任务评测；六个受限仿真设置中平均无碰撞任务成功率52.8%，最强安全基线仅23.7%；无遮挡场景保持基座策略性能；真机实验显示静态与动态障碍下碰撞一致减少。

Conclusion: 通过将高层VLA任务意图与"反射式"低层安全响应解耦，VLA-Stem以低成本外挂方式提升受限与动态环境下的执行鲁棒性，提出类脑干机制；但仍依赖真机验证规模与通用性进一步确认。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models integrate high-level reasoning with low-level action execution for end-to-end robotic control. However, most VLA models target static manipulation in unconstrained environments, overlooking spatial constraints and dynamic obstacles. Fine-tuning VLAs on obstacle-avoidance data is a straightforward solution, but collecting such data is costly, especially for dynamic scenarios, and may degrade their original task capabilities. To address these limitations, we propose VLA-Stem, a lightweight, reinforcement-learning-based safety mechanism for VLA systems. Without fine-tuning the underlying VLA policy, VLA-Stem converts its action chunks into motion references and uses trajectory-level guidance to preserve their spatial intent while allowing flexible motion and timing adjustments. Conditioned on these references, robot proprioception, and local obstacle information, the Stem policy adaptively modifies execution at inference time, preserving the original task intent while enabling responsive adaptation to spatial constraints and dynamic obstacles. For robust and sample-efficient training, we combine teacher-student distillation with curriculum learning. We evaluate VLA-Stem on reference following, static spatial constraints, and dynamic obstacle avoidance. Across six constrained simulation settings, VLA-Stem achieves 52.8% average collision-free task success versus 23.7% for the strongest safety baseline, while preserving base-policy performance in obstacle-free settings; real-world experiments further show consistent collision reductions under static and dynamic obstacles. These results demonstrate that VLA-Stem couples high-level VLA task intent with rapid, reflex-like responses to immediate physical risks, providing a brainstem-inspired mechanism for robust robot execution.

</details>


### [203] [PDRVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Model with Physical Dynamics Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37381)
*Hanqing Wang,Zhaotian Cai,Ruichuan An,Bohan Zeng,Tao Zhang,Yilin Chen,Xu Jiang,Kaiquan Chen,Chongyang Wu,Haoran Li,Fan Yang,Mingan Lin,Wentao Zhang,Zenan Zhou,Youzheng Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PDRVLA 在 VLA 中加入轻量 Physical Dynamics Module，从视觉-语言特征中提取物理 token 并直接预测未来 RGB/深度/光流特征，以冻结预训练视频世界模型做辅助监督，避免在线生成视频的高延迟；四个仿真基准成功率 99.3/89.9/93.6/81.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型对物理动力学理解不足；视频世界模型能建模场景动态，但在线生成未来视频会给控制回路引入高延迟，难以实时部署。

Method: 提出 Physical Dynamics Module，作为轻量模块从视觉-语言特征中抽取物理 token 以条件化动作生成，无需在线视频生成。模块通过直接预测未来 RGB、深度与光流特征进行学习，并以冻结预训练视频世界模型提供辅助监督。训练数据全部来自公开数据，并通过 human-to-robot 合成、子任务标注与具身思维链（ECoT）标注扩充。

Result: 四个仿真基准成功率：LIBERO 99.3%、LIBERO-Plus 89.9%、RoboTwin 2.0 93.6%、SimplerEnv 81.5%。消融显示世界模型监督提升未来预测质量，并分析了子任务条件化、未来物体位姿监督与 ECoT 训练对动作成功率的影响。

Conclusion: 以特征级未来预测替代视频生成，可在不牺牲控制频率的前提下为 VLA 注入物理动力学先验，方法简洁且在多基准上表现领先；但证据主要限于仿真，缺真实机器人验证与系统级延迟量化。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models generate robot actions from visual observations and language instructions, but their understanding of physical dynamics remains limited. Video world models can capture scene dynamics, yet generating future videos introduces high latency into the control loop. We introduce PDRVLA, whose lightweight Physical Dynamics Module extracts physical tokens from vision--language features to condition action generation without online video generation. The module learns through direct prediction of future RGB, depth, and optical-flow features, with auxiliary supervision through a frozen pretrained video world model. Training relies entirely on publicly available data, expanded through human-to-robot synthesis, subtask annotation, and embodied chain-of-thought (ECoT) annotation. Across four simulation benchmarks, PDRVLA achieves success rates of 99.3% on LIBERO, 89.9% on LIBERO-Plus, 93.6% on RoboTwin~2.0, and 81.5% on SimplerEnv. Ablations show improved future prediction with world-model supervision and evaluate how subtask conditioning, future object-pose supervision, and ECoT training affect action success.

</details>


### [204] [Learning Quality-Controllable Robot Policies with Iterative Hindsight Relabeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37393)
*Sinjae Kang,Dong Kyu Shin,Jaehong Kim,Kamin Lee,Hyoeun Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Seonghyeon Ye,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出质量可控策略(QCP)，可在推理时控制执行速度、平滑度等质量属性及其组合，并用迭代hindsight relabeling扩展可控质量范围，超越示教质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略评估通常只用二元成功信号，但真实部署还要求执行速度、动作平滑度等质量属性，且需按任务需求动态控制。

Method: QCP以质量标签为条件输入，实现推理时对单个质量属性及其组合的控制；进一步提出迭代后见标签重标定循环：按目标质量标签在线采样rollout，将成功轨迹加入数据集，按实测质量对未来轨迹做hindsight relabeling，再微调策略，从而把可控质量范围扩展到初始示教之外。

Result: 在Robomimic与RoboEval上，迭代过程中任务执行质量与成功率持续提升，生成的成功rollout质量超过初始示教；真机抓取操作中控制速度提升了传送带作业吞吐，控制搬运稳定性提升了端酒杯任务成功率。

Conclusion: 质量可控策略在物理部署中带来实际收益，离线数据+在线hindsight relabeling的自提升机制可突破示教质量上限。

Abstract: Robot policy evaluation often relies on a binary success signal for task completion. In real-world deployment, however, desired execution behaviors are also defined by quality attributes such as execution speed and motion smoothness, which need to be dynamically controlled according to task requirements. To address this, we introduce Quality-Controllable Policy (QCP), a robot policy that enables inference-time control over both individual quality attributes and their combinations. To extend the controllable quality range beyond initial demonstrations, we further propose an iterative hindsight relabeling loop: QCP collects online rollouts under target quality labels, adds successful trajectories to the dataset, relabels the accumulated trajectories in hindsight according to their measured quality, and fine-tunes the policy. Experiments on Robomimic and RoboEval demonstrate that QCP consistently improves both task execution quality and success rates across iterations, producing successful rollouts that exceed the quality of the initial demonstrations. Finally, we validate QCP on real-world manipulation tasks, where controlling speed increases throughput in conveyor operations and controlling transport stability improves success when carrying wine glasses, demonstrating that quality-controllable policies yield practical gains in physical deployment.

</details>


### [205] [AuRaSim: A Ray-Tracing Based Automotive FMCW Radar Simulator for Dynamic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37403)
*Zhaoze Wang,Changxu Zhang,Tai Fei,Christopher Grimm,Yi Jin,Claas Tebruegge,Markus Gardill*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AuRaSim：基于Blender与Sionna RT的光线追踪车载FMCW雷达仿真框架，实现动态场景、含运动的多普勒建模与相干多通道ADC数据立方体合成，数字孪生场景下RD图RMSE 0.147、PSNR 16.93 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成相机与LiDAR仿真已较成熟，但车载雷达仿真需要3D引擎、电磁材料特性、天线配置、波传播机理与相干信号合成的联合建模；现有框架或闭源专有、或过度简化，难以规模化生成雷达原始测量数据。

Method: 复用Blender构建动态场景、Sionna RT做GPU加速电磁光线追踪，并新增雷达专用适配：带复系数的路径级传播、运动感知多普勒建模、基于波形配置的相干多通道ADC立方体合成。

Result: 在数字孪生场景中，仿真与实测距离-多普勒图定量对比达RMSE 0.147、PSNR 16.93 dB，表明合成信号具有较高准确性与保真度。

Conclusion: AuRaSim为可复现的车载雷达仿真与研究提供了完整、可扩展的开放平台，弥补雷达原始数据合成工具的空白。

Abstract: Modern simulators can generate synthetic camera and LiDAR data with accurate annotations for downstream tasks. In contrast, automotive radar simulation remains less mature because it requires not only a 3D engine to model dynamic objects in the scenes but also electromagnetic material properties, antenna configurations, wave propagation mechanisms, and coherent signal synthesis. Existing frameworks are either proprietary or overly simplified, limiting the scalability of radar raw measurements. This paper presents AuRaSim, a ray-tracing-based automotive frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar simulation framework for dynamic scenarios. Rather than implementing all components from scratch, AuRaSim builds on Blender for efficient dynamic-scene construction and Sionna RT for graphics processing unit (GPU) accelerated electromagnetic ray tracing, while introducing radar-specific adaptations for path-resolved propagation with complex coefficients, motion-aware Doppler modeling, and subsequently coherent multi-channel analog-to-digital converter (ADC) cube synthesis based on waveform configurations. Quantitative comparison between simulated and measured range-Doppler maps in digital-twin scenarios achieves a root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 0.147 and a peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of 16.93 dB, demonstrating the accuracy and fidelity of the synthetic signal. AuRaSim therefore provides a complete and extensible platform for reproducible automotive radar simulation and research. Project homepage: this https URL .

</details>


### [206] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning of Co-Adaptive Hip Exoskeleton Control (MARL-Exo) for Metabolic Energy Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37441)
*Yifei Yuan,Jakob Wolf,Ghaith Androwis,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARL-Exo：在仿真中用代谢能量奖励令全身肌肉骨骼模型与双侧髋外骨骼策略协同适应训练，学到的单一群体级控制器零样本迁移到 8 名受试者，佩戴外骨骼净代谢率较被动设备降低 18.3%，其中 6 人相对无设备行走降低最高 24.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外骨骼助力多依赖 per-user human-in-the-loop 优化，需逐人调参、成本高且难以泛化；作者希望验证：以生理代谢能量为目标、在仿真中与人模型共同进化的策略，能否在不做用户级微调的情况下直接迁移到新用户，并考察机械功率整形项对学习结果的影响。

Method: 提出多智能体强化学习框架 MARL-Exo：一个全身模型（304 个肌肉执行器，随机化解剖结构、步态与速度）与一个双侧髋外骨骼策略分两阶段同步训练。外骨骼仅观测延迟的髋关节运动学及其历史指令；奖励基于生理能量学模型给出的肌肉代谢功率，分别设置纯代谢（Metabolic）与叠加机械功率整形（Metabolic + Power）两种版本。Metabolic 策略未重训练即部署到 8 名健康成人以 1.25 m/s 行走，采用间接测热法测量。

Result: 仿真中 Metabolic 策略使建模肌肉代谢功率相比无设备行走降低 13.8%，Metabolic + Power 仅 8.8%。实机上相对被动模式净代谢率降低 18.3 ± 6.6%（全部 8 人）；相对无设备平均降低 7.4 ± 10.0%，6 人抵消 13.1% 的佩戴代价，最多低于自身无设备成本 24.2%。

Conclusion: 代谢驱动的共同适应策略可零样本迁移至新用户并持续降低行走代谢成本，而显式功率整形反而削弱仿真收益；该方法产出单一群体级控制器，并支持在人体验证前于仿真中筛选助力目标。

Abstract: Objective: This study aims to determine whether a hip-exoskeleton policy trained in simulation, by co-adapting with a musculoskeletal human model under a metabolic-energy reward, reduces the metabolic cost of walking in new users without per-user tuning, and how mechanical-power shaping affects the learned assistance. Methods: We developed MARL-Exo, a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework in which a full-body human model (304 muscle actuators; randomized anatomy, gait, and speed) and a bilateral hip-exoskeleton policy are trained concurrently in two stages. The exoskeleton observes only delayed hip kinematics and its past commands; its reward includes muscle metabolic power from a physiologically based energetics model, alone (Metabolic) or with mechanical-power shaping (Metabolic + Power). The Metabolic policy was deployed and tested without retraining in eight healthy adults walking at 1.25 m/s, using indirect calorimetry. Results: In simulation, the Metabolic policy reduced modeled muscle metabolic power by 13.8% relative to no-device walking, versus 8.8% for Metabolic + Power. On hardware, MARL-Exo reduced net metabolic rate by 18.3 $\pm$ 6.6% relative to Passive in all eight participants. Relative to No-Exo, the mean reduction was 7.4 $\pm$ 10.0%; six participants more than offset the 13.1% device-wearing penalty, walking up to 24.2% below their No-Exo cost. Conclusion: A metabolically informed policy co-adapted in simulation transferred to users without per-user tuning and consistently reduced the metabolic cost of walking with the device, while explicit power shaping reduced the simulated benefit. Significance: Unlike per-user human-in-the-loop optimization, this approach yields a single population-level controller and allows assistance objectives to be screened in simulation before human testing.

</details>


### [207] [Electric-Field Induction Motor with Discrete Circuit Elements and Improved Rotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37452)
*Alex S. Miller,Jeffrey H. Lang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证一种宏观尺度电场感应电机，其转子在有限个导电叶片之间引入离散电路元件，以模拟磁感应电机"深槽"(deep bar)转子的频率相关阻抗特性，从而在宽转速范围内提升转矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电场驱动执行器在纳米尺度和生物系统中占主导，在低质量、真空、高温等宏观场景也有潜力；但已有电场感应电机转子仅由单一连续材料构成，电阻率、介电常数与电路特性设计自由度受限。同时，磁感应电机中的"深槽"转子利用趋肤效应在高频下降低转子时间常数、改善宽速域性能，而电场电机缺乏对应机制。

Method: 用导电叶片间的离散电路元件构建转子，使转子表面电路阻抗随激励频率变化，以电气方式而非几何趋肤效应复现"深槽"特性；随后对该结构进行建模分析、参数设计，并搭建宏观尺度样机进行实验验证。

Result: 摘要仅声称"propose, analyze, design and demonstrate"了一台宏观尺度电场感应电机，并给出定性结论：相较单材料转子，在宽速度范围内获得改进的转矩表现；未提供数值化的转矩、效率或对比数据。

Conclusion: 离散电路转子为电场驱动机器提供了超越连续材料限制的设计自由度，并将磁电机中成熟的深槽思想迁移至电场域，扩展了电场感应电机的宽速域转矩能力。

Abstract: Electric-field-based actuators and machines lead the design space at the nanoscale and in biological systems, while having the potential to be used in favorable macroscale applications which prioritize minimal mass, performance in a vacuum, or performance in high temperatures. Electric-field induction motors consist of a set of stator electrodes which induce opposite charges onto a movable rotor surface. In earlier work, rotor surfaces for electric-field induction motors have been made of single continuum materials, which are limited in possible resistivity, permittivity and circuitry characteristics. To enhance design freedom, we develop a rotor for electric-field-based machines which may be constructed out of discrete circuit components between a finite number of conductive vanes. Magnetic-field induction motors, which are common in machinery, often utilize ``deep bar'' rotors, consisting of conducting bars which decrease the rotor time constant with increasing frequency using the skin depth effect. We propose, analyze, design and demonstrate a macro-scale electric-field induction motor which uses circuitry on the rotor surface to emulate the ``deep bar'' design, providing improved torque over a wide range of speeds.

</details>


### [208] [Autonomous fixed-wing interception using reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37470)
*Daan van Hamersveld,Ewoud Smeur,Iurii Kriachko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 固定翼拦截无人机用强化学习训练神经网络策略，将滤波后的单目视觉观测直接映射为执行器指令，在仿真与真实飞行中完成对机动固定翼目标的拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中型中空长航时无人机（如Shahed类）缺乏低成本防空手段；四旋翼拦截器续航短、只能近距拦截低空目标，固定翼平台续航足但仍依赖人工操控，因此需要自动化的固定翼视觉拦截方案。

Method: 自建Python仿真环境，用强化学习与PPO训练端到端策略：输入为经过滤波的单目视觉观测，输出直接为执行器指令；同时实现两个经典制导控制器作为仿真基线，并将策略部署到真实固定翼平台进行飞行测试。

Result: 策略在仿真与真实飞行中均能完成拦截；仿真中在大多数条件下优于经典制导控制器；真实飞行中成功拦截机动固定翼目标。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了AI在固定翼拦截无人机自主控制上的潜力，为低成本反长航时无人机提供了端到端视觉制导的可验证路径。

Abstract: Medium altitude, long endurance unmanned aerial systems such as Shahed-type strike drones are an unanswered threat in modern conflicts. Conventional air defense systems are too expensive to provide a feasible large-scale solution. Quadcopter interceptors are a cheap, battle-proven alternative, but their extremely limited flight time restricts them to low altitude targets at short range, whereas fixed-wing platforms have the endurance to reach higher altitude targets from longer distances. However, such interceptors are still operated manually. Recent developments in learning-based controllers show potential for automation. In this work, a fixed-wing interceptor is presented that is controlled by a neural network that maps filtered monocular vision observations directly to actuator commands. The policy was trained in a custom Python environment using reinforcement learning and proximal policy optimization. The policy was then deployed on a fixed-wing platform and tested in real interception scenarios. As a baseline, two classical guidance controllers were also developed and tested in simulation. The policy is able to intercept targets both in simulation and in real flight. In simulation, the policy outperforms the classical controllers under most conditions. The interceptor was ultimately successfully validated in real flight by intercepting maneuvering fixed-wing targets. This novel system shows the potential of artificial intelligence for autonomous control of fixed-wing interceptor drones.

</details>


### [209] [Ego-CEVR: Learning Cross-Embodiment Visual Representations from Human Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37509)
*Keru Zhou,Zhirui Fang,Xinrun Xu,Yaopeng Lou,Zeyuan He,Zihao Zhu,Xinze Niu,Chen Wang,Tianfu Li,Samith Va,Bin Qian,Junwei Li,Wenjia Ding,Yicheng Xu,Chang Zhou,Ziwei Wang,Li Liu,Xiu Li,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ego-CEVR：分层视觉表征学习框架，全局模块用自我中心视频做未来预测学任务状态与进度，交互模块借腕部观测+机器人动作监督学局部对齐与接触线索，双残差路径同时喂给策略，支持无动作标签的大规模预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何把低成本、可规模化的自我中心视频转化为机器人操作能力：人类 ego 视频富含高层任务语境，而真机示教（腕部视角+动作标注）提供细粒度动作对齐监督，二者需被合理分工而非混训。

Method: 数据原生分层预训练：Global 模块在 ego 视频上以未来预测目标学习物体关系、任务状态与时间进度；Interaction 模块在全局语境下解释腕部观测，用机器人动作监督学习局部对齐与接触线索；两条独立残差路径分别接入动作策略；Global 模块可独立在大规模人类 ego 视频与多本体机器人视频上预训练，无需动作标签或配对腕部观测。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅声明支持跨人类/多本体视频的独立预训练与表征解耦设计。

Conclusion: 以表征分工替代数据混训是关键洞见，属有方法新意的 VLA 预训练范式；但缺少真实机器人实验指标与大范围消融，证据强度不足以判定为核心必读。

Abstract: As egocentric video becomes increasingly available for embodied learning, an important research question is how to turn this low-cost, scalable source of data into robot manipulation capabilities. Egocentric videos naturally provide rich context for high-level task understanding, while real-robot demonstrations with wrist observations and action annotations provide supervision for fine-grained action alignment. We present Ego-CEVR, a data-native framework for hierarchical visual representation learning that assigns distinct learning objectives to global and interaction representations. The global module learns object relations, task state, and temporal progress through future prediction on egocentric video. The Interaction module interprets wrist observations in global context and learns local alignment and contact cues through robot action supervision. Separate residual paths expose both representations to the action policy, preserving direct access to task context alongside fine-grained execution information. Ego-CEVR supports independent pretraining of its global video module on large-scale human egocentric videos and videos from diverse robot embodiments, without requiring robot action labels or paired wrist observations.

</details>
