### [210] [World Dreamer v5-lite: A Multi-Robot World Action Model with One Dreamer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37518)
*Guoning Wu,Zijian Cai,Yuhang Zhang,Ying Liu,Yongbin Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World Dreamer v5-lite：以共享世界状态为核心的多机器人 world-action 模型，标定后的视频特征工作空间对齐、空间尺寸不随视角增加而变化，VLM 为每机器人提供一个 query 读取共享状态并交换位姿信息，动作流相互分离；配合掩码视角协同训练，在 RoboFactory 上达 74.0% 成功率（+23.7 pt），真实机器人 78.8%（+13.8 pt），RoboCasa365 58.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人控制需融合分布式相机视角并预测各自动作序列，但现有 VLA 与世界-动作模型多为单机器人；简单拼接多视角无法建立视角间的空间对应关系，因而需要一个共享的空间化世界状态来承载跨视角信息。

Method: （1）用预训练视频编码器提取特征，经标定投影到工作空间坐标下的共享世界状态，其空间分辨率固定、不随视角数量增长；（2）VLM 提供场景上下文并为每个机器人产生一个 query，query 读取共享状态并在其间进行位姿感知信息交换，但动作解码流保持独立；（3）掩码视角协同训练：在视觉两分支中随机屏蔽部分相机视角而保留全部动作监督目标，迫使模型利用其他机器人共享的观测。

Result: 预训练数据超过 10,000 小时轨迹，涵盖 20 种仿真机器人、RoboFactory 与真实机器人。RoboFactory 平均成功率 74.0%，超最强基线 23.7 个百分点；四项真实机器人任务平均 78.8%，领先 13.8 个百分点；RoboCasa365 总体 58.08%。

Conclusion: 显式空间化且规模恒定的共享世界状态是高效多机器人 world-action 建模的关键，配合掩码视角协同训练可显著提升跨视角协同能力，并在仿真与真实机器人上取得一致性增益。

Abstract: Multi-robot control requires integrating distributed camera views while predicting separate action sequences. Most vision-language-action and world-action models target one robot. Combining multiple views alone does not establish spatial correspondence between them. World Dreamer v5-lite is a multi-robot world-action model built around a shared world state for robot-specific action prediction. Calibrated features from a pretrained video encoder populate this state in workspace coordinates; its spatial size remains fixed as views are added. A vision-language model provides scene context and one query per robot. The queries read the shared state and exchange pose-aware information, while their action streams remain separate. Masked-view cooperative training withholds selected camera views from both visual branches while retaining all action targets. This encourages the use of observations shared by other robots. Pretraining uses more than 10,000 hours of trajectories from 20 simulated robot types, RoboFactory, and real robots. On RoboFactory, World Dreamer v5-lite achieves 74.0% mean success, 23.7 percentage points above the strongest evaluated baseline. Across four real-robot tasks, it reaches 78.8% mean success, a margin of 13.8 points. It also reaches 58.08% overall on RoboCasa365.

</details>


### [211] [Leveraging Distributed Sensing and Hyperdimensional Computing for Non-Line-of-Sight Passive Object Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37519)
*Placido Mursia,Marco Rossanese,Francesco Devoti,Adrian Lendinez Ibanez,Lanfranco Zanzi,Xi Li,Xavier Costa-Perez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Proposes an ISAC-based framework that uses distributed channel measurements (no cameras/LiDAR) to sense passive entities outside a robot's FoV, embedding measurements via hyperdimensional computing for risk-aware classification.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Passive entities (humans, movable obstacles) outside robot FoV cause safety risks; conventional LiDAR/radar/camera sensing is limited by line-of-sight and occlusion.

Method: Distributed channel measurements from communication infrastructure, encoded into high-dimensional hyperdimensional computing representations; risk-aware classification without explicit channel reconstruction; ray-tracer simulation plus ROS-based validation in smart factory scenarios.

Result: Up to 89% classification accuracy under severe occlusions, improved safety and path planning, low computational complexity.

Conclusion: Non-LoS, communication-only passive sensing beyond FoV is feasible for risk-aware robot navigation in occlusion-heavy smart factory settings.

Abstract: In smart factory environments, passive entities such as humans and movable obstacles may interfere with autonomous robot operations, posing significant safety risks. Detecting potential conflicts between robots and entities outside their field-of-view (FoV) is therefore of paramount importance. However, conventional passive object detection systems rely on sensors such as LiDAR, radar, or cameras, which operate under direct line-of-sight (LoS) and are limited by obstacles and occlusions. To overcome these limitations, integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables environmental perception via the communication infrastructure thanks to cooperation among distributed wireless devices. In this paper, we propose a framework for passive entity sensing beyond the robot's FoV that relies solely on distributed channel measurements from the communication infrastructure to perform risk-aware classification. These measurements are embedded into high-dimensional representations using hyperdimensional computing (HDC), enabling robust and scalable learning without explicit channel reconstruction. Numerical simulations based on a commercial ray tracer and ROS-based validation in realistic smart factory scenarios demonstrate improved safety and enhanced path planning with up to 89% accuracy under severe occlusions, while maintaining low computational complexity.

</details>


### [212] [Which Hand Was Late? Diagnosis for Robot Failure Recovery in Bimanual Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37533)
*Baptiste Bonin,Caro Strickland,Audrey Durand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在仿真双臂工具使用任务上，按传感通道测量故障诊断器能读出哪些故障原因：工具滑脱只能在工具手上读；哪只手滞后需双臂传感；单臂传感只能读自身滞后；工具手滞后时"又弱又晚"不可从被动传感辨识。并提出"不可辨识即升级"的修复策略，两次尝试内可修复与已知真因同样多的失败；VLM内部状态几乎完美编码滞后手，但该方向与读答案的方向无关，答案是否报告取决于提问措辞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作故障诊断器（多为VLM）训练与评测都基于单臂、由成功示范扰动生成的失败，掩盖了双臂工具使用中特有的一类失败（工具仍在手但滑出卡槽、双手时序错位、多重并发原因），因此需要系统刻画每种传感通道的可辨识性边界，并检验VLM诊断的可信度来源。

Method: 构建仿真双臂工具使用任务，失败原因由记录轨迹（trace）显式标注，从而可按传感通道（工具手、非工具手、双臂）逐一测量各原因的"可辨识性"；设计修复策略：当原因不可辨识时主动升级（escalation）；同时用探测方法分析VLM内部表征与答案读出方向的关系，并做提问措辞扰动实验。

Result: 工具滑脱仅可从工具手读出；滞后手需双臂信息，单臂至多部分读出对侧；工具手滞后时"晚且弱"不可辨识。两次尝试内，升级式修复策略的成功修复量与告知真因的策略相当。VLM内部状态几乎完美编码滞后手，但该方向与其答案读出方向的距离等同随机方向；答案是否报告极大依赖问题措辞。

Conclusion: 故障诊断应被表述为可辨识性问题而非单纯识别问题：传感配置决定上限，不可辨识时显式升级比盲目推断更有效；VLM的"内部知道"不等于"口头报告"，诊断可靠性须经措辞鲁棒性检验。但结论基于仿真与被动传感设定，真实机器人尚待验证。

Abstract: Failure diagnosers for robot manipulation are increasingly vision--language models (VLMs), trained and evaluated on single-arm failures produced by perturbing a successful demonstration. Bimanual tool use adds failures of other kinds: a tool that slips out of its socket while still held, two correct gestures made at different times, and attempts that fail for two reasons at once. On a simulated bimanual tool-use task whose failure causes are read from the recorded trace, we measure what each sensing channel lets a diagnoser tell. The tool's slip is read on the tool hand; which hand was late is read on both arms, while a sensor on one hand reads its own hand's delay and at most part of the other's; and whether a late attempt was also pushed too weakly is not identifiable from passive sensing when the tool hand is late. Within two attempts, a repair policy that escalates when the cause is not identifiable repairs as many failures as one told the true cause. A VLM's internal state carries the late hand almost perfectly, along a direction no closer to the one its answer reads than a random one; whether the answer reports it depends on the wording of the question.

</details>


### [213] [Predict the Swing, Match the Speed: Robotic Pipe Stabbing With Learned Stereo Vision and Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37535)
*Mohamed Ashraf Mostafa,Taher El-Fakharany,Samir Khaled*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StabBot：预测管柱摆动速度并主动"迎击"的机器人对扣系统，结合摆锤卡尔曼滤波、ResNet-18三维关键点定位与残差强化学习，在200组随机作业中实现100%对扣成功（传统固定导引74%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统钻井接单根时，钻杆被吊卡甩入井口会以运动速度撞向固定"对扣导引"(stabbing guide)，造成高接触速度与峰值冲击力，易损伤螺纹。作者希望让机器人在同一速度下"追上"摆动中的钻杆，以速度匹配实现柔和接触与高成功率。

Method: (1) 摆锤物理模型 + 由钻具记录(tally)标定的卡尔曼滤波，在双目图像中跟踪管端摆动；(2) 六轴机械臂 + 开口V型滚轮头，按匹配速度接近、以触觉确认最后毫米、自动对齐销/盒中心并带侧向柔顺随游车下行；(3) ResNet-18关键点网络在3D中定位管体、销鼻与盒口，残差强化学习策略精修捕获动作；(4) 在1:4 Froude缩放的MuJoCo中做200组配对随机试验（9种规格，2-3/8″油管至20″套管）。

Result: 成功率100%（对照固定导引74%）；中位接触速度18.4→2.3 cm/s，中位峰值头-管作用力11.3→1.4 kN，且200组全部更低（Wilcoxon p<10^-30）；关键点网络中位误差0.4 mm、每对双目2.3 ms；加入20 mm噪声、300 ms延迟、85%丢帧、20 mm标定偏差、20 cm吊卡偏移与40 cm摆幅后仍保持100%。

Conclusion: 速度匹配式主动对扣显著优于被动固定导引，且具备较强鲁棒性；但所有结论均来自1:4 Froude缩放仿真，缺乏实机/现场验证，工程化仍需补足。

Abstract: A joint of pipe run into the hole swings from the elevator over the well and strikes a fixed stabbing guide at whatever speed the swing has. StabBot, a robotic stabbing system, predicts the swing and meets the pipe at its own speed. A pendulum Kalman filter tuned from the pipe tally tracks the joint in stereo images. A six-axis arm with an open V-shaped roller head closes in at matched velocity, confirms the last millimetres by touch, aims the pin over the box when the elevator hangs off centre, and follows the block down with lateral compliance. A ResNet-18 keypoint network locates the pipe, pin nose and box mouth in 3-D, and a residual reinforcement-learning policy refines the capture. On 200 paired random jobs with nine sizes from 2-3/8-in. tubing to 20-in. casing, StabBot seated every joint with the pipe touching only rollers, against 74% for a conventional fixed guide. Median contact speed fell from 18.4 to 2.3 cm/s and median peak head-pipe force from 11.3 to 1.4 kN, and the force was lower in all 200 jobs (Wilcoxon $p < 10^{-30}$). The network locates the pipe with 0.4-mm median error in 2.3 ms per stereo pair. With the network in the loop, success stayed at 100% under 20 mm of added noise, 300-ms delay, 85% frame loss, 20-mm calibration offset, 20-cm elevator offset and 40-cm swings. All results come from a 1:4 Froude-scaled MuJoCo model and are reported at full scale.

</details>


### [214] [Rendering-Free Event Frame Simulation for Fast Reinforcement Learning of Autonomous Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37543)
*Dequan Ou,Austin Lopez,Quentin Missinne,Christophe De Wagter,Guido C.H.E. de Croon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出解析式事件相机仿真器，直接由场景几何与自身运动合成事件流（免渲染），单GPU上17μs生成64x64事件观测，比渲染流水线快400倍，使端到端RL避障训练30分钟内收敛，策略可零样本迁移到Isaac Sim与真实56g Crazyflie（int8 1.2MB，机上推理，三条真实赛道82%成功率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机低延迟、高动态范围、毫瓦级功耗，适合小型无人机在杂乱环境敏捷穿行；但现有仿真器需先渲染真实感图像再转事件，使大规模强化学习慢到不可行，缺乏可训练、可sim-to-real的轻量事件仿真手段。

Method: 利用事件相机本质测量"运动对比度边缘"这一事实，跳过视觉渲染，直接从场景几何与自身运动解析地合成事件流；将仿真器嵌入RL训练环，并做int8量化以部署到Crazyflie（Prophesee GenX320 + OpenMV N6），无微调、无外部计算、无定位。

Result: 64x64事件观测17μs/帧，较SOTA渲染管线快400倍以上；目标导向避障RL 30分钟内收敛（比渲染事件训练快三个数量级）；零样本迁移到光线追踪Isaac Sim；未见杂乱赛道成功率83%（中等难度、可通行概率约0.57），平均目标速度2.4 m/s，纹理密集室内大厅保持70%；真实三条递增难度赛道成功率82%（5/5、5/6、4/6）。

Conclusion: 解析式事件仿真为事件视觉提供了高效且可迁移的训练范式，打通"快速仿真—RL—量化—机载部署"全链路，可在超轻量平台上实现仅凭事件相机的无地图避障飞行。

Abstract: Event cameras offer low latency, high dynamic range, and milliwatt-level power consumption, making them ideal for agile robots such as tiny drones navigating through cluttered environments. However, existing simulators generally render photorealistic images before converting them into events, making large-scale reinforcement learning prohibitively slow. We propose an analytic event simulator that exploits what event cameras fundamentally measure - moving contrast edges - to synthesize event streams directly from scene geometry and ego-motion without visual rendering. Our simulator generates a 64x64 event observation in 17 microseconds on a single GPU - over 400x faster than a state-of-the-art rendering pipeline - enabling end-to-end reinforcement learning for goal-directed obstacle avoidance to converge in under 30 minutes - three orders of magnitude faster than training on rendered events. Policies transfer zero-shot to physically based event simulations (ray-traced Isaac Sim). Our policy reaches 83% success in cluttered courses unseen during training (mid-difficulty, traversability probability approx. 0.57), at 2.4 m/s average goal velocity, and retains 70% in a simulated texture-dense indoor hall. Furthermore, we quantized the policy to a 1.2 MB int8 network running fully onboard a 56-gram Crazyflie equipped with a Prophesee GenX320 event camera and an OpenMV N6 microcontroller board. Without any fine-tuning, external processing, or positioning, the policy achieves an average success rate of 82% (5/5, 5/6, 4/6 flights) across three real-world obstacle courses of increasing difficulty. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [215] [When, Where, and What to Correct: Human-Guided Adaptive Residual Correction for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37547)
*Anukriti Singh,Maxime Caniot,Pratap Tokekar,Firas Abi-Farraj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARC：冻结模仿策略，用稀疏人类纠正确定"何时/何处"探索，再用RL仅学习7维残差动作中的2-3维子空间，把局部失效当作局部问题修正，真机成功率33.3%→75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿策略整体轨迹执行尚可，但在需要"最后一厘米"精度的局部区域失败；重训全局策略或在全动作空间做残差探索都误把局部失败当作全局学习问题，代价高且易破坏已学行为。

Method: ARC（Adaptive Residual Correction）为轻量后训练模块：基础策略完全冻结，RL在其上学习残差修正。人类纠偏不作为模仿目标，而是用于三件事——用干预前状态（pre-intervention states）训练"何时激活"残差控制器；用纠正几何定义残差空间中的低维子空间来限定"何处"自主探索；任务奖励决定"执行什么"残差。真机三个精密操作任务，7关节残差空间。

Result: 真机三任务平均成功率33.3%→75.0%；人类纠偏所定义的任务相关2–3维子空间可保留纠正平方幅度的96.0%–97.7%，说明稀疏纠正足以定位并约束自主后训练。

Conclusion: 稀疏人类纠正的价值在于"定位问题+约束搜索空间"，而非充当最终动作标签；局部残差RL可与冻结策略组合，高效修复last-mile精度缺陷。

Abstract: Imitation policies that reliably execute most of a manipulation trajectory can still fail in localized regions requiring last-mile precision. Updating the full policy or exploring residual actions everywhere treats this local failure as a global learning problem. We introduce Adaptive Residual Correction (ARC), a lightweight post-training module that uses reinforcement learning (RL) to learn a residual correction on top of a frozen policy while using sparse human corrections to structure autonomous exploration. Rather than treating human corrections as imitation targets, ARC uses pre-intervention states to determine 'when' residual control should become active and correction geometry to determine 'where' in residual action space autonomous refinement should explore; task reward determines 'what' residual is ultimately executed. Across three precision-manipulation tasks on a real robot, ARC improves average task success from 33.3% to 75.0%. The human corrections define task-dependent 2-3 dimensional subspaces of the seven-joint residual space that retain 96.0-97.7% of squared correction magnitude. Together, these results show that sparse human corrections can localize and constrain autonomous post-training without requiring the observed correction itself to serve as the final action target.

</details>


### [216] [Kinematic Constraint-Referenced Evaluation for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37550)
*Baptiste Bonin,Caro Strickland,Audrey Durand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Constraint-Referenced Strategy（CRS）：令指令末端执行器 twist 投影到物体关节轴上，将沿关节方向的进展与违反约束的浪费分量分离，从而诊断"成功但方式错误"的操纵策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操纵策略评价以二值任务成功为主，无法判断指令手势是否与物体运动学（关节轴）相容；成功掩盖了迁移失败，需要可归因的约束参照指标。

Method: CRS 需要关节模型及由其导出的关节跟随脚本控制器，不需示范数据或学习式判别器。将末端 twist 分解为沿轴投影分量与离轴分量两条通道分别度量，并对关节轴误差做灵敏度分析。

Result: 74 个仿真铰接物体上，成功指标只把三种迁移失败体现为成功率下降而无归因，CRS 两通道给出各自签名。门训练 BC 策略在 110 个抽屉回合中成功 59 次，其通过回合的离轴旋转约为抽屉训练策略的 40 倍（1.27 vs 0.032 rad/s）。相对相同轴评分的脚本控制器，CRS 判定在关节轴误差 ≤30° 时不变，绝对阈值约容忍 8°。

Conclusion: 以约束参照而非二值成功评价操纵策略，可揭示隐藏的迁移失败并具鲁棒性；属评价方法贡献，缺乏真实机器人验证与闭环改进。

Abstract: Manipulation policies are evaluated predominantly by binary task success, which cannot tell whether the commanded gesture is the one the object's kinematics admit. We introduce Constraint-Referenced Strategy (CRS), which projects the commanded end-effector twist onto the object's joint axis, separating progress along the joint from off-constraint waste. CRS requires a joint model and a joint-following scripted controller derived from it, but no recorded demonstration or learned judge. On 74 simulated articulated objects, success registers three transfer failures as lower rates but attributes none, while the two CRS channels give each a distinct signature. A door-trained behavior-cloning policy opens drawers in 59 of 110 episodes, and in these passes it commands about $40\times$ the off-axis rotation of a drawer-trained policy's passes ($1.27$ vs.\ $0.032$~rad/s). Relative to a scripted controller scored with the same axis, CRS verdicts are unchanged under joint-axis errors up to $30^\circ$; absolute thresholds tolerate about $8^\circ$.

</details>


### [217] [Learning Visuomotor Quadrotor Flight Through Dynamic Narrow Gaps via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37557)
*Riccardo Bianco,Yunfan Ren,Jiaxu Xing,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可微动态窄缝公式，以时变缝平面符号距离门控对齐/居中/平滑碰撞项，将稀疏穿越事件转为稠密路径目标；单阶段、无从零教师/规划器训练视觉-惯性到机体指令策略，仿真99.3%、真机80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿越移动窄缝需厘米级空间对齐与精确时序，可行集极窄；既有规划与学习方法依赖特权状态、教师、规划器或蒸馏，直接从头学习动态窄缝穿越仍未解决。

Method: 设计可微的移动缝隙代价公式：用到时变缝隙平面的有符号距离作为门控，调度居中项、姿态对齐项与平滑碰撞项，把稀疏的穿越成功事件转化为稠密的逐路径优化目标；将其嵌入视觉特征+IMU到集体推力与机体角速度的控制策略，单阶段端到端训练，无需教师、规划器与蒸馏。

Result: 仿真benchmark中路径梯度显著优于score-function梯度，单GPU数分钟训练后成功率99.3%；策略直接迁移至真机，仅用机载视觉与惯性测量从多种初始条件穿越动态窄缝，10次物理试验成功率80%。

Conclusion: 可微门控的稠密路径目标使敏捷视觉飞行策略可从零单阶段习得并零样本迁移真机，为高精度时机敏感机动的直接学习提供了通用范式。

Abstract: Threading a quadrotor through a moving narrow gap is a demanding test of agile flight, requiring centimeter-level spatial alignment and precise temporal execution. We address this challenge using a control policy that operates directly on onboard visual and inertial measurements to output collective thrust and body-rate commands. The task is difficult because success lies on a thin feasible manifold: small errors in timing, position, attitude, or visual observability cause collision. Prior planning and learning methods rely on privileged state, teachers, planners, or distillation; learning dynamic narrow-gap traversal directly, from scratch, remains open. We introduce a differentiable moving-gap formulation that gates centering, alignment, and smooth collision terms by signed distance to a time-varying gap plane, turning a sparse traversal event into a dense pathwise objective. We integrate this formulation into a visual-feature-to-command policy trained in one stage from random initialization, without a teacher, planner, or distillation. We validate our approach in both synthetic and real-world experiments. In controlled benchmarks, we demonstrate that pathwise gradients significantly outperform score-function gradients for precision-critical tasks, yielding a 99.3% success rate in simulation after only a few minutes of training on a single GPU. Furthermore, the policy transfers directly to a real quadrotor, successfully crossing a dynamic narrow gap from varied initial conditions using onboard sensing and inertial measurements, achieving an 80% success rate over 10 physical trials.

</details>


### [218] [EgoThumb: A Vision-Driven Supernumerary Robotic Thumb for Autonomous Grasp and Holding Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37582)
*Jianyu Hou,Jiawei Fu,Weikang Wan,Minghui Jiang,Jiajun Li,Hao Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoThumb：全开源的视觉驱动超数机器人拇指，前臂远程双肌腱驱动+掌侧自视角相机，用视觉模仿学习策略直接从第一人称图像回归连续关节位置，实现跨用户、跨环境的自主"持握-操作"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超数机器人手指硬件笨重、仅能操作大物体，且依赖显式手部动作触发、缺乏环境上下文，交互不自然、泛化差。

Method: 紧凑型双肌腱驱动手指，驱动器迁移至前臂以降低末端重量并实现精确主动关节控制；掌侧自视角相机输入ego-centric图像，经视觉模仿学习策略隐式解码手与目标物体意图，直接输出连续关节位置。

Result: 实现鲁棒自主操作，并在多样用户与环境中展现较强泛化；硬件与软件全部开源（摘要未给出定量指标）。

Conclusion: 把"环境上下文感知"从显式触发改为视觉隐式意图解码，是超数肢体走向自然、可推广交互的可行路线。

Abstract: Supernumerary robotic fingers augment human dexterity by enabling a single hand to perform complex "hold-and-manipulate" tasks that typically require bimanual coordination. However, existing devices are often hindered by bulky hardware that limits interaction to large objects and rely on explicit hand motion triggers that lack environmental context. To address these challenges, we present EgoThumb, a fully open-source, vision-driven supernumerary robotic thumb. EgoThumb features a compact, dual tendon-driven finger whose actuation is relocated to the forearm, reducing distal weight while enabling precise active joint control. The system utilizes a visual imitation learning policy that maps egocentric images from a palm-facing camera directly to continuous joint positions. By implicitly decoding human intent from visual cues capturing both the hand and the object of interest, EgoThumb achieves robust autonomous manipulation and strong generalization across diverse users and environments. All hardware designs and software implementations are fully open-sourced at this https URL .

</details>


### [219] [RESPLE2: Inertial-Informed Recursive Non-Uniform Spline Estimation for Multi-Sensor Odometry with Global Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37602)
*Ziyu Cao,William Talbot,Song Li,Pakpong Chirarattananon,Fredrik Gustafsson,Marco Hutter,Kailai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RESPLE2：基于非均匀B样条与递归IEKF的连续时间多传感器状态估计框架，利用节点钳制边界性质实现惯性驱动的控制点精确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器里程计在剧烈加速度等高动态运动下易失效，需要连续时间轨迹表示与灵活融合异构传感器（LiDAR、相机、IMU、腿部运动学），同时保持实时性。

Method: 以非均匀B样条建模6-DoF运动；基于knot-clamping边界性质，用部分惯性观测直接预测控制点，节点位置随运动与测量可用性自适应；在迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波（IEKF）中执行该预测，并引入reset步骤在节点扩展时保持轨迹一致性；更新步通过连续时间观测模型在各传感器原生时间戳融合异构测量；另设轻量全局优化模块，直接优化样条控制点并加入LiDAR-视觉回环约束。

Result: 在手持、履带、足式及空中-足式混合平台等公开与自采数据集上，覆盖极端加速度的高动态机动；对应传感器配置下精度与鲁棒性达到或超过SOTA多传感器里程计，剧烈运动下优势尤为明显，并保持实时运行。

Conclusion: 提供了一种理论上自洽、可实时运行的连续时间多传感器状态估计方案，代码与数据集开源。

Abstract: We present RESPLE2, a recursive multi-sensor state estimation framework using non-uniform B-splines for 6-DoF motion representation. By exploiting the knot-clamping boundary property, we propose a theoretically grounded mechanism for exact control-point prediction directly informed by inertial kinematics along the trajectory, with knot placement adapted to motion and measurement availability. Within an iterated extended Kalman filter, we perform this prediction using a subset of inertial measurements. A subsequent reset step preserves trajectory consistency as the knots extend. The update step further incorporates heterogeneous sensor measurements at their native timestamps through continuous-time observation models, supporting flexible combinations of LiDARs, cameras, inertial measurement units, and leg kinematics. We further develop a lightweight global optimization module that directly refines non-uniform B-spline control points by incorporating loop-closure constraints derived from LiDAR-visual cues. We conduct extensive evaluations on public and experimental datasets across handheld, tracked, legged, and hybrid aerial-legged platforms, covering highly dynamic maneuvers with extreme accelerations. Compared with state-of-the-art multi-sensor odometry systems, RESPLE2 achieves competitive or superior estimation accuracy and robustness in the corresponding sensor configurations, particularly under aggressive motion, while maintaining real-time operation. We publicly release our code and experimental datasets at this https URL .

</details>


### [220] [Pushing Dexterity and Safety for In-Hand Object Pose Reaching That Works Remarkably Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37652)
*Ian Chuang,Hiya Shah,Vamsi Chilamkur,Iman Soltani,Claire Tomlin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套无需人类数据的仿真训练配​​方（多样复位/目标生成、自适应摩擦课程、系统辨识），使 LEAP 五指手实现手内物体位姿可达（in-hand pose reaching），并叠加可调保守度的学习式残差安全滤波，零样本迁移真机后大幅降低掉落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手的技能学习仍局限于简单抓取或重复旋转等基础动作；当操作难度上升时，"不掉落"等安全性指标却很少被度量。作者希望把"手内任意位姿可达"这一高难度、长序列任务作为研究平台，同时显式度量并优化性能—安全权衡。

Method: 1) 在仿真中训练 LEAP 手策略，不用人类示范；2) 通过生成的抓取数据集实现多样化复位与目标采样（覆盖长序列连续目标）；3) 采用自适应摩擦课程（adaptive friction curriculum）；4) 细致系统辨识以实现零样本 sim-to-real；5) 观测可用动作捕捉或单只腕部深度相机；6) 在任务策略之上训练学习式残差安全滤波器，其保守度可调，用于在性能与安全间折中。

Result: 真机评测 13.3 小时、4 物体、多种腕部朝向、4800 个未见目标：基于状态观测的策略 95.2% 目标达成（2 cm、0.4 rad 容差内）；加滤波器后掉落减少 75%，仍达成 91.5%。策略还能在任意位姿下旋转灯泡、达到分布外目标、承受快速手臂运动与扰动，并仅凭腕部相机作为便携技能（如拧灯泡）。

Conclusion: 给出了一套可复现、开源的全流程配方，把高维灵巧操作中的性能、可靠性与安全性作为可量化对象，为后续研究提供平台；表明仿真课程化训练+系统辨识足以支撑零样本真机灵巧操作，且学习式安全滤波能有效改善安全—性能折中。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered hands is the next frontier of robotics, yet learned hand skills remain limited to simple grasps or basic repeated motions, such as rotating an object. As robots attempt more demanding in-hand maneuvers, safety, such as not dropping the object, becomes critical, yet it is rarely measured. We study in-hand object pose reaching, a task that demands diverse and difficult grasps: moving an object to almost any position and orientation within reach of the fingers, at any wrist orientation, through long sequences of consecutive goals. We present a recipe for training such policies for a LEAP hand in simulation without human data, built on a range of insights, from diverse resets and goals via generated grasp datasets to an adaptive friction curriculum. With careful system identification, the policies transfer zero-shot to the real hand, using either motion capture or a single wrist-mounted depth camera, and prove remarkably capable and robust. On top of the task policy, we add a learned residual safety filter with adjustable conservativeness, letting us tune the balance between performance and safety and greatly reduce object drops. In 13.3 hours of real-robot evaluation over 4,800 unseen goals across 4 objects and diverse wrist orientations, the state-based policy reaches 95.2% of goals within 2 cm and 0.4 rad, and the filter cuts its drops by 75% while still reaching 91.5% of goals. The policies can also spin a lightbulb held in an arbitrary pose, reach out-of-distribution goals, withstand fast arm motion and perturbations, and, needing only a wrist camera, serve as portable skills such as screwing in a lightbulb. We release the full recipe as a platform for studying performance, reliability, and safety in high-dimensional dexterous systems. Videos and more are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [221] [ThinkWorld: World Imagination Reinforces Chain-of-Dynamics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37666)
*Chaehyun Kim,Chi-Pin Huang,Jisoo Kim,Min-Hung Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Seungryong Kim,Fu-En Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ThinkWorld 将动作推理表示为"动力学链"(Chain-of-Dynamics, CoD)——连接当前状态与未来状态的离散运动 token 序列，作为跨具身通用动作表示；先用带动作标签的示教初始化 CoD，再通过预测其诱导的未来状态并用强化学习奖励预测更准确的 CoD，从而让无动作标注的人类视频以状态转移形式直接监督同一推理空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型的泛化受限于带动作标注机器人示教的多样性与覆盖度；人类视频蕴含更广泛的行为与环境经验，但"可扩展数据"与"显式动作接地"之间存在根本性权衡。目标是打通人类视频与机器人示教共享的动作推理空间。

Method: 提出 CoD（动力学链）作为跨具身动作表示，由离散 motion token 序列构成，刻画当前世界到未来状态的中间动力学。两阶段训练：①用动作标注示教初始化 CoD；②对每个 CoD 预测其诱导的未来，用强化学习（世界想象/预测-观测一致性）奖励预测更贴合真实观测状态转移的 CoD，使无动作标注视频通过观测到的状态转移提供直接监督。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人上的实验表明可有效实现人→机器人迁移，并对未见任务与环境具有较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以世界动力学为中介的统一动作表示 + 想象驱动的强化学习，可在无需人类视频动作标注的前提下扩充 VLA 训练信号，为跨具身经验共享提供可行路径；摘要未报告定量提升幅度，需查正文与代码/真机细节验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have made substantial progress in robotic manipulation, but their generalization remains constrained by the diversity and coverage of action-labeled robot demonstrations. Human videos provide much broader behavioral and environmental experience, while learning from them faces a fundamental trade-off between scalable data and explicit action grounding. We introduce ThinkWorld, a VLA learning framework that grounds action reasoning in world dynamics and strengthens it through reinforcement learning with world imagination. ThinkWorld represents each action thought as a Chain-of-Dynamics (CoD), a sequence of discrete motion tokens that captures the intermediate dynamics connecting the current world to a future state and serves as a common action representation across embodiments. We first initialize CoD with action-labeled demonstrations, then predict the future induced by each CoD and reinforce those whose predictions better match the observed outcome. This enables videos without action annotations to provide direct supervision from observed state transitions, allowing human and robot experience to refine the same reasoning space. Experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate effective human-to-robot transfer and strong generalization to unseen tasks and environments.

</details>


<div id='physics.med-ph'></div>

# physics.med-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Retrospective macromolecular pool fraction mapping from conventional clinical MRI: a physics-guided self-supervised deep learning framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37213)
*Jelmer van Lune,Miha Fuderer,Oscar van der Heide,Matteo Maspero,Martin B. Schilder,Jan Willem Dankbaar,Stefano Mandija,Alessandro Sbrizzi*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 提出物理引导的自监督深度学习框架，从常规临床脑MRI（T1w、T2w、FLAIR）反演大分子池分数（MPF），无需配对定量参考扫描，在大规模多中心数据上验证了稳定性与病灶敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPF 是对髓鞘完整性敏感的可定量影像生物标志物，但传统定量磁化传递（qMT）采集与重建流程复杂，难以用于大规模研究。作者希望用常规临床序列回顾性地估计 MPF，从而使既有影像档案与前瞻性常规扫描可被复用。

Method: 将两池磁化传递解析信号模型（把序列平均射频发射功率与表观纵向弛豫联系起来）嵌入网络作为物理约束，构建自监督学习框架，无需定量参考标签；用自适应实例归一化（AdaIN）将中间特征以采集参数为条件调制，以适配异构的临床协议。

Result: 在 7,705 次 3T 扫描（多中心、多厂商、多协议）上训练与评估。1,237 个测试会话中，白质 MPF=0.182±0.007、灰质 MPF=0.144±0.009，跨厂商×协议组间变异系数仅 2.65% 与 5.63%；70 个带脱髓鞘疾病标注的会话中，病灶区 MPF 较正常表现白质低 35.6%；同一健康志愿者两种临床协议的 MPF 图体素级平均绝对百分比差 7.7%，一致性相关系数 0.888。

Conclusion: 磁化传递信息引导的自监督学习可从常规临床 MRI 获得稳定 MPF 图，为脱髓鞘病理的大规模研究（含回顾性影像档案）提供可扩展方案，降低对专用 qMT 采集的依赖。

Abstract: Macromolecular pool fraction (MPF) is a quantitative imaging biomarker sensitive to myelin integrity and other changes in tissue macromolecular content, but specialized acquisitions and reconstruction methods limit its use in large-scale studies. We present a physics-guided, self-supervised deep learning framework for retrospectively estimating MPF directly from conventional clinical brain MRI ($T_1$-weighted, $T_2$-weighted, and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery [FLAIR]) without paired quantitative reference scans. The framework embeds an analytical two-pool magnetization transfer signal model that related sequence-averaged radiofrequency transmit power to apparent longitudinal relaxation. To accommodate heterogeneous clinical protocols, the network conditions intermediate features on acquisition parameters via adaptive instance normalization. It was trained and evaluated on a multisite, multivendor, multiprotocol dataset of 7,705 MRI sessions (3 T). Across 1,237 test sessions, mean MPF was $0.182 \pm 0.007$ in white matter and $0.144 \pm 0.009$ in gray matter, with limited variation across vendor-by-protocol groups (between-group coefficient of variation: 2.65% and 5.63%, respectively). In 70 sessions with demyelinating-disease labels, MPF was 35.6% lower in segmented lesions than in normal-appearing white matter. MPF maps from two different clinical protocols acquired in a healthy volunteer showed a mean voxel-wise absolute percentage difference of 7.7% and a concordance correlation coefficient of $0.888$, indicating good cross-protocol consistency. These results suggest that magnetization transfer-informed self-supervised learning enables stable MPF mapping from conventional clinical MRI, providing a scalable approach to investigate demyelinating pathology in both existing imaging archives and prospectively acquired conventional clinical scans without dedicated qMT acquisitions.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Understanding Human-Robot Collaboration through Joint Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37298)
*Chengwen Zhang,Jianyang Wang,Chang Liu,Yukun Wang,Yuan Xu,Zhoutong Ye,Jie Cai,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PRISMA 2020系统综述，纳入2021–2026年83篇人机协作（HRC）实证论文，用Joint Action理论构建"表征—预测—整合"三能力、九构念的统一框架，映射研究分布与共现、指出证据空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Joint Action理论提供了理解协作中相互依赖贡献协调的概念工具，但这些概念在HRC实证研究中如何被实例化与组合仍高度碎片化，缺乏统一梳理。

Method: 遵循PRISMA 2020的系统综述；筛选2021–2026年83篇实证论文；采用理论驱动的框架分析，将文献归入9个构念、3类能力（Representation、Prediction、Integration），并统计构念的分布与共现模式。

Result: 给出九构念框架下实证证据的分布图与共现结构，识别出证据集中的区域与缺失的构念组合。

Conclusion: 将HRC实证研究映射到统一Joint Action框架，提供连贯的领域概览、证据空白清单与后续研究指南。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration requires partners to coordinate interdependent contributions toward a shared goal. Joint Action theory provides concepts for understanding this coordination, but how these concepts are instantiated and combined across empirical research remains fragmented. We conducted a systematic review following PRISMA 2020, identifying 83 empirical papers published between 2021 and 2026. Through theory-driven framework analysis, we organized the literature into nine constructs across three capacities: Representation, Prediction, and Integration. We analyze how these constructs are distributed and co-occur across studies, revealing where empirical evidence concentrates and where it is absent. By mapping empirical HRC research onto a unified Joint Action framework, this review offers a coherent overview of HRC studies, identifies gaps in current evidence, and provides guidelines for future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [The Trinity:Capability-Driven Recursive Self-Improvement for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36077)
*Weidong Tang,Ruiheng Wang,Jingxuan Zhang,Hexin Wang,Haoyuan Chen,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Yixing Ma,Zichen Jiang,Ziyu Guo,Haodong Li,Zhengbo Jiao,Zhiqing Cui,Xinxiang Yin,Shuo Liang,Zhenglin Wan,Hongzhan Lin,Wangbo Zhao Kaipeng Zhang,Pengfei Zhou,Odin Zhang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 The Trinity 递归自进化框架，用 Creator/Explorer/Evolver 三角色把任务树按"能力需求"重组为层级能力树，实现世界生成与 Harness 进化的协同演化，并在 28 个训练基准、18 个未见基准（含 2 个真实具身任务）上平均提升 34/26 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体扩展面临两大瓶颈：一是构建异构且可验证的环境成本高昂，二是人工迭代 Harness 无法跟上任务多样性增长。作者的关键观察是：模态与表层形式不同的任务往往共享同一底层能力需求，因此可按能力而非任务表象来组织训练与进化。

Method: 三角色递归自改进框架：Evolver 基于已验证执行轨迹，将原始任务树重组为按共享能力需求分层的能力树；Creator 实施 Capability-Driven Real2Sim2Real，对同一节点下的异构任务抽象出共同需求并构建简化可验证的仿真世界用于能力学习，再在原任务上测迁移；Explorer 沿能力树通过选择性继承逐步进化可复用能力模块，形成固定拓扑下跨迭代累积验证更新的 Harness 树；最终用 Capability-Aware Harness Router 依据公开任务信息路由到匹配的模块配置。

Result: 28 个训练基准平均性能提升 34 分，18 个未见基准提升 26 分，并包含两项真实世界具身任务。

Conclusion: 以"能力"而非"任务表层类别"作为组织单位，可以把环境构建与 Agent 脚手架进化耦合为递归自改进闭环，且进化出的专用 Harness 集合在迁移与跨任务泛化上均显著优于基线，显示能力树+路由的组织形式具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Multimodal intelligence can be viewed as human-guided co-evolution between worlds and agents through interaction. However, scaling this process faces two bottlenecks: constructing heterogeneous, verifiable environments is costly, and manual Harness iteration cannot keep pace with growing task diversity and complexity. A key insight is that tasks with different modalities and surface forms can share the same underlying capability requirements. We therefore introduce The Trinity, a recursive self-improvement framework coupling world generation with Harness evolution through three roles: Creator, Explorer, and Evolver. Drawing on verified execution trajectories, Evolver shifts the basis of task organization from surface categories to shared requirements, reorganizing the original task tree into a hierarchical capability tree. We introduce Capability-Driven Real2Sim2Real: for heterogeneous tasks at the same node, Creator abstracts their common requirements into simplified, verifiable simulated worlds for capability learning, while evaluation on original tasks measures transfer. Along this capability tree, Explorer progressively evolves reusable capability modules through selective inheritance, forming a Harness tree whose validated updates accumulate across iterations within a fixed topology. The resulting Harnesses specialize in different capabilities, motivating a Capability-Aware Harness Router that uses public task information to select compatible module configurations from the tree. Our evolved Harnesses improve mean performance by 34 points across 28 training benchmarks and 26 points across 18 unseen benchmarks, including two real-world embodied tasks.

</details>


### [225] [A Heart Means Recursion: Exploring Visual Meaning and Communication in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36449)
*Alexa R. Tartaglini,Zhengxuan Wu,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Martin Wattenberg,Been Kim,Yuxuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 视觉符号（如红心）能隐式地强烈影响VLM在无关任务上的行为，导致模型间视觉通信出现"生成-解读鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VLM是否具备与人类相似的视觉沟通能力，并理解视觉符号如何影响模型行为及其在模型间通信中的可用性。

Method: 行为学风格的干预实验：向模型输入简单视觉符号观察其对编程等任务的影响，对比模型的显式解释/思维链与其实际行为；进而设计模型间视觉通信博弈（传达抽象概念与故事）。

Result: 发现符号具有显著但隐式的引导效应，模型对符号的自述解释与真实行为严重不一致；模型倾向于发送直觉性的形状原语却难以有效引导对方；在抽象概念与故事传递中模型展现出创造性，但部分表达对人类不直观。

Conclusion: VLM具备丰富、创造性且具视觉基础的沟通潜力，但其视觉意义的生成与解读方式显著偏离人类约定，人机视觉通信仍面临挑战。

Abstract: Do vision-language models (VLMs) share our ability to communicate visually? We share preliminary findings, starting from a surprising observation: simple visual symbols like a red heart can strongly influence model behavior on seemingly unrelated tasks, such as steering models towards recursive solutions on simple coding problems. Notably, this steering effect is largely implicit, as models' direct interpretations and thinking traces about visual symbols diverge substantially from their observed behavior. In visual communication between models, this leads to a generation--interpretation gap: while models tend to send shape primitives that they (and humans) find intuitive, these visual cues often fail to steer other models. In further communication games involving abstract concepts and stories, models can creatively use visuals to transmit meaning to one another, although some communications remain non-intuitive to humans. These findings reveal that VLMs might be capable of rich, creative, and visually-grounded communication, but their interpretation and production of visual meaning diverges from human conventions in surprising ways. Together this work highlights both the promise and challenges of visual communication between humans and VLMs.

</details>


### [226] [Polarity--Time Interaction and Multi-Level Reliability for Event-Based Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36625)
*Yonglong Zhang,Zongwu Xie,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向SPADES事件相机空间目标位姿估计，提出可靠性感知框架，串联极性-时间定位、几何位姿恢复、事件-边与CAD投影对齐的局部精修及序列级轨迹优化，在官方测试集上取得0.0474综合位姿分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏事件响应导致关键点误差分布不均，其对航天器位姿的影响取决于对应几何构型与连续观测可靠性，因此需要显式建模观测可靠性而非等权处理关键点与帧。

Method: 三分支表示（正事件、负事件、近期事件时间戳）经跨分支注意力与门控融合后做时空聚合；有序语义点头估计相对对应精度，用于几何约束位姿求解；事件边缘与CAD投影一致性引导带关键点几何保护项的局部精修；序列级用10维描述子（热图形状、重投影一致性、几何条件数）设定时间对齐的观测权重。

Result: 官方SPADES测试集综合位姿分数0.0474，较PASAN+IRIS低约5.4%（更优）；消融中基础定位+均匀PnP为0.0772，完整级联降至0.0514；相同单帧观测下，常数权重0.0549降至质量加权0.0514。

Conclusion: 可靠性加权在关键点层与序列层均带来实质增益，表明"精度估计+几何保护+观测尺度调节"的级联设计优于等权紧耦合方案；但增益幅度有限且限于特定事件相机数据集与空间目标场景。

Abstract: Sparse event responses produce uneven keypoint errors whose effects on spacecraft pose depend on correspondence geometry and the reliability of successive observations. We present a reliability-aware framework connecting polarity--time localization, geometric recovery, event--CAD refinement, and trajectory optimization. A three-branch representation combines positive events, negative events, and recent-event timing through cross-branch attention and gating, followed by spatiotemporal aggregation. An ordered semantic-point head estimates relative correspondence precision for geometrically constrained pose recovery. Event-edge and CAD-projection agreement then guide local pose refinement under keypoint-geometry safeguards. At the sequence level, a ten-dimensional descriptor combines heatmap shape, reprojection consistency, and geometric conditioning to set time-aligned observation scales. On the official SPADES challenge test set, the complete system achieves a combined pose score of 0.0474, approximately 5.4\% below PASAN+IRIS. Validation ablations reduce the score from 0.0772 for base localization with uniform PnP to 0.0514 for the complete cascade; on identical single-frame observations, quality weighting reduces the score from 0.0549 with constant-weight optimization to 0.0514.

</details>


### [227] [Learning Where to Trust: Reliability-Guided Expert Routing and Velocity Gating for 3D Deformable Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36672)
*Chenyu Guo,Wenjie Dai,Yiqian Chang,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yunfan Wei,Shuhan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReliMorph，一种以"对应可靠性"为路由信号的对称三维形变配准框架，将匹配不确定性评估与空间专家融合、残差速度门控及由粗到细的更新闭环耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型形变配准中，局部匹配可靠性与空间上下文选择、形变更新强度之间缺乏显式耦合；在均匀、弱纹理、大形变区域，模糊对应会导致错误的形变更新。

Method: 共享解剖特征+模态特定适配生成相关分布以刻画对应模糊性，得到可靠性线索；该线索用于(1)空间专家融合路由，(2)粗到细金字塔中的残差速度门控，(3)由逆一致性不一致度反馈驱动更细层的路由与门控，构成对称配准框架。

Result: 大量实验表明在解剖重叠度指标上稳定优于对比方法，覆盖单模态与多模态配准任务。

Conclusion: 首次将空间专家路由与残差速度门控同时以对应可靠性为条件，为不确定性感知形变配准提供了可复用的路由式建模范式。

Abstract: Deformable image registration relies on accurate anatomical correspondences. However, explicitly coupling local matching reliability with spatial context selection and deformation update strength remains insufficiently explored in learning-based registration. This challenge is particularly pronounced in homogeneous, weakly textured, and severely deformed regions, where ambiguous matches may induce erroneous deformation updates. We propose ReliMorph, a reliability-routed symmetric framework for 3D deformable registration that couples correspondence assessment with spatial context selection and update this http URL correlation distributions characterize correspondence ambiguity using shared anatomical features with modality-specific adaptation. These reliability cues guide spatial expert fusion and gate residual velocities during coarse-to-fine refinement. Updated inverse-inconsistency feedback guides routing and gating at finer pyramid levels. To our knowledge, ReliMorph is the first 3D deformable registration framework to jointly condition spatial expert routing and residual velocity gating on correspondence reliability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReliMorph consistently outperforms competing methods in anatomical overlap, highlighting its effectiveness across unimodal and multimodal registration tasks.

</details>


### [228] [GUI-JEPA: Candidate-Conditioned JEPA Latents as a Fast Action Policy for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36877)
*Bingchao Wang,Yongcheng Jing,Shunyu Liu,Chengyu Wang,Jun Huang,Jie Yang,Wei Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GUI-JEPA proposes a decoder-free GUI agent policy built on a JEPA encoder pretrained on unlabeled screenshots; an action/region-conditioned latent predictor maps the screen latent to one latent per candidate action, and lightweight heads score all candidates against the instruction in one batched pass without token decoding. On AndroidControl it is 2.8-4.0x faster than a fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL-7B agent at 6% of its parameters, reaching 86-92% of that model's in-distribution accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing 7B-scale VLM GUI agents decode each action token by token, and even candidate-scoring agents keep an LLM/VLM in the loop, making them slow and memory-hungry. The paper aims to remove autoregressive decoding and the large backbone from the action-selection path.

Method: A JEPA encoder is pretrained on unlabeled GUI screenshots to form a screen latent space. An action- and region-conditioned latent predictor converts the screen latent into one latent per candidate action; lightweight scoring heads then rank all candidates against the language instruction in a single batched forward pass, with no token generation. The authors also run ablations showing the scorer depends on the latent pathway, and that a randomly initialized predictor performs comparably to one pretrained on screen dynamics.

Result: On AndroidControl, GUI-JEPA is 2.8-4.0x faster on the same GPU than a similarly fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL-7B agent, while achieving 86-92% of its in-distribution accuracy with only 6% of its parameters. Ablations indicate the scorer relies on the latent pathway and that the predictor need not be pretrained on dynamics.

Conclusion: Latent-space, decoder-free JEPA policies are a viable and far more efficient alternative to token-generating VLM GUI agents, with the predictor trained for decision-making rather than screen-dynamics prediction. The approach is adjacent to but outside the core Physical AI/robotics/3D scope, mainly of interest as an efficient latent-action interface and JEPA-in-agent design pattern.

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are typically fine-tuned 7B-scale vision-language models (VLMs) that generate each action as text. However, these VLMs decode every action token by token, and even agents that score candidate actions keep a large language model or VLM in the loop, making them slow and memory-hungry. To address these issues, we propose GUI-JEPA, to our knowledge the first decoder-free GUI agent policy built on a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), which selects actions in the latent space of a JEPA encoder pretrained on unlabelled GUI screenshots. Its action- and region-conditioned latent predictor turns the screen latent into one latent per candidate action, letting lightweight heads score all candidates against the instruction in a single batched pass, without decoding a token. We further show that the scorer relies on this latent pathway and that the predictor is trained for the decision itself: a randomly initialised predictor does just as well as one pretrained on screen dynamics. On AndroidControl, GUI-JEPA is 2.8-4.0x faster on the same GPU than a Qwen2.5-VL-7B agent we fine-tune with the same inputs, while reaching 86-92% of its in-distribution accuracy with 6% of its parameters. Code is available at this https URL

</details>
